1	# git-sync Rewrite Issue List
2	
3	This document converts the review memo into a concrete issue list for a from-scratch rewrite branch.
4	
5	The intent is not to patch the current architecture incrementally first. The intent is to:
6	
7	1. Preserve the parts of the current implementation that are directionally correct.
8	2. Throw away the parts that are structurally wrong or too costly to evolve.
9	3. Rebuild in a way that makes comparison against the current branch explicit.
10	
11	## Rewrite Goal
12	
13	Rebuild `git-sync` as a smaller set of focused packages around the same product goal:
14	
15	- remote-to-remote smart HTTP Git mirroring
16	- empty-target bootstrap relay
17	- narrow incremental relay
18	- fallback materialized push path when relay is not safe
19	
20	## Preserve These Ideas
21	
22	- Relay-first design is correct.
23	- Empty-target bootstrap as a distinct strategy is correct.
24	- Narrow incremental relay instead of trying to make every update streamable is correct.
25	- Typed result/output structs are useful.
26	- Machine-readable output is worth keeping.
27	- Integration-heavy testing is the right testing style for this tool.
28	
29	## Replace These Structural Choices
30	
31	- Monolithic orchestration in `internal/syncer/syncer.go`
32	- Mixed protocol, auth, planning, execution, stats, and measurement concerns in one package
33	- Partially custom, partially `go-git` transport stack
34	- Implicit capability handling spread across request builders
35	- Batch planning that probes by repeatedly fetching full packs and discarding them
36	
37	## Proposed Rewrite Shape
38	
39	- `internal/gitproto`
40	  - pkt-line
41	  - smart HTTP v1/v2
42	  - capability negotiation
43	  - ref advertisement and fetch/push request building
44	- `internal/planner`
45	  - desired ref construction
46	  - mapping normalization and validation
47	  - plan generation
48	  - prune policy
49	  - checkpoint planning
50	- `internal/auth`
51	  - credential resolution chain
52	  - token refresh
53	  - token store backends
54	- `internal/strategy/bootstrap`
55	  - one-shot relay bootstrap
56	  - batched bootstrap
57	- `internal/strategy/incremental`
58	  - incremental relay eligibility and execution
59	- `internal/strategy/materialized`
60	  - fetch, local object materialization, and encode/repack push
61	- `internal/syncer`
62	  - top-level orchestration only
63	
64	## Issues
65	
66	## Correctness
67	
68	### 1. Batched bootstrap can claim tag refs were pushed when no tag ref was created
69	
70	Status: open
71	
72	Problem:
73	- In the batched bootstrap tag phase, `FetchPack` returning `git.NoErrAlreadyUpToDate` can skip tag creation entirely even when the tag ref is absent and only the tag object is already reachable.
74	- The result still reports success.
75	
76	Current code:
77	- [internal/syncer/syncer.go](/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/syncer/syncer.go:1308)
78	- [internal/syncer/syncer.go](/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/syncer/syncer.go:1328)
79	
80	Rewrite requirement:
81	- Ref creation must be independent from pack necessity.
82	- Support command-only tag creation when objects already exist.
83	
84	Comparison check:
85	- A tag whose target object is already present on target must still be created during bootstrap.
86	
87	### 2. Duplicate target mappings are silently accepted
88	
89	Status: open
90	
91	Problem:
92	- Multiple mappings to the same target ref overwrite each other silently.
93	
94	Current code:
95	- [internal/syncer/syncer.go](/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/syncer/syncer.go:1655)
96	
97	Rewrite requirement:
98	- Mapping validation must reject duplicate target refs.
99	- Validation failure must happen before planning or network activity.
100	
101	Comparison check:
102	- `--map main:stable --map release:stable` must fail fast with a clear error.
103	
104	### 3. Mapping normalization accepts inconsistent ref kinds and partially-qualified refs
105	
106	Status: open
107	
108	Problem:
109	- Invalid combinations survive normalization and fail later with misleading errors.
110	
111	Current code:
112	- [internal/syncer/syncer.go](/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/syncer/syncer.go:1706)
113	
114	Rewrite requirement:
115	- Normalize short branch names consistently.
116	- Reject branch-to-tag and tag-to-branch mappings.
117	- Reject mixed fully-qualified and shorthand forms when ambiguous.
118	
119	Comparison check:
120	- Invalid mappings must fail at validation time with precise messages.
121	
122	### 4. Sideband preference is backwards
123	
124	Status: open
125	
126	Problem:
127	- The current code prefers `sideband` before `sideband64k`.
128	
129	Current code:
130	- [internal/syncer/syncer.go](/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/syncer/syncer.go:3020)
131	
132	Rewrite requirement:
133	- Prefer `sideband64k` whenever both are available.
134	
135	Comparison check:
136	- Negotiation must pick the highest-capability sideband mode.
137	
138	### 5. Pack reader leak in bootstrap batch loop
139	
140	Status: open
141	
142	Problem:
143	- Batch loop fetches a `packReader` without robust close discipline on all error paths.
144	
145	Current code:
146	- [internal/syncer/syncer.go](/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/syncer/syncer.go:1257)
147	
148	Rewrite requirement:
149	- Stream lifecycle must be explicit and testable.
150	- Every fetch stream must have one owner responsible for close.
151	
152	Comparison check:
153	- Failing batch pushes must not leak HTTP response bodies.
154	
155	### 6. Protocol v2 tag fetches request `include-tag` without capability gating
156	
157	Status: open
158	
159	Problem:
160	- v2 request building sends `include-tag` without first checking server support.
161	
162	Current code:
163	- [internal/syncer/protocol_v2.go](/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/syncer/protocol_v2.go:639)
164	
165	Rewrite requirement:
166	- Capability handling must be centralized and typed.
167	- Unsupported optional features must never be requested.
168	
169	Comparison check:
170	- Tag sync against a v2 server without `include-tag` support must behave correctly.
171	
172	### 7. OAuth refresh failures are swallowed and stale tokens are reused
173	
174	Status: open
175	
176	Problem:
177	- Token refresh failure degrades into later auth failure with poor diagnostics.
178	
179	Current code:
180	- [internal/syncer/syncer.go](/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/syncer/syncer.go:2775)
181	
182	Rewrite requirement:
183	- Distinguish refresh failure from remote auth rejection.
184	- Surface the actual cause.
185	
186	Comparison check:
187	- Expired token plus refresh failure must produce an explicit auth-refresh error path.
188	
189	## Concurrency And Safety
190	
191	### 8. `statsCollector` is not safely synchronized
192	
193	Status: open
194	
195	Problem:
196	- The stats map is mutated and read across goroutines without full synchronization.
197	
198	Current code:
199	- [internal/syncer/syncer.go](/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/syncer/syncer.go:3041)
200	
201	Rewrite requirement:
202	- Stats collection must be race-free under concurrent HTTP activity.
203	
204	Comparison check:
205	- Rewrite branch should pass `go test -race ./...`.
206	
207	### 9. Unbounded response reads can cause avoidable memory blowups
208	
209	Status: open
210	
211	Problem:
212	- Some response paths use unbounded reads from remote servers.
213	
214	Current code:
215	- `protocol_v2.go:713` from review notes
216	
217	Rewrite requirement:
218	- Bound responses where protocol shape permits.
219	- Stream where possible instead of buffering whole bodies.
220	
221	Comparison check:
222	- Large or malicious server responses must not be able to force unbounded buffering in normal control paths.
223	
224	### 10. File token store has no locking
225	
226	Status: open
227	
228	Problem:
229	- Multiple processes can corrupt token storage.
230	
231	Current code:
232	- [internal/syncer/syncer.go](/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/syncer/syncer.go:2931)
233	
234	Rewrite requirement:
235	- Add process-safe locking if file store remains supported.
236	- Or explicitly scope file store to single-process/dev usage and document that.
237	
238	## Architecture
239	
240	### 11. `syncer.go` is a monolith
241	
242	Status: open
243	
244	Problem:
245	- One file currently owns protocol setup, planning, batching, auth, stats, and execution.
246	
247	Current code:
248	- [internal/syncer/syncer.go](/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/syncer/syncer.go:397)
249	
250	Rewrite requirement:
251	- Package boundaries must isolate protocol, planning, auth, and execution strategies.
252	
253	Comparison check:
254	- Top-level orchestrator should be visibly smaller and mostly wiring.
255	
256	### 12. Entry points duplicate setup work
257	
258	Status: open
259	
260	Problem:
261	- `Run`, `Bootstrap`, `Probe`, and `Fetch` all repeat protocol validation, stats setup, connection setup, and ref discovery.
262	
263	Rewrite requirement:
264	- Introduce a shared session/setup layer.
265	
266	### 13. Functions carry too much ambient state
267	
268	Status: open
269	
270	Problem:
271	- Large helpers like batched bootstrap depend on too many parameters and too much shared context.
272	
273	Rewrite requirement:
274	- Introduce explicit session/context objects with narrow responsibilities.
275	
276	## Performance And Scalability
277	
278	### 14. Batch planning probes by repeatedly fetching full packs and discarding them
279	
280	Status: open
281	
282	Problem:
283	- `sourcePackExceedsLimit` can turn checkpoint planning into repeated full-pack fetches.
284	
285	Current code:
286	- [internal/syncer/syncer.go](/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/syncer/syncer.go:1601)
287	
288	Rewrite requirement:
289	- Avoid repeated throwaway fetches as the primary sizing mechanism.
290	- Reuse fitted packs or use a cheaper sizing heuristic.
291	
292	Comparison check:
293	- Rewrite branch should significantly reduce HTTP round-trips during batch planning.
294	
295	### 15. Materialized fallback path does not scale to large repos
296	
297	Status: open
298	
299	Problem:
300	- The non-relay path stores fetched objects in memory.
301	
302	Rewrite requirement:
303	- Decide explicitly whether large non-relay sync is supported.
304	- If yes, redesign storage strategy.
305	- If no, fail early and clearly outside safe operating bounds.
306	
307	### 16. Fast-forward checks can degenerate into full graph walks
308	
309	Status: open
310	
311	Problem:
312	- `reachesCommitHash` can traverse very large histories.
313	
314	Current code:
315	- [internal/syncer/syncer.go](/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/syncer/syncer.go:2533)
316	
317	Rewrite requirement:
318	- Make ancestry checking cost visible and bounded where possible.
319	
320	### 17. Packet parsing allocates too aggressively
321	
322	Status: open
323	
324	Problem:
325	- Packet reader allocates per packet and may create unnecessary GC churn.
326	
327	Current code:
328	- [internal/syncer/protocol_v2.go](/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/syncer/protocol_v2.go:75)
329	
330	Rewrite requirement:
331	- Reuse buffers where practical.
332	
333	## Test Gaps
334	
335	### 18. Core planning functions are under-tested directly
336	
337	Status: open
338	
339	Missing direct tests include:
340	- `buildDesiredRefs`
341	- `buildPlans`
342	- `planRef`
343	- `normalizeMapping`
344	- `objectsToPush`
345	- `collectPushObjects`
346	- `firstParentChain`
347	- `autoBatchMaxPackBytes`
348	- `bootstrapResumeIndex` error path
349	
350	Rewrite requirement:
351	- Pure planning and validation logic should be unit-testable without HTTP fixtures.
352	
353	### 19. Relay eligibility logic is only tested indirectly
354	
355	Status: open
356	
357	Missing direct tests include:
358	- `canIncrementalRelay`
359	- `canFullTagCreateRelay`
360	- `relayFallbackReason`
361	
362	Rewrite requirement:
363	- Strategy selection must be testable independently from network execution.
364	
365	### 20. Protocol v2 error handling is under-tested
366	
367	Status: open
368	
369	Missing test coverage includes:
370	- malformed pkt-lines
371	- truncated packet bodies
372	- missing `version 2`
373	- malformed `ls-refs`
374	- unsupported capability combinations
375	
376	Rewrite requirement:
377	- Protocol parser behavior must be locked down with explicit failure tests.
378	
379	### 21. Missing behavioral coverage
380	
381	Status: open
382	
383	Known missing cases:
384	- empty source repo
385	- tag force-retarget
386	- duplicate/conflicting mappings
387	- context cancellation
388	- batch resume mismatch
389	- batch cutover partial failure
390	- tag creation when target objects already exist
391	
392	### 22. No benchmark coverage for the expensive paths
393	
394	Status: open
395	
396	Rewrite requirement:
397	- Add benchmarks for relay path overhead, planning overhead, and fallback graph/object work.
398	
399	## Rewrite Branch Acceptance Criteria
400	
401	- All mapping validation happens before network activity.
402	- Capability negotiation is centralized and enforced consistently.
403	- Relay strategies are separate packages with explicit inputs and outputs.
404	- Tag creation is correct whether or not a pack transfer is needed.
405	- Stats and logging are concurrency-safe.
406	- Protocol parsing has explicit malformed-input tests.
407	- Rewrite passes `go test ./...` and `go test -race ./...`.
408	- Rewrite includes benchmarks for the critical planning and execution paths.
409	- Rewrite branch can be compared against current behavior using the same integration scenarios.
410	
411	## Suggested Execution Order
412	
413	1. Define package boundaries and minimal interfaces.
414	2. Rebuild mapping validation and planning first.
415	3. Rebuild protocol and capability layer.
416	4. Rebuild bootstrap relay.
417	5. Rebuild incremental relay.
418	6. Rebuild fallback materialized push path.
419	7. Rebuild auth and token store behavior.
420	8. Port and expand tests.
421	9. Compare rewrite branch behavior against current integration fixtures.
422	
423

1	# git-sync Rewrite Memo
2	
3	This memo captures the architectural conclusions behind the rewrite branch. It is meant to be read alongside [rewrite-issue-list.md](/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/docs/rewrite-issue-list.md).
4	
5	## Why Rewrite Instead Of Patch In Place
6	
7	The core idea in `git-sync` is correct, but the current implementation has crossed the point where targeted fixes alone will keep the design clean.
8	
9	The repo has three simultaneous realities:
10	
11	- The product model is good.
12	- There are concrete correctness bugs and scaling issues.
13	- Too many concerns are collapsed into one implementation file and one package shape.
14	
15	That combination makes a rewrite branch reasonable. The goal is not novelty. The goal is to:
16	
17	1. Preserve the correct product decisions.
18	2. Remove accidental complexity.
19	3. Make edge-case correctness and performance behavior explicit.
20	
21	## Current System Summary
22	
23	The current code is a remote-to-remote Git mirroring CLI over smart HTTP.
24	
25	Main execution modes:
26	
27	- `bootstrap`
28	  - empty-target relay
29	- `sync`
30	  - normal planning and reconciliation
31	- incremental relay
32	  - narrow fast-path for safe updates
33	- materialized fallback
34	  - decode/repack path when relay is not safe
35	- batched bootstrap
36	  - large initial migration fallback
37	
38	Most logic currently lives in:
39	
40	- [cmd/git-sync/main.go](/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/cmd/git-sync/main.go)
41	- [internal/syncer/syncer.go](/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/syncer/syncer.go)
42	- [internal/syncer/protocol_v2.go](/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/syncer/protocol_v2.go)
43	
44	## What The Current Implementation Gets Right
45	
46	### 1. Relay-first is the right core design
47	
48	The most important idea in this repo is the relay model:
49	
50	- fetch a pack from source
51	- avoid decoding and re-encoding when safe
52	- stream it into target
53	
54	That is the right shape for large remote-to-remote mirroring.
55	
56	### 2. Bootstrap as a separate strategy is correct
57	
58	An empty-target initial sync is materially different from incremental reconciliation. Treating bootstrap as its own path is the right call.
59	
60	### 3. Incremental relay should remain intentionally narrow
61	
62	The code is right not to force every update into a relay path. Narrow strategy selection is preferable to fragile protocol cleverness.
63	
64	### 4. Integration-heavy testing is the right style
65	
66	This tool is protocol-heavy. Integration tests and `git-http-backend` style validation are more valuable here than a mostly unit-test-only suite.
67	
68	### 5. Machine-readable output is worth preserving
69	
70	The typed result model and JSON output are good product choices and should survive the rewrite.
71	
72	## What Is Structurally Wrong In The Current Shape
73	
74	### 1. Too many concerns live in one file
75	
76	[internal/syncer/syncer.go](/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/syncer/syncer.go) currently mixes:
77	
78	- planning
79	- strategy selection
80	- bootstrap batching
81	- push mechanics
82	- transport setup
83	- auth lookup
84	- token refresh
85	- stats
86	- measurement
87	
88	That concentration is the main architectural problem.
89	
90	### 2. Transport logic is split across two models
91	
92	The code partially relies on `go-git` transport and partially bypasses it with custom smart-HTTP and protocol-v2 handling. That creates a dual-stack design:
93	
94	- some transport concerns are implicit through `go-git`
95	- others are explicit in custom code
96	
97	This is hard to reason about and hard to test consistently.
98	
99	### 3. Capability handling is too scattered
100	
101	Protocol feature checks should be centralized and typed. Right now they are spread across request builders and strategy paths.
102	
103	### 4. Batch planning is too expensive and too implicit
104	
105	Checkpoint planning currently leans on repeated fetch-and-discard probing. That makes the planner expensive, network-heavy, and difficult to reason about.
106	
107	### 5. Validation is not front-loaded enough
108	
109	Mappings and ref-policy decisions should fail before network activity. The current implementation allows some invalid configurations to survive into later execution phases.
110	
111	## Rewrite Principles
112	
113	### 1. Make strategy boundaries explicit
114	
115	Each execution strategy should have:
116	
117	- explicit inputs
118	- explicit capability requirements
119	- explicit output/result contract
120	
121	### 2. Make validation a first-class phase
122	
123	Before any network activity:
124	
125	- normalize mappings
126	- reject invalid combinations
127	- reject duplicate targets
128	- construct the managed ref set
129	
130	### 3. Make capability negotiation a first-class type
131	
132	Instead of ad hoc checks across the codebase, capability negotiation should produce a typed description of what the server actually supports.
133	
134	### 4. Prefer small packages over one “smart” package
135	
136	The rewrite should reduce cross-cutting responsibility, not just move lines around.
137	
138	### 5. Keep the product surface stable where possible
139	
140	The rewrite should try to preserve:
141	
142	- CLI shape
143	- JSON output contract where practical
144	- user-visible strategy semantics
145	
146	## Proposed Package Model
147	
148	### `internal/gitproto`
149	
150	Responsibilities:
151	
152	- pkt-line reading/writing
153	- smart HTTP request/response handling
154	- protocol v1 and v2 support
155	- capability negotiation
156	- ref advertisement
157	- fetch request building
158	- push request building
159	
160	Why:
161	- The current code already wants this abstraction.
162	- The rewrite should own the HTTP protocol layer directly instead of mixing custom transport with `go-git` transport behavior.
163	
164	### `internal/planner`
165	
166	Responsibilities:
167	
168	- mapping normalization
169	- mapping validation
170	- desired ref construction
171	- prune policy
172	- action planning
173	- checkpoint planning for batching
174	
175	Why:
176	- This is the highest-leverage pure logic in the system.
177	- It should be testable without HTTP.
178	
179	### `internal/auth`
180	
181	Responsibilities:
182	
183	- credential source ordering
184	- git credential helper integration
185	- Entire token lookup
186	- refresh behavior
187	- token store backends
188	
189	Why:
190	- Auth currently adds a lot of noise to orchestration.
191	- It should be isolated and explicit.
192	
193	### `internal/strategy/bootstrap`
194	
195	Responsibilities:
196	
197	- one-shot bootstrap relay
198	- batched bootstrap
199	- checkpoint execution
200	- temp ref cutover
201	
202	Why:
203	- Bootstrap is already a separate product concept and deserves separate execution code.
204	
205	### `internal/strategy/incremental`
206	
207	Responsibilities:
208	
209	- incremental relay eligibility
210	- relay fetch/push execution
211	
212	Why:
213	- This path has distinct capability and safety rules.
214	
215	### `internal/strategy/materialized`
216	
217	Responsibilities:
218	
219	- local object materialization
220	- object closure
221	- encode/repack push
222	
223	Why:
224	- This path is meaningfully different from relay paths and should be isolated from them.
225	
226	### `internal/syncer`
227	
228	Responsibilities:
229	
230	- top-level orchestration
231	- session assembly
232	- choosing a strategy
233	- collecting results
234	
235	Why:
236	- The orchestrator should become smaller, not smarter.
237	
238	## Recommended Interfaces
239	
240	These do not need to be exact, but the rewrite should aim for this level of separation.
241	
242	### Source-side interfaces
243	
244	- `RefLister`
245	- `PackFetcher`
246	- `CommitGraphFetcher`
247	
248	### Target-side interfaces
249	
250	- `RefAdvertiser`
251	- `PackPusher`
252	- `CommandPusher`
253	
254	### Planning interfaces
255	
256	- `MappingValidator`
257	- `Planner`
258	- `CheckpointPlanner`
259	
260	The point is not abstraction for its own sake. The point is to make:
261	
262	- planning testable without HTTP
263	- protocol testable without planning
264	- strategy testable without CLI parsing
265	
266	## go-git In The Rewrite
267	
268	Recommended position:
269	
270	- own the smart HTTP protocol layer directly
271	- keep `go-git` packfile/object support only where it still buys value
272	
273	Reasoning:
274	
275	- The current code already bypasses `go-git` transport in important places.
276	- Owning the HTTP/protocol layer removes the dual-stack problem.
277	- Keeping packfile/object utilities can still be pragmatic for the materialized fallback path.
278	
279	## Logging
280	
281	Replace ad hoc `progressf`-style stderr logging with structured logging.
282	
283	Desired properties:
284	
285	- log levels
286	- structured fields
287	- easy correlation of branch/batch/protocol information
288	- consistent formatting across text and JSON-oriented automation use
289	
290	`slog` is the obvious standard-library fit.
291	
292	## Performance Position
293	
294	The rewrite should not try to make every path maximally optimal on day one. It should make performance costs visible and bounded.
295	
296	Priority performance goals:
297	
298	- avoid repeated fetch-and-discard pack probing
299	- reduce unbounded buffering
300	- make non-relay cost explicit
301	- keep relay path cheap
302	
303	It is acceptable for the materialized fallback path to remain expensive if the rewrite makes those limits explicit and predictable.
304	
305	## Testing Position
306	
307	The rewrite should preserve the current integration-heavy style and add stronger unit coverage around pure logic.
308	
309	Desired balance:
310	
311	- unit tests for planning, validation, checkpointing, and protocol parsing
312	- integration tests for end-to-end smart HTTP behavior
313	- `git-http-backend` coverage for realistic wire compatibility
314	- race tests
315	- benchmarks for the expensive paths
316	
317	## Migration Strategy
318	
319	This rewrite branch should not start by porting every function. It should start by rebuilding the design boundaries.
320	
321	Recommended order:
322	
323	1. Define package boundaries and interfaces.
324	2. Rebuild validation and planning first.
325	3. Rebuild protocol handling and capability negotiation.
326	4. Rebuild bootstrap relay.
327	5. Rebuild incremental relay.

## Comprehensive Exploration Report: git-sync Codebase

### Project Overview
**git-sync** is a Go project for remote-to-remote Git mirroring over smart HTTP. It mirrors refs from a source remote to a target remote without creating a local checkout, using an in-memory `go-git` object store and talking smart HTTP directly via `info/refs`, `upload-pack`, and `receive-pack` services.

**Go Version:** 1.25.2
**Module:** `github.com/soph/git-sync`

---

### Directory and File Structure

```
/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/
├── cmd/
│   └── git-sync/
│       ├── main.go (371 lines)
│       └── main_test.go (438 lines)
├── internal/
│   └── syncer/
│       ├── syncer.go (3,143 lines) - MAIN FILE
│       ├── protocol_v2.go (782 lines)
│       ├── syncer_test.go (241 lines)
│       ├── protocol_v2_test.go (117 lines)
│       ├── auth_test.go (183 lines)
│       ├── integration_test.go (1,602 lines)
│       ├── git_http_backend_test.go (736 lines)
│       ├── entire_local_smoke_test.go (336 lines)
│       ├── entire_local_smoke_test_test.go (36 lines)
│       └── live_bootstrap_test.go (72 lines)
├── docs/

### go.mod Dependencies

**Direct Dependencies:**
- `github.com/go-git/go-billy/v5 v5.8.0` - Filesystem abstraction
- `github.com/go-git/go-git/v5 v5.17.0` - Git operations library
- `github.com/zalando/go-keyring v0.2.8` - Keyring access (for credential storage)

**Transitive Dependencies:** 30+ (crypto, network, and Git-related libraries)

---

### Main Entry Point: cmd/git-sync/main.go

**Key Functions:**
- `main()` - Entry point, delegates to `run()`
- `run(ctx, args)` - Command dispatcher
- `runSyncLike(ctx, name, args, dryRun)` - Handles "sync" and "plan" commands
- `runBootstrap(ctx, args)` - Handles "bootstrap" command
- `runProbe(ctx, args)` - Handles "probe" command (inspect source/target)
- `runFetch(ctx, args)` - Handles "fetch" command (exercise negotiation)
- `usageError(message)` - Generates comprehensive usage text
- `parseMapping(raw)` - Parses ref mappings (src:dst format)
- `printOutput(jsonOutput, value)` - Outputs text or JSON
- `marshalOutput(value)` - JSON marshaling

**CLI Commands:**
1. **sync** - Execute planned changes to target
2. **plan** - Compute source-to-target ref actions (dry-run)
3. **bootstrap** - Seed empty target with relay behavior
4. **probe** - Inspect source remote and optionally target
5. **fetch** - Exercise source-side fetch negotiation without pushing

**Shared Flags (sync/plan/bootstrap):**
- Source/target URLs and auth (token, username, bearer token, TLS skip verify)
- `--branch` - Comma-separated branch list
- `--map` - Ref mapping (src:dst form)
- `--tags` - Mirror tags
- `--force` - Allow non-fast-forward updates
- `--prune` - Delete managed target refs
- `--stats` - Transfer statistics
- `--measure-memory` - Memory/time sampling
- `--json` - Machine-readable output
- `--protocol` - Protocol mode: auto, v1, v2
- `-v` - Verbose logging

### Protocol V2 Implementation: internal/syncer/protocol_v2.go (782 lines)

**Key Types:**
```go
type sourceRefService struct {
    protocol string // "v1" or "v2"
    v1       *packp.AdvRefs
    v2       *v2CapabilityAdvertisement
}

type v2CapabilityAdvertisement struct {
    Capabilities map[string]string
}

type packetReader struct {
    r *bufio.Reader
}

type packetType int // packetTypeData, Flush, Delim, ResponseEnd

type wrappedReadCloser struct {
    io.Reader
    io.Closer
}
```

**Key Functions:**

*Ref Discovery:*
- `listSourceRefs(ctx, conn, cfg)` - Detect protocol and list refs (auto-detects v2 with fallback to v1)
- `listSourceRefsV1(ctx, conn)` - V1 ref listing
- `listSourceRefsV2(ctx, conn, adv, cfg)` - V2 ref listing with ls-refs
- `decodeV2LSRefs(r)` - Decode ls-refs response

*V2 Capability Handling:*
- `decodeV2CapabilityAdvertisement(r)` - Parse v2 capability advertisement
- `encodeV2CommandRequest(command, capabilityArgs, commandArgs)` - Build v2 command request
- `v2CapabilityAdvertisement.Supports(name)` - Check capability support
- `v2CapabilityAdvertisement.Value(name)` - Get capability value
- `sourceCapabilities()`, `advCapabilities()` - Extract capability lists

*Packet Handling:*
- `newPacketReader(r)` - Create packet reader
- `packetReader.ReadPacket()` - Read pkt-line format packets
- `pktlineLength(header)` - Parse pkt-line hex length
- `asciiHexToByte(b)` - Hex parsing helper

*Fetch Operations:*
- `fetchSourceRefsV2(ctx, repo, conn, adv, desired, targetRefs)` - V2 fetch with wants/haves
- `fetchSourcePackV2(ctx, conn, adv, desired, targetRefs)` - V2 pack fetch (streaming)
- `storeV2FetchPack(repo, r)` - Store v2 fetch response
- `openV2FetchPackStream(body)` - Open v2 packfile stream
- `sourceFetchRequestV2(adv, desired, targetRefs)` - Build v2 fetch request
- `fetchSourceCommitGraphV2()` - Fetch commit graph subset

*Helper Functions:*
- `sourceRefPrefixes(cfg)` - Determine ref-prefix arguments
- `fetchCapabilitySupports(adv, feature)` - Check fetch capability
- `v2RequestCapabilities(adv)` - Build request capabilities
- `storeFetchedSourceRefs(repo, desired)` - Store local refs
- `skipV2Section(reader)` - Skip v2 response sections

*HTTP/RPC:*
- `requestInfoRefs(ctx, conn, service, gitProtocol)` - GET /info/refs
- `advertisedRefsV1(ctx, conn, service)` - V1 ref advertisement
- `decodeV1AdvertisedRefs(data)` - Parse v1 advertisement
- `postRPC(ctx, conn, service, body, gitProtocolV2)` - POST RPC (buffered)
- `postRPCStream(ctx, conn, service, body, gitProtocolV2)` - POST RPC (streaming)
- `postRPCWithPhase()`, `postRPCStreamWithPhase()` - Variants with stats phase header
- `applyAuth(req, authMethod)` - Apply auth to HTTP request

**Protocol Support:**
- **V1:** Traditional git smart HTTP with advertised-refs + upload-pack/receive-pack
- **V2:** Modern protocol with ls-refs, fetch commands, capability advertisement
- **Auto:** Attempts V2 first, falls back to V1
- Push remains V1 (no V2 push support)

---

### Key Package Interfaces & Design Patterns

**Repository Interaction:**
- Uses in-memory `go-git` repository (`github.com/go-git/go-git/v5`)
- Object storage via `memory.NewStorage()`
- Direct Git protocol via smart HTTP

**Authentication Resolution Order:**
1. Explicit CLI flags (`--source-token`, `--target-token`)
2. Environment variables (`GITSYNC_SOURCE_TOKEN`, `GITSYNC_TARGET_TOKEN`)
3. Local git credential helper (`git credential fill`)
4. Keyring lookup (Entire DB token storage)
5. Anonymous access

**Relay Modes:**
- **Bootstrap Relay:** Full source pack → target receive-pack (initial empty target)
- **Batched Bootstrap:** Large repos split into checkpoint batches
- **Incremental Relay:** Fast-forward updates + new tags without local decode
- **Fallback:** Full local decode and repack for complex updates

**Plan Action States:**
- `ActionCreate` - New ref on target
- `ActionUpdate` - Fast-forward or forced update
- `ActionDelete` - Prune managed ref
- `ActionSkip` - No action needed
- `ActionBlock` - Blocked (non-fast-forward without --force)

**Batching Strategy:**
- Protocol v2 only
- Branch refs are batched
- Temporary refs under `refs/gitsync/bootstrap/heads/`
- Resume from existing temp refs
- Default batch size: 512 MiB (configurable)
- GitHub large repo detection (>1.5 GB)

---

### Build & Testing

**Build:**
```bash
go build ./cmd/git-sync
```

**Testing:**
```bash
go test ./...  # Standard tests (in-process smart HTTP servers)
GOCACHE=/tmp/go-build go test ./...  # With cache directory override
GITSYNC_E2E_GIT_HTTP_BACKEND=1 go test ./internal/syncer -run TestRun_GitHTTPBackendSync -v
GITSYNC_E2E_LIVE_LINUX=1 go test ./internal/syncer -run TestBootstrap_LiveLinuxSource -v
```

**Test Environment Variables:**
- `GITSYNC_E2E_GIT_HTTP_BACKEND=1` - Enable git-http-backend tests
- `GITSYNC_E2E_LIVE_LINUX=1` - Enable Linux kernel repo smoke tests
- `GITSYNC_E2E_ENTIRE_*` - Entire local instance tests
- `GITSYNC_E2E_ENTIRE_SOURCE_URL`, `GITSYNC_E2E_ENTIRE_BRANCH`
- `GITSYNC_E2E_ENTIRE_PROTOCOL`, `GITSYNC_E2E_ENTIRE_BATCH_MAX_PACK_BYTES`

---

### Key Design Decisions

1. **Protocol V2 Limited to Fetch:** Push remains v1 because control is already good and the main win is source-side discovery/download
2. **In-Memory Objects:** All objects kept in memory for duration of run; target remains incremental
3. **Smart HTTP Only:** No local checkout, no ssh/git protocol support
4. **Explicit Ref Mapping:** No wildcard support; users specify exact mappings
5. **Fast-Forward Safety:** Default safe behavior, opt-in force/prune
6. **Relay Path:** When possible, stream source pack directly to target without local decode
7. **Batching:** For large repos, split initial bootstrap into checkpoints to work within push size limits

1	package syncer
2	
3	import (
4		"bufio"
5		"bytes"
6		"context"

14		git "github.com/go-git/go-git/v5"
15		"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing"
16		"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/format/packfile"
17		"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/format/pktline"
18		"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/protocol/packp"
19		"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/protocol/packp/capability"
20		"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/protocol/packp/sideband"
21		"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/transport"
22		transporthttp "github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/transport/http"
23		"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/utils/ioutil"
24	)
25	
26	const (
27		delimPkt       = "0001"
28		responseEndPkt = "0002"
29		statsPhaseHdr  = "X-Git-Sync-Stats-Phase"
30	)
31	
32	type packetType int
33	
34	const (
35		packetTypeData packetType = iota
36		packetTypeFlush
37		packetTypeDelim
38		packetTypeResponseEnd
39	)
40	
41	type v2CapabilityAdvertisement struct {
42		Capabilities map[string]string
43	}
44	
45	func (a *v2CapabilityAdvertisement) Supports(name string) bool {
46		if a == nil {
47			return false
48		}
49		_, ok := a.Capabilities[name]
50		return ok
51	}
52	
53	func (a *v2CapabilityAdvertisement) Value(name string) string {
54		if a == nil {
55			return ""
56		}
57		return a.Capabilities[name]
58	}
59	
60	type packetReader struct {
61		r *bufio.Reader
62	}
63	
64	func newPacketReader(r io.Reader) *packetReader {
65		if br, ok := r.(*bufio.Reader); ok {
66			return &packetReader{r: br}
67		}
68		return &packetReader{r: bufio.NewReader(r)}
69	}
70	
71	func (r *packetReader) Reader() *bufio.Reader {
72		return r.r
73	}
74	
75	func (r *packetReader) ReadPacket() (packetType, []byte, error) {
76		header := make([]byte, 4)
77		if _, err := io.ReadFull(r.r, header); err != nil {
78			return packetTypeData, nil, err
79		}
80	
81		switch string(header) {
82		case "0000":
83			return packetTypeFlush, nil, nil
84		case delimPkt:
85			return packetTypeDelim, nil, nil
86		case responseEndPkt:
87			return packetTypeResponseEnd, nil, nil
88		}
89	
90		var headerArr [4]byte
91		copy(headerArr[:], header)
92		n, err := pktlineLength(headerArr)
93		if err != nil {
94			return packetTypeData, nil, err
95		}
96		if n <= 4 {
97			return packetTypeData, nil, pktline.ErrInvalidPktLen
98		}
99	
100		payload := make([]byte, n-4)
101		if _, err := io.ReadFull(r.r, payload); err != nil {
102			return packetTypeData, nil, err
103		}
104		return packetTypeData, payload, nil
105	}
106	
107	func pktlineLength(header [4]byte) (int, error) {
108		var n int
109		for _, b := range header {
110			value, err := asciiHexToByte(b)
111			if err != nil {
112				return 0, pktline.ErrInvalidPktLen
113			}
114			n = 16*n + int(value)
115		}
116		return n, nil
117	}
118	
119	func asciiHexToByte(b byte) (byte, error) {
120		switch {
121		case b >= '0' && b <= '9':
122			return b - '0', nil
123		case b >= 'a' && b <= 'f':
124			return b - 'a' + 10, nil
125		case b >= 'A' && b <= 'F':
126			return b - 'A' + 10, nil
127		default:
128			return 0, pktline.ErrInvalidPktLen
129		}
130	}
131	
132	func decodeV2CapabilityAdvertisement(r io.Reader) (*v2CapabilityAdvertisement, error) {
133		reader := newPacketReader(r)
134	
135		var (
136			kind    packetType
137			payload []byte
138			err     error
139		)
140		for {
141			kind, payload, err = reader.ReadPacket()
142			if err != nil {
143				return nil, err
144			}
145			if kind == packetTypeFlush {
146				continue
147			}
148			if kind != packetTypeData {
149				return nil, fmt.Errorf("unexpected packet type %v before protocol advertisement", kind)
150			}
151			if strings.HasPrefix(string(payload), "# service=") {
152				continue
153			}
154			if string(payload) != "version 2\n" {
155				return nil, fmt.Errorf("unexpected protocol advertisement %q", payload)
156			}
157			break
158		}
159	
160		adv := &v2CapabilityAdvertisement{Capabilities: map[string]string{}}
161		for {
162			kind, payload, err = reader.ReadPacket()
163			if err != nil {
164				return nil, err
165			}
166			if kind == packetTypeFlush {
167				return adv, nil
168			}
169			if kind != packetTypeData {
170				return nil, fmt.Errorf("unexpected packet type %v in capability advertisement", kind)
171			}
172	
173			line := strings.TrimSuffix(string(payload), "\n")
174			name, value, _ := strings.Cut(line, "=")
175			adv.Capabilities[name] = value
176		}
177	}
178	
179	func encodeV2CommandRequest(command string, capabilityArgs []string, commandArgs []string) ([]byte, error) {
180		var buf bytes.Buffer
181		enc := pktline.NewEncoder(&buf)
182		if err := enc.EncodeString("command=" + command + "\n"); err != nil {
183			return nil, err
184		}
185		for _, arg := range capabilityArgs {
186			if err := enc.EncodeString(arg + "\n"); err != nil {
187				return nil, err
188			}
189		}
190		if len(commandArgs) > 0 {
191			if _, err := buf.WriteString(delimPkt); err != nil {
192				return nil, err
193			}
194			for _, arg := range commandArgs {
195				if err := enc.EncodeString(arg + "\n"); err != nil {
196					return nil, err
197				}
198			}
199		}
200		if err := enc.Flush(); err != nil {

1	package main
2	
3	import (
4		"context"
5		"encoding/json"
6		"errors"
7		"flag"
8		"fmt"
9		"os"
10		"strings"
11	
12		"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing"
13		"github.com/soph/git-sync/internal/syncer"
14	)
15	
16	func main() {
17		if err := run(context.Background(), os.Args[1:]); err != nil {
18			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "error: %v\n", err)
19			os.Exit(1)
20		}
21	}
22	
23	func run(ctx context.Context, args []string) error {
24		if len(args) == 0 {
25			return usageError("")
26		}
27	
28		switch args[0] {
29		case "sync":
30			return runSyncLike(ctx, "sync", args[1:], false)
31		case "plan":
32			return runSyncLike(ctx, "plan", args[1:], true)
33		case "bootstrap":
34			return runBootstrap(ctx, args[1:])
35		case "probe":
36			return runProbe(ctx, args[1:])
37		case "fetch":
38			return runFetch(ctx, args[1:])
39		case "help", "-h", "--help":
40			return usageError("")
41		default:
42			return usageError(fmt.Sprintf("unknown command %q", args[0]))
43		}
44	}
45	
46	func runSyncLike(ctx context.Context, name string, args []string, dryRun bool) error {
47		fs := flag.NewFlagSet(name, flag.ContinueOnError)
48		fs.SetOutput(os.Stderr)
49	
50		cfg := syncer.Config{DryRun: dryRun}
51		var mappings multiStringFlag
52		var jsonOutput bool
53	
54		fs.StringVar(&cfg.Source.URL, "source-url", "", "source repository URL")
55		fs.StringVar(&cfg.Target.URL, "target-url", "", "target repository URL")
56	
57		fs.StringVar(&cfg.Source.Token, "source-token", envOr("GITSYNC_SOURCE_TOKEN", ""), "source token/password")
58		fs.StringVar(&cfg.Target.Token, "target-token", envOr("GITSYNC_TARGET_TOKEN", ""), "target token/password")
59		fs.StringVar(&cfg.Source.Username, "source-username", envOr("GITSYNC_SOURCE_USERNAME", "git"), "source basic auth username")
60		fs.StringVar(&cfg.Target.Username, "target-username", envOr("GITSYNC_TARGET_USERNAME", "git"), "target basic auth username")
61		fs.BoolVar(&cfg.Source.SkipTLSVerify, "source-insecure-skip-tls-verify", envBool("GITSYNC_SOURCE_INSECURE_SKIP_TLS_VERIFY"), "skip TLS certificate verification for the source")
62		fs.BoolVar(&cfg.Target.SkipTLSVerify, "target-insecure-skip-tls-verify", envBool("GITSYNC_TARGET_INSECURE_SKIP_TLS_VERIFY"), "skip TLS certificate verification for the target")
63	
64		fs.StringVar(&cfg.Source.BearerToken, "source-bearer-token", envOr("GITSYNC_SOURCE_BEARER_TOKEN", ""), "source bearer token")
65		fs.StringVar(&cfg.Target.BearerToken, "target-bearer-token", envOr("GITSYNC_TARGET_BEARER_TOKEN", ""), "target bearer token")
66	
67		branches := fs.String("branch", "", "comma-separated branch list; default is all source branches")
68		fs.Var(&mappings, "map", "ref mapping in src:dst form; short names map branches, full refs map exact refs")
69		fs.BoolVar(&cfg.IncludeTags, "tags", false, "mirror tags")
70		fs.BoolVar(&cfg.Force, "force", false, "allow non-fast-forward branch updates and retarget tags")
71		fs.BoolVar(&cfg.Prune, "prune", false, "delete managed target refs that no longer exist on source")
72		fs.BoolVar(&cfg.ShowStats, "stats", false, "print transfer statistics")
73		fs.BoolVar(&cfg.MeasureMemory, "measure-memory", false, "sample elapsed time and Go heap usage")
74		fs.BoolVar(&jsonOutput, "json", false, "print JSON output")
75		fs.StringVar(&cfg.ProtocolMode, "protocol", envOr("GITSYNC_PROTOCOL", "auto"), "protocol mode: auto, v1, or v2")
76		fs.BoolVar(&cfg.Verbose, "v", false, "verbose logging")
77	
78		if err := fs.Parse(args); err != nil {
79			return err
80		}
81	
82		positional := fs.Args()
83		if cfg.Source.URL == "" && len(positional) > 0 {
84			cfg.Source.URL = positional[0]
85		}
86		if cfg.Target.URL == "" && len(positional) > 1 {
87			cfg.Target.URL = positional[1]
88		}
89		if len(positional) > 2 {
90			return usageError("too many positional arguments")
91		}
92	
93		if *branches != "" {
94			cfg.Branches = splitCSV(*branches)
95		}
96		for _, raw := range mappings {
97			mapping, err := parseMapping(raw)
98			if err != nil {
99				return err
100			}
101			cfg.Mappings = append(cfg.Mappings, mapping)
102		}
103	
104		if cfg.Source.URL == "" || cfg.Target.URL == "" {
105			return usageError(name + " requires source and target repository URLs")
106		}
107	
108		result, err := syncer.Run(ctx, cfg)
109		if err != nil {
110			return err
111		}
112		printOutput(jsonOutput, result)
113	
114		if !dryRun && result.Blocked > 0 {
115			return errors.New("one or more branches were skipped because the target was not fast-forwardable")
116		}
117		return nil
118	}
119	
120	func runBootstrap(ctx context.Context, args []string) error {
121		fs := flag.NewFlagSet("bootstrap", flag.ContinueOnError)
122		fs.SetOutput(os.Stderr)
123	
124		cfg := syncer.Config{}
125		var mappings multiStringFlag
126		var jsonOutput bool
127	
128		fs.StringVar(&cfg.Source.URL, "source-url", "", "source repository URL")
129		fs.StringVar(&cfg.Target.URL, "target-url", "", "target repository URL")
130	
131		fs.StringVar(&cfg.Source.Token, "source-token", envOr("GITSYNC_SOURCE_TOKEN", ""), "source token/password")
132		fs.StringVar(&cfg.Target.Token, "target-token", envOr("GITSYNC_TARGET_TOKEN", ""), "target token/password")
133		fs.StringVar(&cfg.Source.Username, "source-username", envOr("GITSYNC_SOURCE_USERNAME", "git"), "source basic auth username")
134		fs.StringVar(&cfg.Target.Username, "target-username", envOr("GITSYNC_TARGET_USERNAME", "git"), "target basic auth username")
135		fs.BoolVar(&cfg.Source.SkipTLSVerify, "source-insecure-skip-tls-verify", envBool("GITSYNC_SOURCE_INSECURE_SKIP_TLS_VERIFY"), "skip TLS certificate verification for the source")
136		fs.BoolVar(&cfg.Target.SkipTLSVerify, "target-insecure-skip-tls-verify", envBool("GITSYNC_TARGET_INSECURE_SKIP_TLS_VERIFY"), "skip TLS certificate verification for the target")
137	
138		fs.StringVar(&cfg.Source.BearerToken, "source-bearer-token", envOr("GITSYNC_SOURCE_BEARER_TOKEN", ""), "source bearer token")
139		fs.StringVar(&cfg.Target.BearerToken, "target-bearer-token", envOr("GITSYNC_TARGET_BEARER_TOKEN", ""), "target bearer token")
140	
141		branches := fs.String("branch", "", "comma-separated branch list; default is all source branches")
142		fs.Var(&mappings, "map", "ref mapping in src:dst form; short names map branches, full refs map exact refs")
143		fs.BoolVar(&cfg.IncludeTags, "tags", false, "mirror tags")
144		fs.BoolVar(&cfg.ShowStats, "stats", false, "print transfer statistics")
145		fs.BoolVar(&cfg.MeasureMemory, "measure-memory", false, "sample elapsed time and Go heap usage")
146		fs.BoolVar(&jsonOutput, "json", false, "print JSON output")
147		fs.Int64Var(&cfg.MaxPackBytes, "max-pack-bytes", 0, "abort bootstrap if the streamed source pack exceeds this many bytes")
148		fs.Int64Var(&cfg.BatchMaxPackBytes, "batch-max-pack-bytes", 0, "split branch bootstrap into relay batches capped at this many bytes per batch")
149		fs.StringVar(&cfg.ProtocolMode, "protocol", envOr("GITSYNC_PROTOCOL", "auto"), "protocol mode: auto, v1, or v2")
150		fs.BoolVar(&cfg.Verbose, "v", false, "verbose logging")
151	
152		if err := fs.Parse(args); err != nil {
153			return err
154		}
155	
156		positional := fs.Args()
157		if cfg.Source.URL == "" && len(positional) > 0 {
158			cfg.Source.URL = positional[0]
159		}
160		if cfg.Target.URL == "" && len(positional) > 1 {
161			cfg.Target.URL = positional[1]
162		}
163		if len(positional) > 2 {
164			return usageError("too many positional arguments")
165		}
166	
167		if *branches != "" {
168			cfg.Branches = splitCSV(*branches)
169		}
170		for _, raw := range mappings {
171			mapping, err := parseMapping(raw)
172			if err != nil {
173				return err
174			}
175			cfg.Mappings = append(cfg.Mappings, mapping)
176		}
177	
178		if cfg.Source.URL == "" || cfg.Target.URL == "" {
179			return usageError("bootstrap requires source and target repository URLs")
180		}
181	
182		result, err := syncer.Bootstrap(ctx, cfg)
183		if err != nil {
184			return err
185		}
186		printOutput(jsonOutput, result)
187		return nil
188	}
189	
190	func runProbe(ctx context.Context, args []string) error {
191		fs := flag.NewFlagSet("probe", flag.ContinueOnError)
192		fs.SetOutput(os.Stderr)
193	
194		cfg := syncer.Config{}
195		var jsonOutput bool
196		fs.StringVar(&cfg.Source.URL, "source-url", "", "source repository URL")
197		fs.StringVar(&cfg.Target.URL, "target-url", "", "optional target repository URL")
198		fs.StringVar(&cfg.Source.Token, "source-token", envOr("GITSYNC_SOURCE_TOKEN", ""), "source token/password")
199		fs.StringVar(&cfg.Target.Token, "target-token", envOr("GITSYNC_TARGET_TOKEN", ""), "target token/password")
200		fs.StringVar(&cfg.Source.Username, "source-username", envOr("GITSYNC_SOURCE_USERNAME", "git"), "source basic auth username")
201		fs.StringVar(&cfg.Target.Username, "target-username", envOr("GITSYNC_TARGET_USERNAME", "git"), "target basic auth username")
202		fs.StringVar(&cfg.Source.BearerToken, "source-bearer-token", envOr("GITSYNC_SOURCE_BEARER_TOKEN", ""), "source bearer token")
203		fs.StringVar(&cfg.Target.BearerToken, "target-bearer-token", envOr("GITSYNC_TARGET_BEARER_TOKEN", ""), "target bearer token")
204		fs.BoolVar(&cfg.Source.SkipTLSVerify, "source-insecure-skip-tls-verify", envBool("GITSYNC_SOURCE_INSECURE_SKIP_TLS_VERIFY"), "skip TLS certificate verification for the source")
205		fs.BoolVar(&cfg.Target.SkipTLSVerify, "target-insecure-skip-tls-verify", envBool("GITSYNC_TARGET_INSECURE_SKIP_TLS_VERIFY"), "skip TLS certificate verification for the target")
206		fs.BoolVar(&cfg.IncludeTags, "tags", false, "include tag ref prefixes in probe")
207		fs.StringVar(&cfg.ProtocolMode, "protocol", envOr("GITSYNC_PROTOCOL", "auto"), "protocol mode: auto, v1, or v2")
208		fs.BoolVar(&cfg.ShowStats, "stats", false, "print transfer statistics")
209		fs.BoolVar(&cfg.MeasureMemory, "measure-memory", false, "sample elapsed time and Go heap usage")
210		fs.BoolVar(&jsonOutput, "json", false, "print JSON output")
211	
212		if err := fs.Parse(args); err != nil {
213			return err
214		}
215	
216		positional := fs.Args()
217		if cfg.Source.URL == "" && len(positional) > 0 {
218			cfg.Source.URL = positional[0]
219		}
220		if cfg.Target.URL == "" && len(positional) > 1 {
221			cfg.Target.URL = positional[1]
222		}
223		if len(positional) > 2 {
224			return usageError("too many positional arguments")
225		}
226		if cfg.Source.URL == "" {
227			return usageError("probe requires a source repository URL")
228		}
229	
230		result, err := syncer.Probe(ctx, cfg)
231		if err != nil {
232			return err
233		}
234		printOutput(jsonOutput, result)
235		return nil
236	}

238	func runFetch(ctx context.Context, args []string) error {
239		fs := flag.NewFlagSet("fetch", flag.ContinueOnError)
240		fs.SetOutput(os.Stderr)
241	
242		cfg := syncer.Config{}
243		var haveRefs multiStringFlag
244		var haveHashesRaw multiStringFlag
245		var jsonOutput bool
246	
247		fs.StringVar(&cfg.Source.URL, "source-url", "", "source repository URL")
248		fs.StringVar(&cfg.Source.Token, "source-token", envOr("GITSYNC_SOURCE_TOKEN", ""), "source token/password")
249		fs.StringVar(&cfg.Source.Username, "source-username", envOr("GITSYNC_SOURCE_USERNAME", "git"), "source basic auth username")
250		fs.StringVar(&cfg.Source.BearerToken, "source-bearer-token", envOr("GITSYNC_SOURCE_BEARER_TOKEN", ""), "source bearer token")
251		fs.BoolVar(&cfg.Source.SkipTLSVerify, "source-insecure-skip-tls-verify", envBool("GITSYNC_SOURCE_INSECURE_SKIP_TLS_VERIFY"), "skip TLS certificate verification for the source")
252		branches := fs.String("branch", "", "comma-separated branch list; default is all source branches")
253		fs.BoolVar(&cfg.IncludeTags, "tags", false, "include tags in the fetch request")
254		fs.StringVar(&cfg.ProtocolMode, "protocol", envOr("GITSYNC_PROTOCOL", "auto"), "protocol mode: auto, v1, or v2")
255		fs.BoolVar(&cfg.ShowStats, "stats", false, "print transfer statistics")
256		fs.BoolVar(&cfg.MeasureMemory, "measure-memory", false, "sample elapsed time and Go heap usage")
257		fs.BoolVar(&jsonOutput, "json", false, "print JSON output")
258		fs.Var(&haveRefs, "have-ref", "source ref name to advertise as have; short names map to branches")
259		fs.Var(&haveHashesRaw, "have", "explicit object hash to advertise as have")
260	
261		if err := fs.Parse(args); err != nil {
262			return err
263		}
264	
265		positional := fs.Args()
266		if cfg.Source.URL == "" && len(positional) > 0 {
267			cfg.Source.URL = positional[0]
268		}
269		if len(positional) > 1 {
270			return usageError("too many positional arguments")
271		}
272		if cfg.Source.URL == "" {
273			return usageError("fetch requires a source repository URL")
274		}
275		if *branches != "" {
276			cfg.Branches = splitCSV(*branches)
277		}
278	
279		haveHashes := make([]plumbing.Hash, 0, len(haveHashesRaw))
280		for _, raw := range haveHashesRaw {
281			hash := plumbing.NewHash(strings.TrimSpace(raw))
282			if hash.IsZero() {
283				return fmt.Errorf("invalid --have %q", raw)
284			}
285			haveHashes = append(haveHashes, hash)
286		}
287	
288		result, err := syncer.Fetch(ctx, cfg, haveRefs, haveHashes)
289		if err != nil {
290			return err
291		}
292		printOutput(jsonOutput, result)
293		return nil
294	}
295	
296	func printOutput(jsonOutput bool, value interface{ Lines() []string }) {
297		if jsonOutput {
298			data, err := marshalOutput(value)
299			if err != nil {
300				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "error: encode JSON output: %v\n", err)
301				os.Exit(1)
302			}
303			fmt.Println(string(data))
304			return
305		}
306	
307		for _, line := range value.Lines() {
308			fmt.Println(line)
309		}
310	}
311	
312	func marshalOutput(value interface{}) ([]byte, error) {
313		return json.MarshalIndent(value, "", "  ")
314	}
315	
316	type multiStringFlag []string
317	
318	func (m *multiStringFlag) String() string {
319		return strings.Join(*m, ",")
320	}
321	
322	func (m *multiStringFlag) Set(value string) error {
323		*m = append(*m, value)
324		return nil
325	}
326	
327	func parseMapping(raw string) (syncer.RefMapping, error) {
328		parts := strings.SplitN(raw, ":", 2)
329		if len(parts) != 2 {
330			return syncer.RefMapping{}, fmt.Errorf("invalid --map %q, expected src:dst", raw)
331		}
332		source := strings.TrimSpace(parts[0])
333		target := strings.TrimSpace(parts[1])
334		if source == "" || target == "" {
335			return syncer.RefMapping{}, fmt.Errorf("invalid --map %q, expected src:dst", raw)
336		}
337		return syncer.RefMapping{Source: source, Target: target}, nil
338	}
339	
340	func splitCSV(value string) []string {
341		parts := strings.Split(value, ",")
342		out := make([]string, 0, len(parts))
343		for _, part := range parts {
344			part = strings.TrimSpace(part)
345			if part != "" {
346				out = append(out, part)
347			}
348		}
349		return out
350	}
351	
352	func envOr(key, fallback string) string {
353		value := os.Getenv(key)
354		if value == "" {
355			return fallback
356		}
357		return value
358	}
359	
360	func envBool(key string) bool {
361		value := strings.TrimSpace(strings.ToLower(os.Getenv(key)))
362		return value == "1" || value == "true" || value == "yes" || value == "on"
363	}

365	func usageError(message string) error {
366		usage := "usage:\n  git-sync sync [flags] <source-url> <target-url>\n  git-sync plan [flags] <source-url> <target-url>\n  git-sync bootstrap [flags] <source-url> <target-url>\n  git-sync probe [flags] <source-url> [target-url]\n  git-sync fetch [flags] <source-url>\n\nsync/plan flags:\n  --branch main,dev\n  --map main:stable\n  --tags\n  --force\n  --prune\n  --stats\n  --measure-memory\n  --json\n  --protocol auto|v1|v2\n  --source-token ...\n  --target-token ...\n  --source-username git\n  --target-username git\n  --source-bearer-token ...\n  --target-bearer-token ...\n  --source-insecure-skip-tls-verify\n  --target-insecure-skip-tls-verify\n  -v\n\nbootstrap flags:\n  --branch main,dev\n  --map main:stable\n  --tags\n  --max-pack-bytes 104857600\n  --batch-max-pack-bytes 1073741824\n  --stats\n  --measure-memory\n  --json\n  --protocol auto|v1|v2\n  --source-token ...\n  --target-token ...\n  --source-username git\n  --target-username git\n  --source-bearer-token ...\n  --target-bearer-token ...\n  --source-insecure-skip-tls-verify\n  --target-insecure-skip-tls-verify\n  -v\n\nprobe flags:\n  --tags\n  --stats\n  --measure-memory\n  --json\n  --protocol auto|v1|v2\n  --source-token ...\n  --source-username git\n  --source-bearer-token ...\n  --target-token ...\n  --target-username git\n  --target-bearer-token ...\n  --source-insecure-skip-tls-verify\n  --target-insecure-skip-tls-verify\n\nfetch flags:\n  --branch main,dev\n  --tags\n  --stats\n  --measure-memory\n  --json\n  --protocol auto|v1|v2\n  --have-ref main\n  --have <hash>\n  --source-token ...\n  --source-username git\n  --source-bearer-token ...\n  --source-insecure-skip-tls-verify\n"
367		if message == "" {
368			return errors.New(strings.TrimSpace(usage))
369		}
370		return fmt.Errorf("%s\n\n%s", message, usage)
371	}

256	func (r Result) Lines() []string {
257		lines := make([]string, 0, len(r.Plans)+8)
258		for _, plan := range r.Plans {
259			label := plan.Branch
260			if plan.TargetRef != "" {
261				label = plan.TargetRef.String()
262			}
263			line := fmt.Sprintf("%s %s", strings.ToUpper(string(plan.Action)), label)
264			if plan.Reason != "" {
265				line += " - " + plan.Reason
266			}
267			lines = append(lines, line)
268		}
269	
270		summary := fmt.Sprintf(
271			"summary: pushed=%d deleted=%d skipped=%d blocked=%d protocol=%s relay=%t relay-mode=%s relay-reason=%s batching=%t batch-count=%d planned-batches=%d",
272			r.Pushed, r.Deleted, r.Skipped, r.Blocked, r.Protocol, r.Relay, r.RelayMode, r.RelayReason, r.Batching, r.BatchCount, r.PlannedBatchCount,
273		)
274		if r.DryRun {
275			summary += " dry-run=true"
276		}
277		lines = append(lines, summary)
278	
279		if r.Stats.Enabled {
280			keys := make([]string, 0, len(r.Stats.Items))
281			for key := range r.Stats.Items {
282				keys = append(keys, key)
283			}
284			sort.Strings(keys)
285			for _, key := range keys {
286				item := r.Stats.Items[key]
287				lines = append(lines, fmt.Sprintf(
288					"stats: %s requests=%d request-bytes=%d response-bytes=%d wants=%d haves=%d commands=%d",
289					item.Name, item.Requests, item.RequestBytes, item.ResponseBytes, item.Wants, item.Haves, item.Commands,
290				))
291			}
292		}
293		if r.Measurement.Enabled {
294			lines = append(lines, fmt.Sprintf(
295				"measurement: elapsed-ms=%d peak-alloc-bytes=%d peak-heap-inuse-bytes=%d total-alloc-bytes=%d gc-count=%d",
296				r.Measurement.ElapsedMillis, r.Measurement.PeakAllocBytes, r.Measurement.PeakHeapInuseBytes, r.Measurement.TotalAllocBytes, r.Measurement.GCCount,
297			))
298		}
299		if r.BootstrapSuggested {
300			lines = append(lines, "hint: target refs are absent; bootstrap can seed them without local object storage")
301		}
302	
303		if r.Batching && len(r.TempRefs) > 0 {
304			lines = append(lines, fmt.Sprintf("batching: temp-refs=%s", strings.Join(r.TempRefs, ",")))
305		}
306	
307		return lines
308	}

396	
397	func Run(ctx context.Context, cfg Config) (Result, error) {
398		measurementDone := startMeasurement(cfg.MeasureMemory)
399		if cfg.ProtocolMode == "" {
400			cfg.ProtocolMode = protocolModeAuto
401		}
402		if cfg.ProtocolMode != protocolModeAuto && cfg.ProtocolMode != protocolModeV1 && cfg.ProtocolMode != protocolModeV2 {
403			return Result{}, fmt.Errorf("unsupported protocol mode %q", cfg.ProtocolMode)
404		}
405	
406		repo, err := git.Init(memory.NewStorage(), nil)
407		if err != nil {
408			return Result{}, fmt.Errorf("init in-memory repository: %w", err)
409		}
410	
411		stats := newStats(cfg.ShowStats)
412		sourceConn, err := newTransportConn(cfg.Source, "source", stats)
413		if err != nil {
414			return Result{}, fmt.Errorf("create source transport: %w", err)
415		}
416		targetConn, err := newTransportConn(cfg.Target, "target", stats)
417		if err != nil {
418			return Result{}, fmt.Errorf("create target transport: %w", err)
419		}
420	
421		sourceRefs, sourceService, err := listSourceRefs(ctx, sourceConn, cfg)
422		if err != nil {
423			return Result{}, fmt.Errorf("list source refs: %w", err)
424		}
425		targetAdv, err := advertisedRefsV1(ctx, targetConn, transport.ReceivePackServiceName)
426		if err != nil {
427			return Result{}, fmt.Errorf("list target refs: %w", err)
428		}
429		targetRefs, err := advertisedReferences(targetAdv)
430		if err != nil {
431			return Result{}, fmt.Errorf("decode target refs: %w", err)
432		}
433	
434		sourceRefMap := refHashMap(sourceRefs)
435		targetRefMap := refHashMap(targetRefs)
436	
437		desiredRefs, managedTargets, err := buildDesiredRefs(sourceRefMap, cfg)
438		if err != nil {
439			return Result{}, err
440		}
441		if len(desiredRefs) == 0 {
442			return Result{}, fmt.Errorf("no source refs matched")
443		}
444		if ok, reason := canBootstrapRelay(cfg, desiredRefs, targetRefMap); ok {
445			if cfg.DryRun {
446				plans, err := buildBootstrapPlans(desiredRefs, targetRefMap)
447				if err != nil {
448					return Result{}, err
449				}
450				return Result{
451					Plans:              plans,
452					DryRun:             true,
453					Relay:              false,
454					RelayMode:          "",
455					RelayReason:        reason,
456					BootstrapSuggested: true,
457					Stats:              stats.snapshot(),
458					Measurement:        measurementDone(),
459					Protocol:           sourceService.protocol,
460				}, nil
461			}
462			return bootstrapWithInputs(ctx, cfg, stats, sourceConn, targetConn, sourceService, targetAdv, desiredRefs, targetRefMap, reason)
463		}
464	
465		if err := sourceService.Fetch(ctx, repo, sourceConn, desiredRefs, targetRefMap); err != nil {
466			if !errors.Is(err, git.NoErrAlreadyUpToDate) {
467				return Result{}, err
468			}
469		}
470	
471		plans, err := buildPlans(repo, desiredRefs, targetRefMap, managedTargets, cfg)
472		if err != nil {
473			return Result{}, err
474		}
475	
476		result := Result{
477			Plans:       plans,
478			DryRun:      cfg.DryRun,
479			Relay:       false,
480			RelayMode:   "",
481			RelayReason: "",
482			Stats:       stats.snapshot(),
483			Measurement: measurementDone(),
484			Protocol:    sourceService.protocol,
485		}
486	
487		pushPlans := make([]BranchPlan, 0, len(plans))
488		for _, plan := range plans {
489			switch plan.Action {
490			case ActionCreate, ActionUpdate:
491				if cfg.DryRun {
492					result.Skipped++
493					continue
494				}
495				pushPlans = append(pushPlans, plan)
496			case ActionDelete:
497				if cfg.DryRun {
498					result.Skipped++
499					continue
500				}
501				pushPlans = append(pushPlans, plan)
502			case ActionSkip:
503				result.Skipped++
504			case ActionBlock:
505				result.Blocked++
506			}
507		}
508	
509		if !cfg.DryRun && result.Blocked > 0 {
510			return result, fmt.Errorf("blocked %d ref update(s); rerun with --force where appropriate", result.Blocked)
511		}
512		result.RelayReason = relayFallbackReason(cfg, pushPlans, targetAdv)
513	
514		if !cfg.DryRun {
515			if ok, reason := canIncrementalRelay(cfg, pushPlans, targetAdv); ok {
516				relayPlans := append([]BranchPlan(nil), pushPlans...)
517				desiredRelay := desiredSubsetForPlans(desiredRefs, relayPlans)
518				packReader, err := sourceService.FetchPack(ctx, sourceConn, desiredRelay, targetRefMap)
519				if err != nil {
520					return result, fmt.Errorf("fetch source pack: %w", err)
521				}
522				defer packReader.Close()
523				packReader = limitPackReadCloser(packReader, cfg.MaxPackBytes)
524				if err := pushPackToTarget(ctx, targetConn, targetAdv, relayPlans, packReader, cfg.Verbose); err != nil {
525					return result, fmt.Errorf("push target refs: %w", err)
526				}
527				result.Relay = true
528				result.RelayMode = "incremental"
529				result.RelayReason = reason
530			} else if ok, reason := canFullTagCreateRelay(pushPlans); ok {
531				relayPlans := append([]BranchPlan(nil), pushPlans...)
532				desiredRelay := desiredSubsetForPlans(desiredRefs, relayPlans)
533				packReader, err := sourceService.FetchPack(ctx, sourceConn, desiredRelay, nil)
534				if err != nil {
535					return result, fmt.Errorf("fetch source tag pack: %w", err)
536				}
537				defer packReader.Close()
538				packReader = limitPackReadCloser(packReader, cfg.MaxPackBytes)
539				if err := pushPackToTarget(ctx, targetConn, targetAdv, relayPlans, packReader, cfg.Verbose); err != nil {
540					return result, fmt.Errorf("push target refs: %w", err)
541				}
542				result.Relay = true
543				result.RelayMode = "incremental"
544				result.RelayReason = reason
545			} else if len(pushPlans) > 0 {
546				if err := ensureLocalObjectsForPush(ctx, repo, sourceConn, sourceService, desiredRefs, pushPlans); err != nil {
547					return result, fmt.Errorf("prepare local objects for push: %w", err)
548				}
549				if err := pushToTarget(ctx, repo, targetConn, targetAdv, pushPlans, targetRefMap, cfg.Verbose); err != nil {
550					return result, fmt.Errorf("push target refs: %w", err)
551				}
552			}
553		}
554	
555		for _, plan := range pushPlans {
556			switch plan.Action {
557			case ActionCreate, ActionUpdate:
558				result.Pushed++
559			case ActionDelete:
560				result.Deleted++
561			}
562		}
563	
564		result.Stats = stats.snapshot()
565		result.Measurement = measurementDone()
566		return result, nil
567	}
568	
569	func Bootstrap(ctx context.Context, cfg Config) (Result, error) {
570		measurementDone := startMeasurement(cfg.MeasureMemory)
571		if cfg.ProtocolMode == "" {
572			cfg.ProtocolMode = protocolModeAuto
573		}
574		if cfg.ProtocolMode != protocolModeAuto && cfg.ProtocolMode != protocolModeV1 && cfg.ProtocolMode != protocolModeV2 {
575			return Result{}, fmt.Errorf("unsupported protocol mode %q", cfg.ProtocolMode)
576		}
577		if cfg.Force {
578			return Result{}, fmt.Errorf("bootstrap does not support --force")
579		}
580		if cfg.Prune {
581			return Result{}, fmt.Errorf("bootstrap does not support --prune")
582		}
583		if cfg.DryRun {
584			return Result{}, fmt.Errorf("bootstrap does not support dry-run; use plan or sync")
585		}
586	
587		stats := newStats(cfg.ShowStats)
588		sourceConn, err := newTransportConn(cfg.Source, "source", stats)
589		if err != nil {
590			return Result{}, fmt.Errorf("create source transport: %w", err)
591		}
592		targetConn, err := newTransportConn(cfg.Target, "target", stats)
593		if err != nil {
594			return Result{}, fmt.Errorf("create target transport: %w", err)
595		}
596	
597		sourceRefs, sourceService, err := listSourceRefs(ctx, sourceConn, cfg)
598		if err != nil {
599			return Result{}, fmt.Errorf("list source refs: %w", err)
600		}
601		targetAdv, err := advertisedRefsV1(ctx, targetConn, transport.ReceivePackServiceName)
602		if err != nil {
603			return Result{}, fmt.Errorf("list target refs: %w", err)
604		}
605		targetRefs, err := advertisedReferences(targetAdv)
606		if err != nil {
607			return Result{}, fmt.Errorf("decode target refs: %w", err)
608		}
609	
610		sourceRefMap := refHashMap(sourceRefs)
611		targetRefMap := refHashMap(targetRefs)
612	
613		desiredRefs, _, err := buildDesiredRefs(sourceRefMap, cfg)
614		if err != nil {
615			return Result{}, err
616		}
617		if len(desiredRefs) == 0 {
618			return Result{}, fmt.Errorf("no source refs matched")
619		}
620		_, reason := canBootstrapRelay(cfg, desiredRefs, targetRefMap)
621		result, err := bootstrapWithInputs(ctx, cfg, stats, sourceConn, targetConn, sourceService, targetAdv, desiredRefs, targetRefMap, reason)
622		result.Measurement = measurementDone()
623		return result, err
624	}
625	
626	func Probe(ctx context.Context, cfg Config) (ProbeResult, error) {
627		measurementDone := startMeasurement(cfg.MeasureMemory)
628		if cfg.ProtocolMode == "" {
629			cfg.ProtocolMode = protocolModeAuto
630		}
631		if cfg.ProtocolMode != protocolModeAuto && cfg.ProtocolMode != protocolModeV1 && cfg.ProtocolMode != protocolModeV2 {
632			return ProbeResult{}, fmt.Errorf("unsupported protocol mode %q", cfg.ProtocolMode)
633		}
634		if cfg.Source.URL == "" {
635			return ProbeResult{}, fmt.Errorf("source repository URL is required")
636		}
637	
638		stats := newStats(cfg.ShowStats)
639		sourceConn, err := newTransportConn(cfg.Source, "source", stats)
640		if err != nil {
641			return ProbeResult{}, fmt.Errorf("create source transport: %w", err)
642		}
643	
644		refs, service, err := listSourceRefs(ctx, sourceConn, cfg)
645		if err != nil {
646			return ProbeResult{}, fmt.Errorf("list source refs: %w", err)
647		}
648	
649		refInfos := make([]RefInfo, 0, len(refs))
650		for _, ref := range refs {
651			if ref.Type() != plumbing.HashReference {
652				continue
653			}
654			refInfos = append(refInfos, RefInfo{
655				Name: ref.Name().String(),
656				Hash: ref.Hash(),
657			})
658		}
659		sort.Slice(refInfos, func(i, j int) bool {
660			return refInfos[i].Name < refInfos[j].Name
661		})
662	
663		result := ProbeResult{
664			SourceURL:     cfg.Source.URL,
665			RequestedMode: cfg.ProtocolMode,
666			Protocol:      service.protocol,
667			RefPrefixes:   sourceRefPrefixes(cfg),
668			Capabilities:  sourceCapabilities(service),
669			Refs:          refInfos,
670			Stats:         stats.snapshot(),
671			Measurement:   measurementDone(),
672		}
673	
674		if cfg.Target.URL != "" {
675			targetConn, err := newTransportConn(cfg.Target, "target", stats)
676			if err != nil {
677				return ProbeResult{}, fmt.Errorf("create target transport: %w", err)
678			}
679			targetAdv, err := advertisedRefsV1(ctx, targetConn, transport.ReceivePackServiceName)
680			if err != nil {
681				return ProbeResult{}, fmt.Errorf("list target refs: %w", err)
682			}
683			result.TargetURL = cfg.Target.URL
684			result.TargetCaps = advCapabilities(targetAdv)
685			result.Stats = stats.snapshot()
686			result.Measurement = measurementDone()
687		}
688	
689		return result, nil
690	}
691	
692	func Fetch(ctx context.Context, cfg Config, haveRefs []string, haveHashes []plumbing.Hash) (FetchResult, error) {
693		measurementDone := startMeasurement(cfg.MeasureMemory)
694		if cfg.ProtocolMode == "" {
695			cfg.ProtocolMode = protocolModeAuto
696		}
697		if cfg.ProtocolMode != protocolModeAuto && cfg.ProtocolMode != protocolModeV1 && cfg.ProtocolMode != protocolModeV2 {
698			return FetchResult{}, fmt.Errorf("unsupported protocol mode %q", cfg.ProtocolMode)
699		}
700		if cfg.Source.URL == "" {
701			return FetchResult{}, fmt.Errorf("source repository URL is required")
702		}
703	
704		repo, err := git.Init(memory.NewStorage(), nil)
705		if err != nil {
706			return FetchResult{}, fmt.Errorf("init in-memory repository: %w", err)
707		}
708	
709		stats := newStats(cfg.ShowStats)
710		sourceConn, err := newTransportConn(cfg.Source, "source", stats)
711		if err != nil {
712			return FetchResult{}, fmt.Errorf("create source transport: %w", err)
713		}
714	
715		sourceRefs, sourceService, err := listSourceRefs(ctx, sourceConn, cfg)
716		if err != nil {
717			return FetchResult{}, fmt.Errorf("list source refs: %w", err)
718		}
719		sourceRefMap := refHashMap(sourceRefs)
720	
721		desiredRefs, _, err := buildDesiredRefs(sourceRefMap, cfg)
722		if err != nil {
723			return FetchResult{}, err
724		}
725		if len(desiredRefs) == 0 {
726			return FetchResult{}, fmt.Errorf("no source refs matched")
727		}
728	
729		targetRefMap := make(map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash)
730		for _, raw := range haveRefs {
731			name := parseHaveRef(raw)
732			hash, ok := sourceRefMap[name]
733			if !ok {
734				return FetchResult{}, fmt.Errorf("have-ref %q not found on source", raw)
735			}
736			targetRefMap[name] = hash
737		}
738		for idx, hash := range haveHashes {
739			targetRefMap[plumbing.ReferenceName(fmt.Sprintf("refs/haves/%d", idx))] = hash
740		}
741	
742		if err := sourceService.Fetch(ctx, repo, sourceConn, desiredRefs, targetRefMap); err != nil {
743			if !errors.Is(err, git.NoErrAlreadyUpToDate) {
744				return FetchResult{}, err
745			}
746		}
747	
748		wants := make([]RefInfo, 0, len(desiredRefs))
749		for _, ref := range desiredRefs {
750			wants = append(wants, RefInfo{Name: ref.SourceRef.String(), Hash: ref.SourceHash})
751		}
752		sort.Slice(wants, func(i, j int) bool { return wants[i].Name < wants[j].Name })
753	
754		objectCount, err := countObjects(repo.Storer)
755		if err != nil {
756			return FetchResult{}, fmt.Errorf("count fetched objects: %w", err)
757		}
758	
759		return FetchResult{
760			SourceURL:      cfg.Source.URL,
761			RequestedMode:  cfg.ProtocolMode,
762			Protocol:       sourceService.protocol,
763			Wants:          wants,
764			Haves:          sortedUniqueHashes(mapsRefValues(targetRefMap)),
765			FetchedObjects: objectCount,
766			Stats:          stats.snapshot(),
767			Measurement:    measurementDone(),
768		}, nil
769	}
770	
771	func buildBootstrapPlans(
772		desired map[plumbing.ReferenceName]desiredRef,
773		targetRefs map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash,
774	) ([]BranchPlan, error) {
775		targetNames := make([]plumbing.ReferenceName, 0, len(desired))
776		for _, want := range desired {
777			targetNames = append(targetNames, want.TargetRef)
778		}
779		sort.Slice(targetNames, func(i, j int) bool { return targetNames[i] < targetNames[j] })
780	
781		plans := make([]BranchPlan, 0, len(targetNames))
782		for _, targetRef := range targetNames {
783			targetHash := targetRefs[targetRef]
784			if !targetHash.IsZero() {
785				return nil, fmt.Errorf("target ref %s already exists; use sync for non-bootstrap runs", targetRef)
786			}
787			want := desired[targetRef]
788			plans = append(plans, BranchPlan{
789				Branch:     want.Label,
790				SourceRef:  want.SourceRef,
791				TargetRef:  want.TargetRef,
792				SourceHash: want.SourceHash,
793				TargetHash: plumbing.ZeroHash,
794				Kind:       want.Kind,
795				Action:     ActionCreate,
796				Reason:     fmt.Sprintf("create %s at %s", want.TargetRef, shortHash(want.SourceHash)),
797			})
798		}
799		return plans, nil

206	type sourceRefService struct {
207		protocol string
208		v1       *packp.AdvRefs
209		v2       *v2CapabilityAdvertisement
210	}
211	
212	func listSourceRefs(ctx context.Context, conn *transportConn, cfg Config) ([]*plumbing.Reference, *sourceRefService, error) {
213		switch cfg.ProtocolMode {
214		case protocolModeV1:
215			adv, refs, err := listSourceRefsV1(ctx, conn)
216			if err != nil {
217				return nil, nil, err
218			}
219			return refs, &sourceRefService{protocol: protocolModeV1, v1: adv}, nil
220		case protocolModeAuto, protocolModeV2:
221			data, err := requestInfoRefs(ctx, conn, transport.UploadPackServiceName, "version=2")
222			if err != nil {
223				return nil, nil, err
224			}
225	
226			if adv, err := decodeV2CapabilityAdvertisement(bytes.NewReader(data)); err == nil {
227				if !adv.Supports("ls-refs") || !adv.Supports("fetch") {
228					return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("source does not advertise required protocol v2 commands")
229				}
230				refs, err := listSourceRefsV2(ctx, conn, adv, cfg)
231				if err != nil {
232					return nil, nil, err
233				}
234				return refs, &sourceRefService{protocol: protocolModeV2, v2: adv}, nil
235			}
236	
237			if cfg.ProtocolMode == protocolModeV2 {
238				return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("source did not negotiate protocol v2")
239			}
240	
241			adv, err := decodeV1AdvertisedRefs(data)
242			if err != nil {
243				return nil, nil, err
244			}
245			refs, err := advertisedReferences(adv)
246			if err != nil {
247				return nil, nil, err
248			}
249			return refs, &sourceRefService{protocol: protocolModeV1, v1: adv}, nil
250		default:
251			return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("unsupported protocol mode %q", cfg.ProtocolMode)
252		}
253	}

255	func (s *sourceRefService) Fetch(ctx context.Context, repo *git.Repository, conn *transportConn, desired map[plumbing.ReferenceName]desiredRef, targetRefs map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash) error {
256		switch s.protocol {
257		case protocolModeV2:
258			return fetchSourceRefsV2(ctx, repo, conn, s.v2, desired, targetRefs)
259		case protocolModeV1:
260			return fetchSourceRefsWithHavesV1(ctx, repo, conn, s.v1, desired, targetRefs)
261		default:
262			return fmt.Errorf("unsupported source protocol %q", s.protocol)
263		}
264	}

266	func (s *sourceRefService) FetchPack(ctx context.Context, conn *transportConn, desired map[plumbing.ReferenceName]desiredRef, targetRefs map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash) (io.ReadCloser, error) {
267		switch s.protocol {
268		case protocolModeV2:
269			return fetchSourcePackV2(ctx, conn, s.v2, desired, targetRefs)
270		case protocolModeV1:

272		default:

274		}

277	func sourceCapabilities(s *sourceRefService) []string {
278		switch s.protocol {
279		case protocolModeV2:
280			keys := make([]string, 0, len(s.v2.Capabilities))
281			for key, value := range s.v2.Capabilities {
282				if value == "" {
283					keys = append(keys, key)
284					continue
285				}
286				keys = append(keys, key+"="+value)
287			}
288			sort.Strings(keys)
289			return keys
290		case protocolModeV1:
291			if s.v1 == nil || s.v1.Capabilities == nil {
292				return nil
293			}
294			all := s.v1.Capabilities.All()
295			items := make([]string, 0, len(all))
296			for _, cap := range all {
297				values := s.v1.Capabilities.Get(cap)
298				if len(values) == 0 {
299					items = append(items, string(cap))
300					continue
301				}
302				for _, value := range values {
303					items = append(items, string(cap)+"="+value)
304				}
305			}
306			sort.Strings(items)
307			return items
308		default:
309			return nil
310		}
311	}

322				items = append(items, string(cap))
323				continue
324			}
325			for _, value := range values {
326				items = append(items, string(cap)+"="+value)
327			}
328		}

363	func fetchSourceRefsV2(
364		ctx context.Context,
365		repo *git.Repository,
366		conn *transportConn,
367		adv *v2CapabilityAdvertisement,
368		desired map[plumbing.ReferenceName]desiredRef,
369		targetRefs map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash,
370	) error {
371		wants := make([]plumbing.Hash, 0, len(desired))
372		for _, ref := range desired {
373			wants = append(wants, ref.SourceHash)
374		}
375		wants = sortedUniqueHashes(wants)
376		haves := sortedUniqueHashes(mapsRefValues(targetRefs))
377		if len(wants) == 0 {
378			return git.NoErrAlreadyUpToDate
379		}
380	
381		commandArgs := make([]string, 0, len(wants)+len(haves)+4)
382		commandArgs = append(commandArgs, "ofs-delta", "no-progress")
383		for _, hash := range wants {
384			commandArgs = append(commandArgs, "want "+hash.String())
385		}
386		for _, hash := range haves {
387			commandArgs = append(commandArgs, "have "+hash.String())
388		}
389		commandArgs = append(commandArgs, "done")
390		conn.stats.addWantsHaves("source upload-pack", len(wants), len(haves))
391	
392		body, err := encodeV2CommandRequest("fetch", v2RequestCapabilities(adv), commandArgs)
393		if err != nil {
394			return err
395		}
396	
397		reader, err := postRPCStreamWithPhase(ctx, conn, transport.UploadPackServiceName, body, true, "upload-pack fetch")
398		if err != nil {
399			return err
400		}
401		defer ioutil.CheckClose(reader, &err)
402	
403		if err := storeV2FetchPack(repo, reader); err != nil {
404			return err
405		}
406	
407		return storeFetchedSourceRefs(repo, desired)
408	}
