410	func fetchSourceCommitGraphV2(
411		ctx context.Context,
412		repo *git.Repository,
413		conn *transportConn,
414		adv *v2CapabilityAdvertisement,
415		ref desiredRef,
416	) error {
417		if !fetchCapabilitySupports(adv, "filter") {
418			return fmt.Errorf("source does not advertise fetch filter support")
419		}
420	
421		commandArgs := []string{
422			"ofs-delta",
423			"no-progress",
424			"filter tree:0",
425			"want " + ref.SourceHash.String(),
426			"done",
427		}
428		conn.stats.addWantsHaves("source upload-pack", 1, 0)
429	
430		body, err := encodeV2CommandRequest("fetch", v2RequestCapabilities(adv), commandArgs)
431		if err != nil {
432			return err
433		}
434	
435		reader, err := postRPCStreamWithPhase(ctx, conn, transport.UploadPackServiceName, body, true, "upload-pack fetch")
436		if err != nil {
437			return err
438		}
439		defer ioutil.CheckClose(reader, &err)
440	
441		if err := storeV2FetchPack(repo, reader); err != nil {
442			return err
443		}
444		return storeFetchedSourceRefs(repo, singleDesiredRef(ref.SourceRef, ref.TargetRef, ref.SourceHash))
445	}
446	
447	func fetchSourcePackV2(
448		ctx context.Context,
449		conn *transportConn,
450		adv *v2CapabilityAdvertisement,
451		desired map[plumbing.ReferenceName]desiredRef,
452		targetRefs map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash,
453	) (io.ReadCloser, error) {
454		body, wants, haves, err := sourceFetchRequestV2(adv, desired, targetRefs)
455		if err != nil {
456			return nil, err
457		}
458		if wants == 0 {
459			return nil, git.NoErrAlreadyUpToDate
460		}
461		conn.stats.addWantsHaves("source upload-pack", wants, haves)
462	
463		reader, err := postRPCStreamWithPhase(ctx, conn, transport.UploadPackServiceName, body, true, "upload-pack fetch")
464		if err != nil {
465			return nil, err
466		}
467		packReader, err := openV2FetchPackStream(reader)
468		if err != nil {
469			_ = reader.Close()
470			return nil, err
471		}
472		return packReader, nil
473	}
474	
475	func storeV2FetchPack(repo *git.Repository, r io.Reader) error {
476		reader := newPacketReader(r)
477		for {
478			kind, payload, err := reader.ReadPacket()
479			if err != nil {
480				if errors.Is(err, io.EOF) {
481					return nil
482				}
483				return fmt.Errorf("decode protocol v2 fetch response: %w", err)
484			}
485	
486			switch kind {
487			case packetTypeFlush:
488				return nil
489			case packetTypeDelim, packetTypeResponseEnd:
490				continue
491			case packetTypeData:
492				line := string(payload)
493				switch line {
494				case "packfile\n":
495					demux := sideband.NewDemuxer(sideband.Sideband64k, reader.Reader())
496					if err := packfile.UpdateObjectStorage(repo.Storer, demux); err != nil {
497						return fmt.Errorf("store source packfile: %w", err)
498					}
499					return nil
500				case "acknowledgments\n", "shallow-info\n":
501					if err := skipV2Section(reader); err != nil {
502						return err
503					}
504				default:
505					return fmt.Errorf("unexpected protocol v2 fetch section %q", strings.TrimSpace(line))
506				}
507			}
508		}
509	}
510	
511	func openV2FetchPackStream(body io.ReadCloser) (io.ReadCloser, error) {
512		reader := newPacketReader(body)
513		for {
514			kind, payload, err := reader.ReadPacket()
515			if err != nil {
516				if errors.Is(err, io.EOF) {
517					return nil, io.ErrUnexpectedEOF
518				}
519				return nil, fmt.Errorf("decode protocol v2 fetch response: %w", err)
520			}
521	
522			switch kind {
523			case packetTypeFlush:
524				return nil, io.ErrUnexpectedEOF
525			case packetTypeDelim, packetTypeResponseEnd:
526				continue
527			case packetTypeData:
528				line := string(payload)
529				switch line {
530				case "packfile\n":
531					return &wrappedReadCloser{
532						Reader: sideband.NewDemuxer(sideband.Sideband64k, reader.Reader()),
533						Closer: body,
534					}, nil
535				case "acknowledgments\n", "shallow-info\n":
536					if err := skipV2Section(reader); err != nil {
537						return nil, err
538					}
539				default:
540					return nil, fmt.Errorf("unexpected protocol v2 fetch section %q", strings.TrimSpace(line))
541				}
542			}
543		}
544	}
545	
546	func skipV2Section(reader *packetReader) error {
547		for {
548			kind, _, err := reader.ReadPacket()
549			if err != nil {
550				return err
551			}
552			if kind == packetTypeDelim || kind == packetTypeFlush {
553				return nil
554			}
555		}
556	}
557	
558	func decodeV2LSRefs(r io.Reader) ([]*plumbing.Reference, error) {
559		reader := newPacketReader(r)
560		var refs []*plumbing.Reference
561		for {
562			kind, payload, err := reader.ReadPacket()
563			if err != nil {
564				return nil, err
565			}
566			if kind == packetTypeFlush {
567				return refs, nil
568			}
569			if kind != packetTypeData {
570				return nil, fmt.Errorf("unexpected packet type %v in ls-refs response", kind)
571			}
572	
573			fields := strings.Fields(strings.TrimSpace(string(payload)))
574			if len(fields) < 2 {
575				return nil, fmt.Errorf("malformed ls-refs response line %q", payload)
576			}
577			hash := plumbing.NewHash(fields[0])
578			name := plumbing.ReferenceName(fields[1])
579			refs = append(refs, plumbing.NewHashReference(name, hash))
580		}
581	}
582	
583	func sourceRefPrefixes(cfg Config) []string {
584		prefixSet := map[string]struct{}{}
585	
586		addPrefix := func(ref plumbing.ReferenceName) {
587			switch {
588			case ref.IsBranch():
589				prefixSet["refs/heads/"] = struct{}{}
590			case ref.IsTag():
591				prefixSet["refs/tags/"] = struct{}{}
592			}
593		}
594	
595		if len(cfg.Mappings) > 0 {
596			for _, mapping := range cfg.Mappings {
597				sourceRef, _, _, err := normalizeMapping(mapping)
598				if err != nil {
599					continue
600				}
601				addPrefix(sourceRef)
602			}
603		} else {
604			prefixSet["refs/heads/"] = struct{}{}
605		}
606		if cfg.IncludeTags {
607			prefixSet["refs/tags/"] = struct{}{}
608		}
609	
610		prefixes := make([]string, 0, len(prefixSet))
611		for prefix := range prefixSet {
612			prefixes = append(prefixes, prefix)
613		}
614		sort.Strings(prefixes)
615		return prefixes
616	}

618	func fetchCapabilitySupports(adv *v2CapabilityAdvertisement, feature string) bool {
619		if adv == nil {
620			return false
621		}
622		values := strings.Fields(adv.Value("fetch"))
623		for _, value := range values {
624			if value == feature {
625				return true
626			}
627		}
628		return false
629	}

638	
639	func sourceFetchRequestV2(
640		adv *v2CapabilityAdvertisement,
641		desired map[plumbing.ReferenceName]desiredRef,
642		targetRefs map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash,
643	) ([]byte, int, int, error) {
644		wants := make([]plumbing.Hash, 0, len(desired))
645		for _, ref := range desired {
646			wants = append(wants, ref.SourceHash)
647		}
648		wants = sortedUniqueHashes(wants)
649		haves := sortedUniqueHashes(mapsRefValues(targetRefs))
650		if len(wants) == 0 {
651			return nil, 0, 0, nil
652		}
653	
654		commandArgs := make([]string, 0, len(wants)+len(haves)+4)
655		commandArgs = append(commandArgs, "ofs-delta", "no-progress")
656		if desiredHasTag(desired) {
657			commandArgs = append(commandArgs, "include-tag")
658		}
659		for _, hash := range wants {
660			commandArgs = append(commandArgs, "want "+hash.String())
661		}
662		for _, hash := range haves {
663			commandArgs = append(commandArgs, "have "+hash.String())
664		}
665		commandArgs = append(commandArgs, "done")
666	
667		body, err := encodeV2CommandRequest("fetch", v2RequestCapabilities(adv), commandArgs)
668		if err != nil {
669			return nil, 0, 0, err
670		}
671		return body, len(wants), len(haves), nil
672	}

679	func storeFetchedSourceRefs(repo *git.Repository, desired map[plumbing.ReferenceName]desiredRef) error {
680		for _, ref := range desired {
681			localRef := plumbing.ReferenceName(localBranchRef(sourceRemoteName, ref.TargetRef.Short()))
682			if ref.Kind == RefKindTag {
683				localRef = plumbing.ReferenceName("refs/remotes/" + sourceRemoteName + "/tags/" + ref.TargetRef.Short())
684			}
685			if err := repo.Storer.SetReference(plumbing.NewHashReference(localRef, ref.SourceHash)); err != nil {
686				return fmt.Errorf("set local source ref %s: %w", ref.SourceRef, err)
687			}
688		}
689		return nil
690	}

692	func requestInfoRefs(ctx context.Context, conn *transportConn, service, gitProtocol string) ([]byte, error) {
693		url := fmt.Sprintf("%s/info/refs?service=%s", conn.endpoint.String(), service)
694		req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodGet, url, nil)
695		if err != nil {

697		}
698		req.Header.Set("Accept", "*/*")
699		req.Header.Set("User-Agent", capability.DefaultAgent())
700		req.Header.Set(statsPhaseHdr, service+" info-refs")

709		}
710		defer res.Body.Close()
711		if err := transporthttp.NewErr(res); err != nil {
712			return nil, err
713		}

758	func postRPCStreamWithPhase(ctx context.Context, conn *transportConn, service string, body []byte, gitProtocolV2 bool, phase string) (io.ReadCloser, error) {
759		url := fmt.Sprintf("%s/%s", conn.endpoint.String(), service)
760		req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodPost, url, bytes.NewReader(body))
761		if err != nil {
762			return nil, err
763		}
764		req.Header.Set("Content-Type", fmt.Sprintf("application/x-%s-request", service))
765		req.Header.Set("Accept", fmt.Sprintf("application/x-%s-result", service))
766		req.Header.Set("User-Agent", capability.DefaultAgent())
767		req.Header.Set(statsPhaseHdr, phase)
768		if gitProtocolV2 {
769			req.Header.Set("Git-Protocol", "version=2")
770		}
771		applyAuth(req, conn.authMethod())
772	
773		res, err := conn.http.Do(req)
774		if err != nil {
775			return nil, err
776		}
777		if err := transporthttp.NewErr(res); err != nil {
778			_ = res.Body.Close()
779			return nil, err
780		}
781		return res.Body, nil
782	}

801	
802	func canBootstrapRelay(
803		cfg Config,
804		desired map[plumbing.ReferenceName]desiredRef,
805		targetRefs map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash,
806	) (bool, string) {
807		if cfg.Force || cfg.Prune {
808			return false, "bootstrap-disabled-by-force-or-prune"
809		}
810		if len(desired) == 0 {
811			return false, "bootstrap-no-managed-refs"
812		}
813		for targetRef := range desired {
814			if !targetRefs[targetRef].IsZero() {
815				return false, "bootstrap-target-ref-exists"
816			}
817		}
818		return true, "empty-target-managed-refs"
819	}
820	
821	func canIncrementalRelay(cfg Config, plans []BranchPlan, targetAdv *packp.AdvRefs) (bool, string) {
822		if cfg.Force || cfg.Prune || cfg.DryRun {
823			return false, "incremental-disabled-by-force-prune-or-dry-run"
824		}
825		if len(plans) == 0 {
826			return false, "incremental-no-plans"
827		}
828		if targetAdv == nil || targetAdv.Capabilities == nil {
829			return false, "incremental-missing-target-capabilities"
830		}
831		if targetAdv.Capabilities.Supports(capability.Capability("no-thin")) {
832			return false, "incremental-target-no-thin"
833		}
834	
835		for _, plan := range plans {
836			switch plan.Kind {
837			case RefKindBranch:
838				if !plan.SourceRef.IsBranch() || !plan.TargetRef.IsBranch() {
839					return false, "incremental-non-branch-mapping"
840				}
841				if plan.Action != ActionUpdate {
842					return false, "incremental-branch-action-not-update"
843				}
844				if plan.TargetHash.IsZero() {
845					return false, "incremental-branch-target-missing"
846				}
847			case RefKindTag:
848				if !plan.SourceRef.IsTag() || !plan.TargetRef.IsTag() {
849					return false, "incremental-non-tag-mapping"
850				}
851				if plan.Action != ActionCreate {
852					return false, "incremental-tag-action-not-create"
853				}
854			default:
855				return false, "incremental-unsupported-ref-kind"
856			}
857		}
858		return true, "fast-forward-branch-or-tag-create"
859	}
860	
861	func relayFallbackReason(cfg Config, plans []BranchPlan, targetAdv *packp.AdvRefs) string {
862		if ok, reason := canIncrementalRelay(cfg, plans, targetAdv); ok {
863			return reason
864		} else if ok, reason := canFullTagCreateRelay(plans); ok {
865			return reason
866		} else {
867			return reason
868		}
869	}
870	
871	func canFullTagCreateRelay(plans []BranchPlan) (bool, string) {
872		if len(plans) == 0 {
873			return false, "incremental-no-plans"
874		}
875		for _, plan := range plans {
876			if plan.Kind != RefKindTag {
877				return false, "incremental-tag-relay-non-tag-plan"
878			}
879			if !plan.SourceRef.IsTag() || !plan.TargetRef.IsTag() {
880				return false, "incremental-tag-relay-non-tag-mapping"
881			}
882			if plan.Action != ActionCreate {
883				return false, "incremental-tag-relay-tag-action-not-create"
884			}
885		}
886		return true, "tag-create-full-pack"
887	}

902	func ensureLocalObjectsForPush(
903		ctx context.Context,
904		repo *git.Repository,
905		sourceConn *transportConn,
906		sourceService *sourceRefService,
907		desired map[plumbing.ReferenceName]desiredRef,
908		plans []BranchPlan,
909	) error {
910		tagDesired := make(map[plumbing.ReferenceName]desiredRef)
911		for _, plan := range plans {
912			if plan.Kind != RefKindTag {
913				continue
914			}
915			if desiredRef, ok := desired[plan.TargetRef]; ok {
916				tagDesired[plan.TargetRef] = desiredRef
917			}
918		}
919		if len(tagDesired) == 0 {
920			return nil
921		}
922		return sourceService.Fetch(ctx, repo, sourceConn, tagDesired, nil)
923	}
924	
925	func bootstrapWithInputs(
926		ctx context.Context,
927		cfg Config,
928		stats *statsCollector,
929		sourceConn *transportConn,
930		targetConn *transportConn,
931		sourceService *sourceRefService,
932		targetAdv *packp.AdvRefs,
933		desiredRefs map[plumbing.ReferenceName]desiredRef,
934		targetRefs map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash,
935		relayReason string,
936	) (Result, error) {
937		plans, err := buildBootstrapPlans(desiredRefs, targetRefs)
938		if err != nil {
939			return Result{}, err
940		}
941	
942		result := Result{
943			Plans:       plans,
944			Relay:       true,
945			RelayMode:   "bootstrap",
946			RelayReason: relayReason,
947			Stats:       stats.snapshot(),
948			Protocol:    sourceService.protocol,
949		}
950	
951		if batchLimit, ok := githubBootstrapBatchMaxPackBytes(ctx, cfg, sourceConn, sourceService); ok {
952			cfg.BatchMaxPackBytes = batchLimit
953			progressf(
954				cfg.Verbose,
955				"bootstrap: github repo-size preflight selected batched mode with batch-max-pack-bytes=%d",
956				cfg.BatchMaxPackBytes,
957			)
958		}
959	
960		if cfg.BatchMaxPackBytes > 0 {
961			return bootstrapBatchedWithInputs(ctx, cfg, stats, sourceConn, targetConn, sourceService, targetAdv, plans, desiredRefs, targetRefs, result)
962		}
963	
964		progressf(cfg.Verbose, "bootstrap: fetching %d ref(s) from source", len(plans))
965	
966		packReader, err := sourceService.FetchPack(ctx, sourceConn, desiredRefs, nil)
967		if err != nil {
968			if errors.Is(err, git.NoErrAlreadyUpToDate) {
969				return result, nil
970			}
971			return result, fmt.Errorf("fetch source pack: %w", err)
972		}
973		packReader = limitPackReadCloser(packReader, cfg.MaxPackBytes)
974	
975		progressf(cfg.Verbose, "bootstrap: pushing %d ref(s) to target", len(plans))
976		pushErr := pushPackToTarget(ctx, targetConn, targetAdv, plans, packReader, cfg.Verbose)
977		if closeErr := packReader.Close(); closeErr != nil && pushErr == nil {
978			pushErr = closeErr
979		}
980		if pushErr != nil {
981			autoBatchSize, ok := autoBatchMaxPackBytes(cfg, sourceService, pushErr)
982			if !ok {
983				return result, fmt.Errorf("push target refs: %w", pushErr)
984			}
985			progressf(
986				cfg.Verbose,
987				"bootstrap: target rejected single-pack push; retrying with batch-max-pack-bytes=%d",
988				autoBatchSize,
989			)
990			cfg.BatchMaxPackBytes = autoBatchSize
991			return bootstrapBatchedWithInputs(ctx, cfg, stats, sourceConn, targetConn, sourceService, targetAdv, plans, desiredRefs, targetRefs, result)
992		}
993	
994		result.Pushed = len(plans)
995		result.Stats = stats.snapshot()
996		return result, nil
997	}
998	
999	func autoBatchMaxPackBytes(cfg Config, sourceService *sourceRefService, err error) (int64, bool) {
1000		if cfg.BatchMaxPackBytes > 0 || !isTargetBodyLimitError(err) {
1001			return 0, false
1002		}
1003		if sourceService == nil || sourceService.protocol != protocolModeV2 || !fetchCapabilitySupports(sourceService.v2, "filter") {
1004			return 0, false
1005		}
1006	
1007		batchLimit := int64(defaultAutoBatchMaxPackBytes)
1008		if targetLimit := targetBodyLimit(err); targetLimit > 0 {
1009			derivedLimit := targetLimit / 2
1010			if derivedLimit <= 0 {
1011				derivedLimit = targetLimit
1012			}
1013			if derivedLimit < batchLimit {
1014				batchLimit = derivedLimit
1015			}
1016		}
1017		if cfg.MaxPackBytes > 0 && cfg.MaxPackBytes < batchLimit {
1018			batchLimit = cfg.MaxPackBytes
1019		}
1020		if batchLimit <= 0 {
1021			return 0, false
1022		}
1023		return batchLimit, true
1024	}
1025	
1026	func githubBootstrapBatchMaxPackBytes(ctx context.Context, cfg Config, sourceConn *transportConn, sourceService *sourceRefService) (int64, bool) {
1027		if cfg.BatchMaxPackBytes > 0 {
1028			return 0, false
1029		}
1030		if sourceConn == nil || sourceConn.endpoint == nil {
1031			return 0, false
1032		}
1033		if sourceService == nil || sourceService.protocol != protocolModeV2 || !fetchCapabilitySupports(sourceService.v2, "filter") {
1034			return 0, false
1035		}
1036		repoSizeKB, ok := lookupGitHubRepoSizeKB(ctx, sourceConn)
1037		if !ok || repoSizeKB < githubLargeRepoThresholdKB {
1038			return 0, false
1039		}
1040	
1041		batchLimit := int64(defaultAutoBatchMaxPackBytes)
1042		if cfg.MaxPackBytes > 0 && cfg.MaxPackBytes < batchLimit {
1043			batchLimit = cfg.MaxPackBytes
1044		}
1045		if batchLimit <= 0 {
1046			return 0, false
1047		}
1048		return batchLimit, true
1049	}
1050	
1051	func lookupGitHubRepoSizeKB(ctx context.Context, sourceConn *transportConn) (int64, bool) {
1052		owner, repo, ok := githubOwnerRepo(sourceConn)
1053		if !ok {
1054			return 0, false
1055		}
1056	
1057		apiURL := strings.TrimRight(githubRepoAPIBaseURL, "/") + "/repos/" + owner + "/" + repo
1058		req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodGet, apiURL, nil)
1059		if err != nil {
1060			return 0, false
1061		}
1062		req.Header.Set("Accept", "application/vnd.github+json")
1063		req.Header.Set("X-GitHub-Api-Version", "2022-11-28")
1064		req.Header.Set("User-Agent", capability.DefaultAgent())
1065		req.Header.Set(statsPhaseHdr, "github repo metadata")
1066	
1067		resp, err := sourceConn.http.Do(req)
1068		if err != nil {
1069			return 0, false
1070		}
1071		defer resp.Body.Close()
1072		if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
1073			return 0, false
1074		}
1075	
1076		var payload struct {
1077			Size int64 `json:"size"`
1078		}
1079		if err := json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(&payload); err != nil {
1080			return 0, false
1081		}
1082		if payload.Size <= 0 {
1083			return 0, false
1084		}
1085		return payload.Size, true
1086	}
1087	
1088	func githubOwnerRepo(sourceConn *transportConn) (string, string, bool) {
1089		if sourceConn == nil || sourceConn.endpoint == nil {
1090			return "", "", false
1091		}
1092		ep := sourceConn.endpoint
1093		if ep.Protocol != "http" && ep.Protocol != "https" {
1094			return "", "", false
1095		}
1096		if !strings.EqualFold(ep.Host, "github.com") {
1097			return "", "", false
1098		}
1099	
1100		path := strings.Trim(ep.Path, "/")
1101		if strings.HasSuffix(path, ".git") {
1102			path = strings.TrimSuffix(path, ".git")
1103		}
1104		parts := strings.Split(path, "/")
1105		if len(parts) != 2 || parts[0] == "" || parts[1] == "" {
1106			return "", "", false
1107		}
1108		return parts[0], parts[1], true
1109	}
1110	
1111	func isTargetBodyLimitError(err error) bool {
1112		if err == nil {
1113			return false
1114		}
1115		message := strings.ToLower(err.Error())
1116		return strings.Contains(message, "body exceeded size limit") ||
1117			(strings.Contains(message, "request body") && strings.Contains(message, "too large")) ||
1118			(strings.Contains(message, "payload") && strings.Contains(message, "too large")) ||
1119			strings.Contains(message, "http 413")
1120	}
1121	
1122	func targetBodyLimit(err error) int64 {
1123		if err == nil {
1124			return 0
1125		}
1126		matches := bodyLimitPattern.FindStringSubmatch(strings.ToLower(err.Error()))
1127		if len(matches) != 2 {
1128			return 0
1129		}
1130		limit, parseErr := strconv.ParseInt(matches[1], 10, 64)
1131		if parseErr != nil {
1132			return 0
1133		}
1134		return limit
1135	}

1137	type bootstrapBatch struct {
1138		Plan        BranchPlan
1139		TempRef     plumbing.ReferenceName
1140		ResumeHash  plumbing.Hash
1141		Checkpoints []plumbing.Hash
1142	}
1143	
1144	func bootstrapBatchedWithInputs(
1145		ctx context.Context,
1146		cfg Config,
1147		stats *statsCollector,
1148		sourceConn *transportConn,
1149		targetConn *transportConn,
1150		sourceService *sourceRefService,
1151		targetAdv *packp.AdvRefs,
1152		plans []BranchPlan,
1153		desiredRefs map[plumbing.ReferenceName]desiredRef,
1154		targetRefs map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash,
1155		result Result,
1156	) (Result, error) {
1157		if sourceService.protocol != protocolModeV2 {
1158			return result, fmt.Errorf("bootstrap batching currently requires protocol v2")
1159		}
1160		if !fetchCapabilitySupports(sourceService.v2, "filter") {
1161			return result, fmt.Errorf("bootstrap batching requires source fetch filter support")
1162		}
1163	
1164		planRefs := make([]desiredRef, 0, len(plans))
1165		tagPlans := make([]BranchPlan, 0, len(plans))
1166		tagDesired := make(map[plumbing.ReferenceName]desiredRef)
1167		for _, plan := range plans {
1168			if plan.Kind == RefKindTag {
1169				tagPlans = append(tagPlans, plan)
1170				if desired, ok := desiredRefs[plan.TargetRef]; ok {
1171					tagDesired[plan.TargetRef] = desired
1172				}
1173				continue
1174			}
1175			if !plan.SourceRef.IsBranch() || !plan.TargetRef.IsBranch() {
1176				return result, fmt.Errorf("bootstrap batching currently supports branch refs and create-only tags")
1177			}
1178			planRefs = append(planRefs, desiredRef{
1179				Kind:       plan.Kind,
1180				Label:      plan.Branch,
1181				SourceRef:  plan.SourceRef,
1182				TargetRef:  plan.TargetRef,
1183				SourceHash: plan.SourceHash,
1184			})
1185		}
1186	
1187		var (
1188			batches []bootstrapBatch
1189			err     error
1190		)
1191		if len(planRefs) > 0 {
1192			progressf(cfg.Verbose, "bootstrap-batch: planning checkpoints for %d branch ref(s)", len(planRefs))
1193			batches, err = planBootstrapBatches(ctx, cfg, sourceConn, sourceService, planRefs, targetRefs)
1194			if err != nil {
1195				return result, err
1196			}
1197		}
1198	
1199		batchLimit := cfg.BatchMaxPackBytes
1200		if cfg.MaxPackBytes > 0 && (batchLimit == 0 || cfg.MaxPackBytes < batchLimit) {
1201			batchLimit = cfg.MaxPackBytes
1202		}
1203	
1204		for _, batch := range batches {
1205			result.PlannedBatchCount += len(batch.Checkpoints)
1206			result.TempRefs = append(result.TempRefs, batch.TempRef.String())
1207			progressf(
1208				cfg.Verbose,
1209				"bootstrap-batch: branch=%s temp-ref=%s planned-batches=%d resume=%s",
1210				batch.Plan.TargetRef,
1211				batch.TempRef,
1212				len(batch.Checkpoints),
1213				shortHash(batch.ResumeHash),
1214			)
1215			current := batch.ResumeHash
1216			startIdx, err := bootstrapResumeIndex(batch.Checkpoints, batch.ResumeHash)
1217			if err != nil {
1218				return result, fmt.Errorf("resume bootstrap batch for %s: %w", batch.Plan.TargetRef, err)
1219			}
1220			for idx := startIdx; idx < len(batch.Checkpoints); idx++ {
1221				checkpoint := batch.Checkpoints[idx]
1222				progressf(
1223					cfg.Verbose,
1224					"bootstrap-batch: branch=%s batch=%d/%d from=%s to=%s",
1225					batch.Plan.TargetRef,
1226					idx+1,
1227					len(batch.Checkpoints),
1228					shortHash(current),
1229					shortHash(checkpoint),
1230				)
1231				stagePlans := []BranchPlan{
1232					{
1233						Branch:     batch.Plan.Branch,
1234						SourceRef:  batch.Plan.SourceRef,
1235						TargetRef:  batch.TempRef,
1236						SourceHash: checkpoint,
1237						TargetHash: current,
1238						Kind:       batch.Plan.Kind,
1239						Action:     actionForTargetHash(current),
1240						Reason:     fmt.Sprintf("%s -> %s via %s", shortHash(current), shortHash(checkpoint), batch.TempRef),
1241					},
1242				}
1243				if idx == len(batch.Checkpoints)-1 {
1244					stagePlans = append(stagePlans, BranchPlan{
1245						Branch:     batch.Plan.Branch,
1246						SourceRef:  batch.Plan.SourceRef,
1247						TargetRef:  batch.Plan.TargetRef,
1248						SourceHash: checkpoint,
1249						TargetHash: plumbing.ZeroHash,
1250						Kind:       batch.Plan.Kind,
1251						Action:     ActionCreate,
1252						Reason:     fmt.Sprintf("create %s at %s", batch.Plan.TargetRef, shortHash(checkpoint)),
1253					})
1254				}
1255	
1256				packReader, err := sourceService.FetchPack(ctx, sourceConn, singleDesiredRef(batch.Plan.SourceRef, batch.TempRef, checkpoint), singleHaveMap(current))
1257				if err != nil {
1258					return result, fmt.Errorf("fetch source batch pack for %s: %w", batch.Plan.TargetRef, err)
1259				}
1260				packReader = limitPackReadCloser(packReader, batchLimit)
1261				if err := pushPackToTarget(ctx, targetConn, targetAdv, stagePlans, packReader, cfg.Verbose); err != nil {
1262					return result, fmt.Errorf("push bootstrap batch for %s: %w", batch.Plan.TargetRef, err)
1263				}
1264				progressf(
1265					cfg.Verbose,
1266					"bootstrap-batch: branch=%s batch=%d/%d complete",
1267					batch.Plan.TargetRef,
1268					idx+1,
1269					len(batch.Checkpoints),
1270				)
1271				current = checkpoint
1272				result.BatchCount++
1273			}
1274	
1275			if current.IsZero() {
1276				return result, fmt.Errorf("bootstrap batching for %s completed with no checkpoint state", batch.Plan.TargetRef)
1277			}
1278			if batch.ResumeHash == batch.Plan.SourceHash {
1279				if err := pushCommandsToTarget(ctx, targetConn, targetAdv, []BranchPlan{{
1280					Branch:     batch.Plan.Branch,
1281					SourceRef:  batch.Plan.SourceRef,
1282					TargetRef:  batch.Plan.TargetRef,
1283					SourceHash: batch.Plan.SourceHash,
1284					TargetHash: plumbing.ZeroHash,
1285					Kind:       batch.Plan.Kind,
1286					Action:     ActionCreate,
1287					Reason:     fmt.Sprintf("create %s at %s", batch.Plan.TargetRef, shortHash(batch.Plan.SourceHash)),
1288				}}, cfg.Verbose); err != nil {
1289					return result, fmt.Errorf("resume bootstrap cutover for %s: %w", batch.Plan.TargetRef, err)
1290				}
1291			}
1292	
1293			deleteTempPlan := BranchPlan{
1294				Branch:     batch.Plan.Branch,
1295				TargetRef:  batch.TempRef,
1296				SourceHash: plumbing.ZeroHash,
1297				TargetHash: current,
1298				Kind:       batch.Plan.Kind,
1299				Action:     ActionDelete,
1300				Reason:     fmt.Sprintf("delete temp ref %s", batch.TempRef),
1301			}
1302			if err := pushCommandsToTarget(ctx, targetConn, targetAdv, []BranchPlan{deleteTempPlan}, cfg.Verbose); err != nil {
1303				return result, fmt.Errorf("delete bootstrap temp ref for %s: %w", batch.Plan.TargetRef, err)
1304			}
1305			progressf(cfg.Verbose, "bootstrap-batch: branch=%s finalized", batch.Plan.TargetRef)
1306		}
1307	
1308		if len(tagPlans) > 0 {
1309			progressf(cfg.Verbose, "bootstrap-batch: pushing %d tag(s) after branch batches", len(tagPlans))
1310			tagTargetRefs := copyRefHashMap(targetRefs)
1311			for _, batch := range batches {
1312				tagTargetRefs[batch.Plan.TargetRef] = batch.Plan.SourceHash
1313			}
1314			packReader, err := sourceService.FetchPack(ctx, sourceConn, tagDesired, tagTargetRefs)
1315			if err != nil {
1316				if !errors.Is(err, git.NoErrAlreadyUpToDate) {
1317					return result, fmt.Errorf("fetch bootstrap tag pack: %w", err)
1318				}
1319			} else {
1320				defer packReader.Close()
1321				packReader = limitPackReadCloser(packReader, cfg.MaxPackBytes)
1322				if err := pushPackToTarget(ctx, targetConn, targetAdv, tagPlans, packReader, cfg.Verbose); err != nil {
1323					return result, fmt.Errorf("push bootstrap tags: %w", err)
1324				}
1325			}
1326		}
1327	
1328		result.Pushed = len(plans)
1329		result.Batching = true
1330		result.RelayMode = "bootstrap-batch"
1331		result.Stats = stats.snapshot()
1332		return result, nil
1333	}
1334	
1335	func actionForTargetHash(hash plumbing.Hash) Action {
1336		if hash.IsZero() {
1337			return ActionCreate
1338		}
1339		return ActionUpdate
1340	}
1341	
1342	func singleDesiredRef(sourceRef, targetRef plumbing.ReferenceName, hash plumbing.Hash) map[plumbing.ReferenceName]desiredRef {
1343		return map[plumbing.ReferenceName]desiredRef{
1344			targetRef: {
1345				Kind:       RefKindBranch,
1346				Label:      targetRef.Short(),
1347				SourceRef:  sourceRef,
1348				TargetRef:  targetRef,
1349				SourceHash: hash,
1350			},
1351		}
1352	}
1353	
1354	func singleHaveMap(hash plumbing.Hash) map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash {
1355		if hash.IsZero() {
1356			return nil
1357		}
1358		return map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash{
1359			plumbing.ReferenceName("refs/gitsync/have"): hash,
1360		}
1361	}
1362	
1363	func bootstrapTempRef(targetRef plumbing.ReferenceName) plumbing.ReferenceName {
1364		return plumbing.ReferenceName("refs/gitsync/bootstrap/heads/" + targetRef.Short())
1365	}
1366	
1367	func planBootstrapBatches(
1368		ctx context.Context,
1369		cfg Config,
1370		sourceConn *transportConn,
1371		sourceService *sourceRefService,
1372		desired []desiredRef,
1373		targetRefs map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash,
1374	) ([]bootstrapBatch, error) {
1375		out := make([]bootstrapBatch, 0, len(desired))
1376		for _, ref := range desired {
1377			checkpoints, err := planBootstrapBranchCheckpoints(ctx, cfg, sourceConn, sourceService, ref)
1378			if err != nil {
1379				return nil, err
1380			}
1381			out = append(out, bootstrapBatch{
1382				Plan: BranchPlan{
1383					Branch:     ref.Label,
1384					SourceRef:  ref.SourceRef,
1385					TargetRef:  ref.TargetRef,
1386					SourceHash: ref.SourceHash,
1387					Kind:       ref.Kind,
1388					Action:     ActionCreate,
1389				},
1390				TempRef:     bootstrapTempRef(ref.TargetRef),
1391				ResumeHash:  targetRefs[bootstrapTempRef(ref.TargetRef)],
1392				Checkpoints: checkpoints,
1393			})
1394		}
1395		return out, nil
1396	}
1397	
1398	func planBootstrapBranchCheckpoints(
1399		ctx context.Context,
1400		cfg Config,
1401		sourceConn *transportConn,
1402		sourceService *sourceRefService,
1403		ref desiredRef,
1404	) ([]plumbing.Hash, error) {
1405		progressf(cfg.Verbose, "bootstrap-batch: fetching commit graph for %s", ref.TargetRef)
1406		graphRepo, err := git.Init(memory.NewStorage(), nil)
1407		if err != nil {
1408			return nil, fmt.Errorf("init bootstrap planning repository: %w", err)
1409		}
1410		if err := fetchSourceCommitGraphV2(ctx, graphRepo, sourceConn, sourceService.v2, ref); err != nil {
1411			return nil, fmt.Errorf("fetch bootstrap planning graph for %s: %w", ref.TargetRef, err)
1412		}
1413	
1414		chain, err := firstParentChain(graphRepo, ref.SourceHash)
1415		if err != nil {
1416			return nil, fmt.Errorf("walk first-parent chain for %s: %w", ref.TargetRef, err)
1417		}
1418		if len(chain) == 0 {
1419			return nil, fmt.Errorf("empty first-parent chain for %s", ref.TargetRef)
1420		}
1421	
1422		checkpoints := make([]plumbing.Hash, 0, len(chain))
1423		prevIdx := -1
1424		prevHash := plumbing.ZeroHash
1425		prevSpan := 0
1426		for prevIdx < len(chain)-1 {
1427			bestIdx, err := largestCheckpointUnderLimit(ctx, cfg, sourceConn, sourceService, ref, chain, prevIdx, prevHash, prevSpan)
1428			if err != nil {
1429				return nil, err
1430			}
1431			if bestIdx <= prevIdx {
1432				return nil, fmt.Errorf("could not find bootstrap checkpoint for %s under batch-max-pack-bytes=%d", ref.TargetRef, cfg.BatchMaxPackBytes)
1433			}
1434			prevSpan = bestIdx - prevIdx
1435			prevIdx = bestIdx
1436			prevHash = chain[bestIdx]
1437			checkpoints = append(checkpoints, prevHash)
1438			progressf(
1439				cfg.Verbose,
1440				"bootstrap-batch: branch=%s planned-checkpoint=%s selected=%d chain-len=%d",
1441				ref.TargetRef,
1442				shortHash(prevHash),
1443				len(checkpoints),
1444				len(chain),
1445			)
1446		}
1447		return checkpoints, nil
1448	}
1449	
1450	func largestCheckpointUnderLimit(
1451		ctx context.Context,
1452		cfg Config,
1453		sourceConn *transportConn,
1454		sourceService *sourceRefService,
1455		ref desiredRef,
1456		chain []plumbing.Hash,
1457		prevIdx int,
1458		prevHash plumbing.Hash,
1459		prevSpan int,
1460	) (int, error) {
1461		return sampledCheckpointUnderLimitByProbe(chain, prevIdx, prevSpan, func(idx int) (bool, error) {
1462			tooLarge, err := sourcePackExceedsLimit(ctx, sourceConn, sourceService, ref, chain[idx], prevHash, cfg.BatchMaxPackBytes)
1463			if err != nil {
1464				return false, fmt.Errorf("measure bootstrap batch for %s at %s: %w", ref.TargetRef, shortHash(chain[idx]), err)
1465			}
1466			if tooLarge {
1467				progressf(cfg.Verbose, "bootstrap-batch: sample %s exceeds limit=%d", shortHash(chain[idx]), cfg.BatchMaxPackBytes)
1468			} else {
1469				progressf(cfg.Verbose, "bootstrap-batch: sample %s fits limit=%d", shortHash(chain[idx]), cfg.BatchMaxPackBytes)
1470			}
1471			return tooLarge, nil
1472		})
1473	}
1474	
1475	func sampledCheckpointUnderLimitByProbe(
1476		chain []plumbing.Hash,
1477		prevIdx int,
1478		prevSpan int,
1479		probe func(idx int) (bool, error),
1480	) (int, error) {
1481		lo := prevIdx + 1
1482		hi := len(chain) - 1
1483		if lo > hi {
1484			return -1, nil
1485		}
1486	
1487		samples := sampledCheckpointCandidates(lo, hi, prevSpan)
1488		best := -1
1489		for _, idx := range samples {
1490			tooLarge, err := probe(idx)
1491			if err != nil {
1492				return -1, err
1493			}
1494			if tooLarge {
1495				continue
1496			}
1497			best = idx
1498			break
1499		}
1500		if best != -1 {
1501			return best, nil
1502		}
1503	
1504		if prevSpan > 1 {
1505			shrunk := prevSpan / 2
1506			if shrunk < 1 {
1507				shrunk = 1
1508			}
1509			idx := lo + shrunk - 1
1510			if idx > hi {
1511				idx = hi
1512			}
1513			if idx >= lo {
1514				tooLarge, err := probe(idx)
1515				if err != nil {
1516					return -1, err
1517				}
1518				if !tooLarge {
1519					return idx, nil
1520				}
1521			}
1522		}
1523	
1524		tooLarge, err := probe(lo)
1525		if err != nil {
1526			return -1, err
1527		}
1528		if tooLarge {
1529			return -1, nil
1530		}
1531		return lo, nil
1532	}
1533	
1534	func sampledCheckpointCandidates(lo, hi int, prevSpan int) []int {
1535		if lo > hi {
1536			return nil
1537		}
1538	
1539		set := map[int]struct{}{}
1540		add := func(idx int) {
1541			if idx < lo {
1542				idx = lo
1543			}
1544			if idx > hi {
1545				idx = hi
1546			}
1547			set[idx] = struct{}{}
1548		}
1549	
1550		projected := hi
1551		if prevSpan > 0 {
1552			projected = lo + prevSpan - 1
1553		}
1554		add(projected)
1555	
1556		const sampleCount = 4
1557		current := projected
1558		for i := 0; i < sampleCount-1; i++ {
1559			if current <= lo {
1560				add(lo)
1561				continue
1562			}
1563			distance := current - lo
1564			current = lo + distance/2
1565			add(current)
1566		}
1567		add(lo)
1568	
1569		candidates := make([]int, 0, len(set))
1570		for idx := range set {
1571			candidates = append(candidates, idx)
1572		}
1573		sort.Sort(sort.Reverse(sort.IntSlice(candidates)))
1574		return candidates
1575	}
1576	
1577	func firstParentChain(repo *git.Repository, tip plumbing.Hash) ([]plumbing.Hash, error) {
1578		commit, err := repo.CommitObject(tip)
1579		if err != nil {
1580			return nil, err
1581		}
1582		reversed := make([]plumbing.Hash, 0, 128)
1583		for {
1584			reversed = append(reversed, commit.Hash)
1585			if len(commit.ParentHashes) == 0 {
1586				break
1587			}
1588			commit, err = repo.CommitObject(commit.ParentHashes[0])
1589			if err != nil {
1590				return nil, err
1591			}
1592		}
1593	
1594		chain := make([]plumbing.Hash, 0, len(reversed))
1595		for i := len(reversed) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
1596			chain = append(chain, reversed[i])
1597		}
1598		return chain, nil
1599	}

1601	func sourcePackExceedsLimit(
1602		ctx context.Context,
1603		sourceConn *transportConn,
1604		sourceService *sourceRefService,
1605		ref desiredRef,
1606		want plumbing.Hash,
1607		have plumbing.Hash,
1608		limit int64,
1609	) (bool, error) {
1610		packReader, err := sourceService.FetchPack(ctx, sourceConn, singleDesiredRef(ref.SourceRef, ref.TargetRef, want), singleHaveMap(have))
1611		if err != nil {
1612			return false, err
1613		}
1614		defer packReader.Close()
1615		_, err = io.Copy(io.Discard, limitPackReadCloser(packReader, limit))
1616		if err == nil {
1617			return false, nil
1618		}
1619		if strings.Contains(err.Error(), "source pack exceeded max-pack-bytes limit") {
1620			return true, nil
1621		}
1622		return false, err
1623	}

1625	func bootstrapResumeIndex(checkpoints []plumbing.Hash, resumeHash plumbing.Hash) (int, error) {
1626		if resumeHash.IsZero() {
1627			return 0, nil
1628		}
1629		for idx, checkpoint := range checkpoints {
1630			if checkpoint == resumeHash {
1631				return idx + 1, nil
1632			}
1633		}
1634		return 0, fmt.Errorf("temp ref hash %s does not match any planned checkpoint", resumeHash)
1635	}
1636	
1637	func selectBranches(source map[string]plumbing.Hash, requested []string) map[string]plumbing.Hash {
1638		if len(requested) == 0 {
1639			return source
1640		}
1641	
1642		selected := make(map[string]plumbing.Hash, len(requested))
1643		for _, branch := range requested {
1644			if hash, ok := source[branch]; ok {
1645				selected[branch] = hash
1646			}
1647		}
1648		return selected
1649	}
1650	
1651	func buildDesiredRefs(sourceRefs map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash, cfg Config) (map[plumbing.ReferenceName]desiredRef, map[plumbing.ReferenceName]managedTarget, error) {
1652		desired := make(map[plumbing.ReferenceName]desiredRef)
1653		managed := make(map[plumbing.ReferenceName]managedTarget)
1654	
1655		addManaged := func(sourceRef, targetRef plumbing.ReferenceName, kind RefKind, hash plumbing.Hash) error {
1656			if hash.IsZero() {
1657				return fmt.Errorf("source ref %s not found", sourceRef)
1658			}
1659			short := targetRef.Short()
1660			desired[targetRef] = desiredRef{
1661				Kind:       kind,
1662				Label:      short,
1663				SourceRef:  sourceRef,
1664				TargetRef:  targetRef,
1665				SourceHash: hash,
1666			}
1667			managed[targetRef] = managedTarget{Kind: kind, Label: short}
1668			return nil
1669		}
1670	
1671		if len(cfg.Mappings) > 0 {
1672			for _, mapping := range cfg.Mappings {
1673				sourceRef, targetRef, kind, err := normalizeMapping(mapping)
1674				if err != nil {
1675					return nil, nil, err
1676				}
1677				if err := addManaged(sourceRef, targetRef, kind, sourceRefs[sourceRef]); err != nil {
1678					return nil, nil, err
1679				}
1680			}
1681		} else {
1682			branches := branchMapFromRefHashMap(sourceRefs)
1683			selected := selectBranches(branches, cfg.Branches)
1684			for branch, hash := range selected {
1685				refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(branch)
1686				if err := addManaged(refName, refName, RefKindBranch, hash); err != nil {
1687					return nil, nil, err
1688				}
1689			}
1690		}
1691	
1692		if cfg.IncludeTags {
1693			for refName, hash := range sourceRefs {
1694				if !refName.IsTag() {
1695					continue
1696				}
1697				if err := addManaged(refName, refName, RefKindTag, hash); err != nil {
1698					return nil, nil, err
1699				}
1700			}
1701		}
1702	
1703		return desired, managed, nil
1704	}
1705	
1706	func normalizeMapping(mapping RefMapping) (plumbing.ReferenceName, plumbing.ReferenceName, RefKind, error) {
1707		src := strings.TrimSpace(mapping.Source)
1708		dst := strings.TrimSpace(mapping.Target)
1709		if src == "" || dst == "" {
1710			return "", "", "", fmt.Errorf("invalid mapping %q:%q", mapping.Source, mapping.Target)
1711		}
1712	
1713		if strings.HasPrefix(src, "refs/") || strings.HasPrefix(dst, "refs/") {
1714			sourceRef := plumbing.ReferenceName(src)
1715			targetRef := plumbing.ReferenceName(dst)
1716			kind := refKindFromName(targetRef)
1717			if kind == "" {
1718				return "", "", "", fmt.Errorf("unsupported mapped ref kind: %s -> %s", src, dst)
1719			}
1720			return sourceRef, targetRef, kind, nil
1721		}
1722	
1723		return plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(src), plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(dst), RefKindBranch, nil
1724	}
1725	
1726	func refKindFromName(name plumbing.ReferenceName) RefKind {
1727		switch {
1728		case name.IsBranch():
1729			return RefKindBranch
1730		case name.IsTag():
1731			return RefKindTag
1732		default:
1733			return ""
1734		}
1735	}
1736	
1737	type desiredRef struct {
1738		Kind       RefKind
1739		Label      string
1740		SourceRef  plumbing.ReferenceName
1741		TargetRef  plumbing.ReferenceName
1742		SourceHash plumbing.Hash
1743	}
1744	
1745	type managedTarget struct {
1746		Kind  RefKind
1747		Label string
1748	}
1749	
1750	func buildPlans(
1751		repo *git.Repository,
1752		desired map[plumbing.ReferenceName]desiredRef,
1753		targetRefs map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash,
1754		managed map[plumbing.ReferenceName]managedTarget,
1755		cfg Config,
1756	) ([]BranchPlan, error) {
1757		if cfg.Prune {
1758			for targetRef := range targetRefs {
1759				if _, ok := managed[targetRef]; ok {
1760					continue
1761				}
1762				switch {
1763				case targetRef.IsTag() && cfg.IncludeTags:
1764					managed[targetRef] = managedTarget{Kind: RefKindTag, Label: targetRef.Short()}
1765				case targetRef.IsBranch() && len(cfg.Mappings) == 0 && len(cfg.Branches) == 0:
1766					managed[targetRef] = managedTarget{Kind: RefKindBranch, Label: targetRef.Short()}
1767				}
1768			}
1769		}
1770	
1771		targetNames := make([]plumbing.ReferenceName, 0, len(managed))
1772		for name := range managed {
1773			targetNames = append(targetNames, name)
1774		}
1775		sort.Slice(targetNames, func(i, j int) bool { return targetNames[i] < targetNames[j] })
1776	
1777		plans := make([]BranchPlan, 0, len(targetNames)+8)
1778		for _, targetRef := range targetNames {
1779			info := managed[targetRef]
1780			want, existsInDesired := desired[targetRef]
1781			targetHash, existsOnTarget := targetRefs[targetRef]
1782	
1783			if !existsInDesired {
1784				if cfg.Prune && existsOnTarget {
1785					plans = append(plans, BranchPlan{
1786						Branch:     info.Label,
1787						TargetRef:  targetRef,
1788						TargetHash: targetHash,
1789						Kind:       info.Kind,
1790						Action:     ActionDelete,
1791						Reason:     fmt.Sprintf("%s -> <deleted>", shortHash(targetHash)),
1792					})
1793				}
1794				continue
1795			}
1796	
1797			if !existsOnTarget {
1798				plans = append(plans, BranchPlan{
1799					Branch:     want.Label,
1800					SourceRef:  want.SourceRef,
1801					TargetRef:  want.TargetRef,
1802					SourceHash: want.SourceHash,
1803					Kind:       want.Kind,
1804					Action:     ActionCreate,
1805					Reason:     fmt.Sprintf("%s -> <new>", shortHash(want.SourceHash)),
1806				})
1807				continue
1808			}
1809	
1810			plan, err := planRef(repo, want, targetHash, cfg.Force)
1811			if err != nil {
1812				return nil, err
1813			}
1814			plans = append(plans, plan)
1815		}
1816	
1817		sort.Slice(plans, func(i, j int) bool {
1818			return plans[i].TargetRef.String() < plans[j].TargetRef.String()
1819		})
1820		return plans, nil
1821	}
1822	
1823	func planRef(repo *git.Repository, want desiredRef, targetHash plumbing.Hash, force bool) (BranchPlan, error) {
1824		plan := BranchPlan{
1825			Branch:     want.Label,
1826			SourceRef:  want.SourceRef,
1827			TargetRef:  want.TargetRef,
1828			SourceHash: want.SourceHash,
1829			TargetHash: targetHash,
1830			Kind:       want.Kind,
1831		}
1832	
1833		if want.SourceHash == targetHash {
1834			plan.Action = ActionSkip
1835			plan.Reason = fmt.Sprintf("%s already current", shortHash(want.SourceHash))
1836			return plan, nil
1837		}
1838	
1839		if want.Kind == RefKindTag {
1840			if force {
1841				plan.Action = ActionUpdate
1842				plan.Reason = fmt.Sprintf("%s -> %s (force tag update)", shortHash(targetHash), shortHash(want.SourceHash))
1843				return plan, nil
1844			}
1845			plan.Action = ActionBlock
1846			plan.Reason = fmt.Sprintf("%s differs from %s; use --force to retarget tag", shortHash(targetHash), shortHash(want.SourceHash))
1847			return plan, nil
1848		}
1849	
1850		sourceCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(want.SourceHash)
1851		if err != nil {
1852			return plan, fmt.Errorf("load source commit for %s: %w", want.TargetRef, err)
1853		}
1854	
1855		isFF, err := reachesCommitHash(repo.Storer, sourceCommit, targetHash)
1856		if err != nil {
1857			return plan, fmt.Errorf("check fast-forward for %s: %w", want.TargetRef, err)
1858		}
1859		if isFF {
1860			plan.Action = ActionUpdate
1861			plan.Reason = fmt.Sprintf("%s -> %s", shortHash(targetHash), shortHash(want.SourceHash))
1862			return plan, nil
1863		}
1864	
1865		if force {
1866			plan.Action = ActionUpdate
1867			plan.Reason = fmt.Sprintf("%s -> %s (force)", shortHash(targetHash), shortHash(want.SourceHash))
1868			return plan, nil
1869		}
1870	
1871		plan.Action = ActionBlock
1872		plan.Reason = fmt.Sprintf("%s is not an ancestor of %s", shortHash(targetHash), shortHash(want.SourceHash))
1873		return plan, nil
1874	}
1875	
1876	func planBranch(repo *git.Repository, branch string, sourceHash, targetHash plumbing.Hash) (BranchPlan, error) {
1877		return planRef(repo, desiredRef{
1878			Kind:       RefKindBranch,
1879			Label:      branch,
1880			SourceRef:  plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(branch),
1881			TargetRef:  plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(branch),
1882			SourceHash: sourceHash,
1883		}, targetHash, false)
1884	}
1885	
1886	func fetchSourceRefsWithHavesV1(
1887		ctx context.Context,
1888		repo *git.Repository,
1889		conn *transportConn,
1890		sourceAdv *packp.AdvRefs,
1891		desired map[plumbing.ReferenceName]desiredRef,
1892		targetRefs map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash,
1893	) error {
1894		session, err := conn.transport.NewUploadPackSession(conn.endpoint, conn.authMethod())
1895		if err != nil {
1896			return fmt.Errorf("open source upload-pack session: %w", err)
1897		}
1898		defer session.Close()
1899	
1900		req := packp.NewUploadPackRequestFromCapabilities(sourceAdv.Capabilities)
1901		for _, ref := range desired {
1902			req.Wants = append(req.Wants, ref.SourceHash)
1903		}
1904		req.Wants = sortedUniqueHashes(req.Wants)
1905		req.Haves = sortedUniqueHashes(mapsRefValues(targetRefs))
1906		if len(req.Wants) == 0 {
1907			return git.NoErrAlreadyUpToDate
1908		}
1909		if sourceAdv.Capabilities.Supports(capability.NoProgress) {
1910			_ = req.Capabilities.Set(capability.NoProgress)
1911		}
1912		if desiredHasTag(desired) && sourceAdv.Capabilities.Supports(capability.IncludeTag) {
1913			_ = req.Capabilities.Set(capability.IncludeTag)
1914		}
1915		conn.stats.addWantsHaves("source upload-pack", len(req.Wants), len(req.Haves))
1916	
1917		reader, err := session.UploadPack(ctx, req)
1918		if err != nil {
1919			if errors.Is(err, transport.ErrEmptyUploadPackRequest) {
1920				return git.NoErrAlreadyUpToDate
1921			}
1922			return fmt.Errorf("source upload-pack: %w", err)
1923		}
1924		defer ioutil.CheckClose(reader, &err)
1925	
1926		if err := packfile.UpdateObjectStorage(repo.Storer, buildSidebandIfSupported(req.Capabilities, reader, nil)); err != nil {
1927			return fmt.Errorf("store source packfile: %w", err)
1928		}
1929	
1930		for _, ref := range desired {
1931			localRef := plumbing.ReferenceName(localBranchRef(sourceRemoteName, ref.TargetRef.Short()))
1932			if ref.Kind == RefKindTag {
1933				localRef = plumbing.ReferenceName("refs/remotes/" + sourceRemoteName + "/tags/" + ref.TargetRef.Short())
1934			}
1935			if err := repo.Storer.SetReference(plumbing.NewHashReference(localRef, ref.SourceHash)); err != nil {
1936				return fmt.Errorf("set local source ref %s: %w", ref.SourceRef, err)
1937			}
1938		}
1939		return nil
1940	}
1941	
1942	func fetchSourcePackV1(
1943		ctx context.Context,
1944		conn *transportConn,
1945		sourceAdv *packp.AdvRefs,
1946		desired map[plumbing.ReferenceName]desiredRef,
1947		targetRefs map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash,
1948	) (io.ReadCloser, error) {
1949		session, err := conn.transport.NewUploadPackSession(conn.endpoint, conn.authMethod())
1950		if err != nil {
1951			return nil, fmt.Errorf("open source upload-pack session: %w", err)
1952		}
1953	
1954		req := packp.NewUploadPackRequestFromCapabilities(sourceAdv.Capabilities)
1955		for _, ref := range desired {
1956			req.Wants = append(req.Wants, ref.SourceHash)
1957		}
1958		req.Wants = sortedUniqueHashes(req.Wants)
1959		req.Haves = sortedUniqueHashes(mapsRefValues(targetRefs))
1960		if len(req.Wants) == 0 {
1961			_ = session.Close()
1962			return nil, git.NoErrAlreadyUpToDate
1963		}
1964		if sourceAdv.Capabilities.Supports(capability.NoProgress) {
1965			_ = req.Capabilities.Set(capability.NoProgress)
1966		}
1967		if desiredHasTag(desired) && sourceAdv.Capabilities.Supports(capability.IncludeTag) {
1968			_ = req.Capabilities.Set(capability.IncludeTag)
1969		}
1970		conn.stats.addWantsHaves("source upload-pack", len(req.Wants), len(req.Haves))
1971	
1972		reader, err := session.UploadPack(ctx, req)
1973		if err != nil {
1974			_ = session.Close()
1975			if errors.Is(err, transport.ErrEmptyUploadPackRequest) {
1976				return nil, git.NoErrAlreadyUpToDate
1977			}
1978			return nil, fmt.Errorf("source upload-pack: %w", err)
1979		}
1980		return &sessionReadCloser{
1981			Reader: buildSidebandIfSupported(req.Capabilities, reader, nil),
1982			closeFn: func() error {
1983				_ = reader.Close()
1984				return session.Close()
1985			},
1986		}, nil
1987	}
1988	
1989	func desiredHasTag(desired map[plumbing.ReferenceName]desiredRef) bool {
1990		for _, ref := range desired {
1991			if ref.Kind == RefKindTag {
1992				return true
1993			}
1994		}
1995		return false
1996	}
1997	
1998	func pushToTarget(
1999		ctx context.Context,
2000		repo *git.Repository,
2001		conn *transportConn,
2002		targetAdv *packp.AdvRefs,
2003		plans []BranchPlan,
2004		targetRefs map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash,
2005		verbose bool,
2006	) error {
2007		session, err := conn.transport.NewReceivePackSession(conn.endpoint, conn.authMethod())
2008		if err != nil {
2009			return fmt.Errorf("open target receive-pack session: %w", err)
2010		}
2011		defer session.Close()
2012	
2013		req := packp.NewReferenceUpdateRequestFromCapabilities(targetAdv.Capabilities)
2014		req.Progress = progressWriter(verbose)
2015		if targetAdv.Capabilities.Supports(capability.Sideband64k) {
2016			_ = req.Capabilities.Set(capability.Sideband64k)
2017		} else if targetAdv.Capabilities.Supports(capability.Sideband) {
2018			_ = req.Capabilities.Set(capability.Sideband)
2019		}
2020	
2021		commands := make([]*packp.Command, 0, len(plans))
2022		objects := make([]plumbing.Hash, 0, len(plans))
2023		hasDelete := false
2024		hasUpdates := false
2025		for _, plan := range plans {
2026			cmd := &packp.Command{
2027				Name: plan.TargetRef,
2028				Old:  targetRefs[plan.TargetRef],
2029			}
2030			switch plan.Action {
2031			case ActionCreate, ActionUpdate:
2032				cmd.New = plan.SourceHash
2033				objects = append(objects, plan.SourceHash)
2034				hasUpdates = true
2035			case ActionDelete:
2036				cmd.New = plumbing.ZeroHash
2037				hasDelete = true
2038			}
2039			commands = append(commands, cmd)
2040		}
2041		req.Commands = commands
2042		conn.stats.addCommands("target receive-pack", len(commands))
2043		if hasDelete {
2044			if !targetAdv.Capabilities.Supports(capability.DeleteRefs) {
2045				return fmt.Errorf("target does not support delete-refs")
2046			}
2047			_ = req.Capabilities.Set(capability.DeleteRefs)
2048		}
2049	
2050		hashesToPush, err := objectsToPush(repo.Storer, objects, targetRefs)
2051		if err != nil {
2052			return fmt.Errorf("compute objects to push: %w", err)
2053		}
2054	
2055		report, err := receivePack(ctx, session, repo.Storer, req, hashesToPush, hasUpdates, !targetAdv.Capabilities.Supports(capability.OFSDelta))
2056		if err != nil {
2057			return err
2058		}
2059		if report != nil {
2060			if err := report.Error(); err != nil {
2061				return err
2062			}
2063		}
2064		return nil
2065	}
2066	
2067	func pushPackToTarget(
2068		ctx context.Context,
2069		conn *transportConn,
2070		targetAdv *packp.AdvRefs,
2071		plans []BranchPlan,
2072		pack io.ReadCloser,
2073		verbose bool,
2074	) error {
2075		session, err := conn.transport.NewReceivePackSession(conn.endpoint, conn.authMethod())
2076		if err != nil {
2077			return fmt.Errorf("open target receive-pack session: %w", err)
2078		}
2079		defer session.Close()
2080	
2081		req := packp.NewReferenceUpdateRequestFromCapabilities(targetAdv.Capabilities)
2082		req.Progress = progressWriter(verbose)
2083		if targetAdv.Capabilities.Supports(capability.Sideband64k) {
2084			_ = req.Capabilities.Set(capability.Sideband64k)
2085		} else if targetAdv.Capabilities.Supports(capability.Sideband) {
2086			_ = req.Capabilities.Set(capability.Sideband)
2087		}
2088	
2089		commands := make([]*packp.Command, 0, len(plans))
2090		for _, plan := range plans {
2091			cmd := &packp.Command{
2092				Name: plan.TargetRef,
2093				Old:  plan.TargetHash,
2094			}
2095			switch plan.Action {
2096			case ActionCreate, ActionUpdate:
2097				cmd.New = plan.SourceHash
2098			default:
2099				return fmt.Errorf("streamed pack push only supports create and update actions")
2100			}
2101			commands = append(commands, cmd)
2102		}
2103		req.Commands = commands
2104		conn.stats.addCommands("target receive-pack", len(commands))
2105	
2106		report, err := receivePackStream(ctx, session, req, pack)
2107		if err != nil {
2108			return err
2109		}
2110		if report != nil {
2111			if err := report.Error(); err != nil {
2112				return err
2113			}
2114		}
2115		return nil
2116	}
2117	
2118	func pushCommandsToTarget(
2119		ctx context.Context,
2120		conn *transportConn,
2121		targetAdv *packp.AdvRefs,
2122		plans []BranchPlan,
2123		verbose bool,
2124	) error {
2125		session, err := conn.transport.NewReceivePackSession(conn.endpoint, conn.authMethod())
2126		if err != nil {
2127			return fmt.Errorf("open target receive-pack session: %w", err)
2128		}
2129		defer session.Close()
2130	
2131		req := packp.NewReferenceUpdateRequestFromCapabilities(targetAdv.Capabilities)
2132		req.Progress = progressWriter(verbose)
2133		if targetAdv.Capabilities.Supports(capability.Sideband64k) {
2134			_ = req.Capabilities.Set(capability.Sideband64k)
2135		} else if targetAdv.Capabilities.Supports(capability.Sideband) {
2136			_ = req.Capabilities.Set(capability.Sideband)
2137		}
2138	
2139		commands := make([]*packp.Command, 0, len(plans))
2140		hasDelete := false
2141		for _, plan := range plans {
2142			cmd := &packp.Command{
2143				Name: plan.TargetRef,
2144				Old:  plan.TargetHash,
2145			}
2146			switch plan.Action {
2147			case ActionCreate, ActionUpdate:
2148				cmd.New = plan.SourceHash
2149			case ActionDelete:
2150				cmd.New = plumbing.ZeroHash
2151				hasDelete = true
2152			default:
2153				return fmt.Errorf("command-only target push does not support %s", plan.Action)
2154			}
2155			commands = append(commands, cmd)
2156		}
2157		req.Commands = commands
2158		conn.stats.addCommands("target receive-pack", len(commands))
2159		if hasDelete {
2160			if !targetAdv.Capabilities.Supports(capability.DeleteRefs) {
2161				return fmt.Errorf("target does not support delete-refs")
2162			}
2163			_ = req.Capabilities.Set(capability.DeleteRefs)
2164		}
2165	
2166		report, err := receivePack(ctx, session, nil, req, nil, false, false)
2167		if err != nil {
2168			return err
2169		}
2170		if report != nil {
2171			if err := report.Error(); err != nil {
2172				return err
2173			}
2174		}
2175		return nil
2176	}
2177	
2178	func receivePack(
2179		ctx context.Context,
2180		session transport.ReceivePackSession,
2181		store storer.Storer,
2182		req *packp.ReferenceUpdateRequest,
2183		hashes []plumbing.Hash,
2184		sendPack bool,
2185		useRefDeltas bool,
2186	) (*packp.ReportStatus, error) {
2187		if !sendPack {
2188			return session.ReceivePack(ctx, req)
2189		}
2190	
2191		rd, wr := io.Pipe()
2192		req.Packfile = rd
2193		done := make(chan error, 1)
2194	
2195		go func() {
2196			enc := packfile.NewEncoder(wr, store, useRefDeltas)
2197			if _, err := enc.Encode(hashes, 10); err != nil {
2198				done <- wr.CloseWithError(err)
2199				return
2200			}
2201			done <- wr.Close()
2202		}()
2203	
2204		report, err := session.ReceivePack(ctx, req)
2205		if err != nil {
2206			_ = rd.Close()
2207			return nil, err
2208		}
2209		if err := <-done; err != nil {
2210			return nil, err
2211		}
2212		return report, nil
2213	}
2214	
2215	func receivePackStream(
2216		ctx context.Context,
2217		session transport.ReceivePackSession,
2218		req *packp.ReferenceUpdateRequest,
2219		pack io.ReadCloser,
2220	) (*packp.ReportStatus, error) {
2221		req.Packfile = pack
2222		report, err := session.ReceivePack(ctx, req)
2223		if err != nil {
2224			_ = pack.Close()
2225			return nil, err
2226		}
2227		return report, pack.Close()
2228	}
2229	
2230	func objectsToPush(store storer.Storer, wants []plumbing.Hash, targetRefs map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash) ([]plumbing.Hash, error) {
2231		targetHaves := sortedUniqueHashes(mapsRefValues(targetRefs))
2232		if len(wants) == 0 {
2233			return nil, nil
2234		}
2235	
2236		haveSet := make(map[plumbing.Hash]struct{}, len(targetHaves))
2237		for _, hash := range targetHaves {
2238			haveSet[hash] = struct{}{}
2239		}
2240	
2241		filteredWants := make([]plumbing.Hash, 0, len(wants))
2242		for _, hash := range sortedUniqueHashes(wants) {
2243			if _, ok := haveSet[hash]; ok {
2244				continue
2245			}
2246			filteredWants = append(filteredWants, hash)
2247		}
2248		if len(filteredWants) == 0 {
2249			return nil, nil
2250		}
2251	
2252		seen := make(map[plumbing.Hash]bool, len(filteredWants)*4)
2253		objects := make([]plumbing.Hash, 0, len(filteredWants)*16)
2254		for _, hash := range filteredWants {
2255			if err := collectPushObjects(store, hash, haveSet, seen, &objects); err != nil {
2256				return nil, err
2257			}
2258		}
2259		return objects, nil
2260	}
2261	
2262	func collectPushObjects(
2263		store storer.EncodedObjectStorer,
2264		hash plumbing.Hash,
2265		externalHaves map[plumbing.Hash]struct{},
2266		seen map[plumbing.Hash]bool,
2267		out *[]plumbing.Hash,
2268	) error {
2269		if hash.IsZero() {
2270			return nil
2271		}
2272		if _, ok := externalHaves[hash]; ok {
2273			return nil
2274		}
2275		if seen[hash] {
2276			return nil
2277		}
2278		seen[hash] = true
2279	
2280		obj, err := store.EncodedObject(plumbing.AnyObject, hash)
2281		if err != nil {
2282			return fmt.Errorf("load object %s: %w", hash, err)
2283		}
2284	
2285		switch obj.Type() {
2286		case plumbing.CommitObject:
2287			commit, err := object.GetCommit(store, hash)
2288			if err != nil {
2289				return fmt.Errorf("load commit %s: %w", hash, err)
2290			}
2291			if err := collectPushObjects(store, commit.TreeHash, externalHaves, seen, out); err != nil {
2292				return err
2293			}
2294			for _, parentHash := range commit.ParentHashes {
2295				if err := collectPushObjects(store, parentHash, externalHaves, seen, out); err != nil {
2296					return err
2297				}
2298			}
2299		case plumbing.TreeObject:
2300			tree, err := object.GetTree(store, hash)
2301			if err != nil {
2302				return fmt.Errorf("load tree %s: %w", hash, err)
2303			}
2304			for _, entry := range tree.Entries {
2305				if err := collectPushObjects(store, entry.Hash, externalHaves, seen, out); err != nil {
2306					return err
2307				}
2308			}
2309		case plumbing.TagObject:
2310			tag, err := object.GetTag(store, hash)
2311			if err != nil {
2312				return fmt.Errorf("load tag %s: %w", hash, err)
2313			}
2314			if err := collectPushObjects(store, tag.Target, externalHaves, seen, out); err != nil {
2315				return err
2316			}
2317		case plumbing.BlobObject:
2318		default:
2319			return fmt.Errorf("unsupported object type %s for %s", obj.Type(), hash)
2320		}
2321	
2322		*out = append(*out, hash)
2323		return nil
2324	}

2354	func (r *packLimitReadCloser) Read(p []byte) (int, error) {

2363	func startMeasurement(enabled bool) func() Measurement {

2366		}

2368		start := time.Now()
2369		var startStats runtime.MemStats
2370		runtime.ReadMemStats(&startStats)
2371	
2372		done := make(chan struct{})
2373		var (
2374			mu            sync.Mutex
2375			peakAlloc     = startStats.Alloc
2376			peakHeapInuse = startStats.HeapInuse
2377			result        Measurement
2378		)
2379	
2380		go func() {
2381			ticker := time.NewTicker(10 * time.Millisecond)
2382			defer ticker.Stop()
2383			for {
2384				select {
2385				case <-done:
2386					return
2387				case <-ticker.C:
2388					var current runtime.MemStats
2389					runtime.ReadMemStats(&current)
2390					mu.Lock()
2391					if current.Alloc > peakAlloc {
2392						peakAlloc = current.Alloc
2393					}
2394					if current.HeapInuse > peakHeapInuse {
2395						peakHeapInuse = current.HeapInuse
2396					}
2397					mu.Unlock()
2398				}
2399			}

2479	func newTransportConn(raw Endpoint, label string, stats *statsCollector) (*transportConn, error) {
2480		ep, err := transport.NewEndpoint(raw.URL)
2481		if err != nil {
2482			return nil, err
2483		}
2484		auth, err := resolveAuthMethod(raw, ep)
2485		if err != nil {
2486			return nil, err
2487		}
2488	
2489		baseTransport := http.DefaultTransport
2490		if raw.SkipTLSVerify {
2491			if cloned, ok := http.DefaultTransport.(*http.Transport); ok {
2492				transportClone := cloned.Clone()
2493				if transportClone.TLSClientConfig == nil {
2494					transportClone.TLSClientConfig = &tls.Config{MinVersion: tls.VersionTLS12}
2495				}
2496				transportClone.TLSClientConfig.InsecureSkipVerify = true
2497				baseTransport = transportClone
2498			}
2499		}
2500	
2501		httpClient := &http.Client{
2502			Transport: &countingRoundTripper{
2503				base:  baseTransport,
2504				label: label,
2505				stats: stats,
2506			},
2507		}
2508	
2509		return &transportConn{
2510			label:     label,
2511			endpoint:  ep,
2512			transport: transporthttp.NewClient(httpClient),
2513			http:      httpClient,
2514			raw:       raw,
2515			auth:      auth,
2516			stats:     stats,
2517		}, nil
2518	}

2533	func reachesCommitHash(store storer.EncodedObjectStorer, start *object.Commit, target plumbing.Hash) (bool, error) {
2534		if start.Hash == target {
2535			return true, nil
2536		}
2537	
2538		seen := map[plumbing.Hash]bool{}
2539		stack := []*object.Commit{start}
2540	
2541		for len(stack) > 0 {
2542			current := stack[len(stack)-1]
2543			stack = stack[:len(stack)-1]
2544			if seen[current.Hash] {
2545				continue
2546			}
2547			seen[current.Hash] = true
2548	
2549			for _, parentHash := range current.ParentHashes {
2550				if parentHash == target {
2551					return true, nil
2552				}
2553				if seen[parentHash] {
2554					continue
2555				}
2556				parent, err := object.GetCommit(store, parentHash)
2557				if err != nil {
2558					if errors.Is(err, plumbing.ErrObjectNotFound) {
2559						continue
2560					}
2561					return false, err
2562				}
2563				stack = append(stack, parent)
2564			}
2565		}
2566	
2567		return false, nil
2568	}

2674	func resolveAuthMethod(raw Endpoint, ep *transport.Endpoint) (transport.AuthMethod, error) {
2675		if auth := raw.authMethod(); auth != nil {
2676			return auth, nil
2677		}
2678		if ep == nil {
2679			return nil, nil
2680		}
2681		if ep.Protocol != "http" && ep.Protocol != "https" {
2682			return nil, nil
2683		}
2684		if username, password, ok := lookupEntireDBCredential(raw, ep); ok {
2685			return &transporthttp.BasicAuth{Username: username, Password: password}, nil
2686		}
2687		username, password, ok := lookupGitCredential(ep)
2688		if !ok {
2689			return nil, nil
2690		}
2691		return &transporthttp.BasicAuth{Username: username, Password: password}, nil
2692	}
2693	
2694	func lookupEntireDBCredential(raw Endpoint, ep *transport.Endpoint) (string, string, bool) {
2695		if ep == nil || ep.Host == "" {
2696			return "", "", false
2697		}
2698		credHost := endpointCredentialHost(ep)
2699		token, ok := lookupEntireDBToken(credHost, endpointBaseURL(ep), raw.SkipTLSVerify)
2700		if !ok || token == "" {
2701			return "", "", false
2702		}
2703		username := raw.Username
2704		if username == "" {
2705			username = "git"
2706		}
2707		return username, token, true
2708	}

2710	func endpointBaseURL(ep *transport.Endpoint) string {
2711		if ep == nil || ep.Host == "" {
2712			return ""
2713		}
2714		scheme := ep.Protocol
2715		if scheme == "" {
2716			scheme = "https"
2717		}
2718		host := ep.Host
2719		if ep.Port > 0 {
2720			host = fmt.Sprintf("%s:%d", host, ep.Port)
2721		}
2722		return scheme + "://" + host
2723	}
2724	
2725	func endpointCredentialHost(ep *transport.Endpoint) string {

2728		}

2731		}
2732		return ep.Host

2735	func lookupEntireDBToken(host, baseURL string, skipTLSVerify bool) (string, bool) {
2736		configDir := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_CONFIG_DIR")
2737		if configDir == "" {
2738			home, err := os.UserHomeDir()
2739			if err != nil {
2740				return "", false
2741			}
2742			configDir = filepath.Join(home, ".config", "entire")
2743		}
2744	
2745		username, ok := loadEntireDBActiveUser(host, configDir)
2746		if !ok || username == "" {
2747			return "", false
2748		}
2749		token, err := getEntireDBTokenWithRefresh(context.Background(), host, username, baseURL, skipTLSVerify)
2750		if err != nil {
2751			return "", false
2752		}
2753		if token == "" {
2754			return "", false
2755		}
2756		return token, true
2757	}
2758	
2759	func loadEntireDBActiveUser(host, configDir string) (string, bool) {
2760		data, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(configDir, "hosts.json"))
2761		if err != nil {
2762			return "", false
2763		}
2764		var hosts map[string]*entireAuthHostInfo
2765		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &hosts); err != nil {
2766			return "", false
2767		}
2768		info := hosts[host]
2769		if info == nil || info.ActiveUser == "" {
2770			return "", false
2771		}
2772		return info.ActiveUser, true
2773	}

2775	func getEntireDBTokenWithRefresh(ctx context.Context, host, username, baseURL string, skipTLSVerify bool) (string, error) {
2776		encodedToken, err := readEntireDBStoredToken(entireCredentialService(host), username)
2777		if err != nil {
2778			return "", err
2779		}
2780		token, expiresAt := decodeTokenWithExpiration(encodedToken)
2781		if token == "" {
2782			return "", nil
2783		}
2784		if !tokenExpiredOrExpiring(expiresAt) {
2785			return token, nil
2786		}
2787		refreshed, err := refreshEntireDBAccessToken(ctx, host, username, baseURL, skipTLSVerify)
2788		if err != nil {
2789			return token, nil
2790		}
2791		return refreshed, nil
2792	}
2793	
2794	func decodeTokenWithExpiration(encoded string) (string, time.Time) {
2795		idx := strings.LastIndex(encoded, "|")
2796		if idx == -1 {
2797			return encoded, time.Time{}
2798		}
2799		token := encoded[:idx]
2800		expiresAtUnix, err := strconv.ParseInt(encoded[idx+1:], 10, 64)
2801		if err != nil {
2802			return encoded, time.Time{}
2803		}
2804		return token, time.Unix(expiresAtUnix, 0)
2805	}
2806	
2807	func tokenExpiredOrExpiring(expiresAt time.Time) bool {
2808		if expiresAt.IsZero() {
2809			return true
2810		}
2811		return time.Now().Add(5 * time.Minute).After(expiresAt)
2812	}
2813	
2814	func refreshEntireDBAccessToken(ctx context.Context, host, username, baseURL string, skipTLSVerify bool) (string, error) {
2815		refreshToken, err := readEntireDBStoredToken(entireCredentialService(host)+":refresh", username)
2816		if err != nil {
2817			return "", err
2818		}
2819		if refreshToken == "" || baseURL == "" {
2820			return "", errors.New("missing refresh token or base url")
2821		}
2822	
2823		form := url.Values{}
2824		form.Set("grant_type", "refresh_token")
2825		form.Set("refresh_token", refreshToken)
2826		form.Set("client_id", entireCLIClientID)
2827	
2828		req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodPost, strings.TrimRight(baseURL, "/")+"/oauth/token", strings.NewReader(form.Encode()))
2829		if err != nil {
2830			return "", err
2831		}
2832		req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/x-www-form-urlencoded")
2833	
2834		client := &http.Client{
2835			Timeout: 30 * time.Second,
2836			Transport: &http.Transport{
2837				TLSClientConfig: &tls.Config{InsecureSkipVerify: skipTLSVerify}, //nolint:gosec
2838			},
2839		}
2840		resp, err := client.Do(req)
2841		if err != nil {
2842			return "", err
2843		}
2844		defer resp.Body.Close()
2845		if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
2846			return "", fmt.Errorf("refresh failed with status %d", resp.StatusCode)
2847		}
2848	
2849		var tokenResp oauthTokenResponse
2850		if err := json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(&tokenResp); err != nil {
2851			return "", err
2852		}
2853		if tokenResp.AccessToken == "" {
2854			return "", errors.New("empty access token in refresh response")
2855		}
2856	
2857		if err := writeEntireDBStoredToken(
2858			entireCredentialService(host),
2859			username,
2860			encodeTokenWithExpiration(tokenResp.AccessToken, tokenResp.ExpiresIn),
2861		); err != nil {
2862			return "", err
2863		}
2864		if tokenResp.RefreshToken != "" {
2865			_ = writeEntireDBStoredToken(entireCredentialService(host)+":refresh", username, tokenResp.RefreshToken)
2866		}
2867		return tokenResp.AccessToken, nil
2868	}
2869	
2870	func encodeTokenWithExpiration(token string, expiresIn int64) string {
2871		return fmt.Sprintf("%s|%d", token, time.Now().Unix()+expiresIn)
2872	}
2873	
2874	func entireCredentialService(host string) string {
2875		return "entire:" + host
2876	}
2877	
2878	func readEntireDBStoredToken(service, username string) (string, error) {
2879		if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TOKEN_STORE") == "file" {
2880			path := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TOKEN_STORE_PATH")
2881			if path == "" {
2882				home, err := os.UserHomeDir()
2883				if err != nil {
2884					return "", err
2885				}
2886				path = filepath.Join(home, ".config", "entiredb", "tokens.json")
2887			}
2888			return readEntireDBFileToken(path, service, username)
2889		}
2890		return keyring.Get(service, username)
2891	}
2892	
2893	func writeEntireDBStoredToken(service, username, password string) error {
2894		if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TOKEN_STORE") == "file" {
2895			path := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TOKEN_STORE_PATH")
2896			if path == "" {
2897				home, err := os.UserHomeDir()
2898				if err != nil {
2899					return err
2900				}
2901				path = filepath.Join(home, ".config", "entiredb", "tokens.json")
2902			}
2903			return writeEntireDBFileToken(path, service, username, password)
2904		}
2905		return keyring.Set(service, username, password)
2906	}
2907	
2908	func readEntireDBFileToken(path, service, username string) (string, error) {
2909		data, err := os.ReadFile(path)
2910		if err != nil {
2911			if os.IsNotExist(err) {
2912				return "", keyring.ErrNotFound
2913			}
2914			return "", err
2915		}
2916		var store map[string]map[string]string
2917		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &store); err != nil {
2918			return "", err
2919		}
2920		users := store[service]
2921		if users == nil {
2922			return "", keyring.ErrNotFound
2923		}
2924		password, ok := users[username]
2925		if !ok {
2926			return "", keyring.ErrNotFound
2927		}
2928		return password, nil
2929	}
2930	
2931	func writeEntireDBFileToken(path, service, username, password string) error {
2932		store := map[string]map[string]string{}
2933		if data, err := os.ReadFile(path); err == nil {
2934			if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &store); err != nil {
2935				return err
2936			}
2937		} else if !os.IsNotExist(err) {
2938			return err
2939		}
2940		if store[service] == nil {
2941			store[service] = map[string]string{}
2942		}
2943		store[service][username] = password
2944		if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(path), 0o700); err != nil {
2945			return err
2946		}
2947		data, err := json.Marshal(store)
2948		if err != nil {
2949			return err
2950		}
2951		return os.WriteFile(path, data, 0o600)
2952	}
2953	
2954	func lookupGitCredential(ep *transport.Endpoint) (string, string, bool) {
2955		input := credentialFillInput(ep)
2956		if input == "" {
2957			return "", "", false
2958		}
2959		output, err := gitCredentialFillCommand(context.Background(), input)
2960		if err != nil {
2961			return "", "", false
2962		}
2963		values := parseCredentialFillOutput(output)
2964		password := values["password"]
2965		if password == "" {
2966			return "", "", false
2967		}
2968		username := values["username"]
2969		if username == "" {
2970			if ep.User != "" {
2971				username = ep.User
2972			} else {
2973				username = "git"
2974			}
2975		}
2976		return username, password, true
2977	}
2978	
2979	func credentialFillInput(ep *transport.Endpoint) string {
2980		if ep == nil || ep.Host == "" {
2981			return ""
2982		}
2983		var builder strings.Builder
2984		builder.WriteString("protocol=")
2985		builder.WriteString(ep.Protocol)
2986		builder.WriteString("\n")
2987		builder.WriteString("host=")
2988		builder.WriteString(ep.Host)
2989		builder.WriteString("\n")
2990		if path := strings.TrimPrefix(ep.Path, "/"); path != "" {
2991			builder.WriteString("path=")
2992			builder.WriteString(path)
2993			builder.WriteString("\n")
2994		}
2995		if ep.User != "" {
2996			builder.WriteString("username=")
2997			builder.WriteString(ep.User)
2998			builder.WriteString("\n")
2999		}
3000		builder.WriteString("\n")
3001		return builder.String()
3002	}
3003	
3004	func parseCredentialFillOutput(output []byte) map[string]string {
3005		values := map[string]string{}
3006		for _, line := range bytes.Split(output, []byte{'\n'}) {
3007			line = bytes.TrimSpace(line)
3008			if len(line) == 0 {
3009				continue
3010			}
3011			key, value, ok := bytes.Cut(line, []byte{'='})
3012			if !ok {
3013				continue
3014			}
3015			values[string(key)] = string(value)
3016		}
3017		return values
3018	}
3019	
3020	func buildSidebandIfSupported(l *capability.List, reader io.Reader, p sideband.Progress) io.Reader {
3021		var t sideband.Type
3022		switch {
3023		case l.Supports(capability.Sideband):
3024			t = sideband.Sideband
3025		case l.Supports(capability.Sideband64k):
3026			t = sideband.Sideband64k
3027		default:
3028			return reader
3029		}
3030	
3031		d := sideband.NewDemuxer(t, reader)
3032		d.Progress = p
3033		return d
3034	}
3035	
3036	type statsCollector struct {
3037		enabled bool
3038		items   map[string]*ServiceStats
3039	}
3040	
3041	func newStats(enabled bool) *statsCollector {
3042		return &statsCollector{enabled: enabled, items: map[string]*ServiceStats{}}
3043	}
3044	
3045	func (s *statsCollector) ensure(name string) *ServiceStats {
3046		item, ok := s.items[name]
3047		if !ok {
3048			item = &ServiceStats{Name: name}
3049			s.items[name] = item
3050		}
3051		return item
3052	}
3053	
3054	func (s *statsCollector) addWantsHaves(name string, wants, haves int) {
3055		if !s.enabled {
3056			return
3057		}
3058		item := s.ensure(name)
3059		item.Wants += wants
3060		item.Haves += haves
3061	}
3062	
3063	func (s *statsCollector) addCommands(name string, commands int) {
3064		if !s.enabled {
3065			return
3066		}
3067		item := s.ensure(name)
3068		item.Commands += commands
3069	}
3070	
3071	func (s *statsCollector) recordRoundTrip(name string, requestBytes, responseBytes int64) {
3072		if !s.enabled {
3073			return
3074		}
3075		item := s.ensure(name)
3076		item.Requests++
3077		item.RequestBytes += requestBytes
3078		item.ResponseBytes += responseBytes
3079	}
3080	
3081	func (s *statsCollector) snapshot() Stats {
3082		out := Stats{Enabled: s.enabled, Items: map[string]*ServiceStats{}}
3083		for key, item := range s.items {
3084			copyItem := *item
3085			out.Items[key] = &copyItem
3086		}
3087		return out
3088	}
3089	
3090	type countingRoundTripper struct {
3091		base  http.RoundTripper
3092		label string
3093		stats *statsCollector
3094	}
3095	
3096	func (rt *countingRoundTripper) RoundTrip(req *http.Request) (*http.Response, error) {
3097		res, err := rt.base.RoundTrip(req)
3098		if err != nil {
3099			return nil, err
3100		}
3101	
3102		serviceName := req.Header.Get(statsPhaseHdr)
3103		if serviceName == "" {
3104			serviceName = req.URL.Query().Get("service")
3105			if serviceName == "" {
3106				serviceName = strings.TrimPrefix(req.URL.Path[strings.LastIndex(req.URL.Path, "/")+1:], "/")
3107			}
3108		}
3109		name := strings.TrimSpace(rt.label + " " + serviceName)
3110		requestBytes := req.ContentLength
3111		if requestBytes < 0 {
3112			requestBytes = 0
3113		}
3114	
3115		res.Body = &countingReadCloser{
3116			ReadCloser: res.Body,
3117			onClose: func(n int64) {
3118				rt.stats.recordRoundTrip(name, requestBytes, n)
3119			},
3120		}
3121		return res, nil
3122	}
3123	
3124	type countingReadCloser struct {
3125		io.ReadCloser
3126		n       int64
3127		onClose func(int64)
3128	}
3129	
3130	func (c *countingReadCloser) Read(p []byte) (int, error) {
3131		n, err := c.ReadCloser.Read(p)
3132		c.n += int64(n)
3133		return n, err
3134	}
3135	
3136	func (c *countingReadCloser) Close() error {
3137		err := c.ReadCloser.Close()
3138		if c.onClose != nil {
3139			c.onClose(c.n)
3140			c.onClose = nil
3141		}
3142		return err
3143	}
