  "content": "package gitproto\n\nimport (\n\t\"bufio\"\n\t\"bytes\"\n\t\"encoding/hex\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"io\"\n\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/format/pktline\"\n)\n\n// PacketType represents the type of a pkt-line packet.\ntype PacketType int\n\nconst (\n\tPacketData        PacketType = iota\n\tPacketFlush                  // 0000\n\tPacketDelim                  // 0001\n\tPacketResponseEnd            // 0002\n)\n\n// PacketReader reads pkt-line formatted data. It reuses a fixed header buffer\n// and a growable payload buffer to reduce allocations (issue #17).\ntype PacketReader struct {\n\tr      *bufio.Reader\n\theader [4]byte\n\tbuf    []byte\n}\n\nfunc NewPacketReader(r io.Reader) *PacketReader {\n\tif br, ok := r.(*bufio.Reader); ok {\n\t\treturn &PacketReader{r: br, buf: make([]byte, 0, 1024)}\n\t}\n\treturn &PacketReader{r: bufio.NewReaderSize(r, 65536), buf: make([]byte, 0, 1024)}\n}\n\n// BufReader returns the underlying buffered reader for direct access\n// (needed by sideband demuxer after switching to pack stream).\nfunc (pr *PacketReader) BufReader() *bufio.Reader {\n\treturn pr.r\n}\n\n// ReadPacket reads the next pkt-line packet. The returned payload slice is\n// only valid until the next call to ReadPacket.\nfunc (pr *PacketReader) ReadPacket() (PacketType, []byte, error) {\n\tif _, err := io.ReadFull(pr.r, pr.header[:]); err != nil {\n\t\treturn PacketData, nil, err\n\t}\n\n\tswitch string(pr.header[:]) {\n\tcase \"0000\":\n\t\treturn PacketFlush, nil, nil\n\tcase \"0001\":\n\t\treturn PacketDelim, nil, nil\n\tcase \"0002\":\n\t\treturn PacketResponseEnd, nil, nil\n\t}\n\n\tn, err := parseHexLength(pr.header)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn PacketData, nil, err\n\t}\n\tif n <= 4 {\n\t\treturn PacketData, nil, pktline.ErrInvalidPktLen\n\t}\n\n\tpayloadLen := n - 4\n\tif payloadLen > cap(pr.buf) {\n\t\tpr.buf = make([]byte, payloadLen)\n\t} else {\n\t\tpr.buf = pr.buf[:payloadLen]\n\t}\n\tif _, err := io.ReadFull(pr.r, pr.buf); err != nil {\n\t\treturn PacketData, nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn PacketData, pr.buf, nil\n}\n\nfunc parseHexLength(header [4]byte) (int, error) {\n\tvar n int\n\tfor _, b := range header {\n\t\tv, ok := hexVal(b)\n\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\treturn 0, pktline.ErrInvalidPktLen\n\t\t}\n\t\tn = 16*n + int(v)\n\t}\n\treturn n, nil\n}\n\nfunc hexVal(b byte) (byte, bool) {\n\tswitch {\n\tcase b >= '0' && b <= '9':\n\t\treturn b - '0', true\n\tcase b >= 'a' && b <= 'f':\n\t\treturn b - 'a' + 10, true\n\tcase b >= 'A' && b <= 'F':\n\t\treturn b - 'A' + 10, true\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn 0, false\n\t}\n}\n\n// EncodeCommand builds a pkt-line encoded v2 command request.\nfunc EncodeCommand(command string, capArgs, cmdArgs []string) ([]byte, error) {\n\tvar buf bytes.Buffer\n\tenc := pktline.NewEncoder(&buf)\n\tif err := enc.EncodeString(\"command=\" + command + \"\\n\"); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tfor _, arg := range capArgs {\n\t\tif err := enc.EncodeString(arg + \"\\n\"); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif len(cmdArgs) > 0 {\n\t\tif _, err := buf.WriteString(\"0001\"); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor _, arg := range cmdArgs {\n\t\t\tif err := enc.EncodeString(arg + \"\\n\"); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif err := enc.Flush(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn buf.Bytes(), nil\n}\n\n// SkipSection reads and discards packets until a delimiter or flush is reached.\nfunc SkipSection(pr *PacketReader) error {\n\tfor {\n\t\tkind, _, err := pr.ReadPacket()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t\tif kind == PacketDelim || kind == PacketFlush {\n\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\n// HashHex is a helper to encode a 20-byte hash as lowercase hex.\nfunc HashHex(h [20]byte) string {\n\treturn hex.EncodeToString(h[:])\n}\n\n// FormatPktLine encodes a single pkt-line from a string payload.\nfunc FormatPktLine(s string) string {\n\tn := len(s) + 4\n\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%04x%s\", n, s)\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/gitproto/capability.go",
  "content": "package gitproto\n\nimport (\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"io\"\n\t\"sort\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/protocol/packp/capability\"\n)\n\n// V2Capabilities represents a parsed protocol v2 capability advertisement.\ntype V2Capabilities struct {\n\tCaps map[string]string\n}\n\n// Supports reports whether the server advertises the named capability.\nfunc (c *V2Capabilities) Supports(name string) bool {\n\tif c == nil {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\t_, ok := c.Caps[name]\n\treturn ok\n}\n\n// Value returns the value string for the named capability.\nfunc (c *V2Capabilities) Value(name string) string {\n\tif c == nil {\n\t\treturn \"\"\n\t}\n\treturn c.Caps[name]\n}\n\n// FetchSupports checks whether a specific feature is listed in the\n// \"fetch\" capability value (space-separated feature list).\nfunc (c *V2Capabilities) FetchSupports(feature string) bool {\n\tif c == nil {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tfor _, f := range strings.Fields(c.Value(\"fetch\")) {\n\t\tif f == feature {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n\n// SortedKeys returns the capabilities as sorted \"key\" or \"key=value\" strings.\nfunc (c *V2Capabilities) SortedKeys() []string {\n\tif c == nil {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\tkeys := make([]string, 0, len(c.Caps))\n\tfor k, v := range c.Caps {\n\t\tif v == \"\" {\n\t\t\tkeys = append(keys, k)\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tkeys = append(keys, k+\"=\"+v)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tsort.Strings(keys)\n\treturn keys\n}\n\n// RequestCapabilities builds the capability arguments for a v2 command request.\nfunc (c *V2Capabilities) RequestCapabilities() []string {\n\tvar caps []string\n\tif agent := c.Value(\"agent\"); agent != \"\" {\n\t\tcaps = append(caps, \"agent=\"+capability.DefaultAgent())\n\t}\n\treturn caps\n}\n\n// DecodeV2Capabilities parses a protocol v2 capability advertisement from an\n// info/refs response body.\nfunc DecodeV2Capabilities(r io.Reader) (*V2Capabilities, error) {\n\treader := NewPacketReader(r)\n\n\t// Skip service line and find \"version 2\\n\"\n\tfor {\n\t\tkind, payload, err := reader.ReadPacket()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tif kind == PacketFlush {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif kind != PacketData {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"unexpected packet type %v before protocol advertisement\", kind)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif strings.HasPrefix(string(payload), \"# service=\") {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif string(payload) != \"version 2\\n\" {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"unexpected protocol advertisement %q\", payload)\n\t\t}\n\t\tbreak\n\t}\n\n\tcaps := &V2Capabilities{Caps: make(map[string]string)}\n\tfor {\n\t\tkind, payload, err := reader.ReadPacket()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tif kind == PacketFlush {\n\t\t\treturn caps, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\tif kind != PacketData {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"unexpected packet type %v in capability advertisement\", kind)\n\t\t}\n\t\tline := strings.TrimSuffix(string(payload), \"\\n\")\n\t\tname, value, _ := strings.Cut(line, \"=\")\n\t\tcaps.Caps[name] = value\n\t}\n}\n\n// PreferredSideband returns the best sideband capability supported by the\n// server. Prefers sideband-64k over sideband (issue #4: sideband preference\n// was backwards in original code).\nfunc PreferredSideband(caps *capability.List) capability.Capability {\n\tif caps.Supports(capability.Sideband64k) {\n\t\treturn capability.Sideband64k\n\t}\n\tif caps.Supports(capability.Sideband) {\n\t\treturn capability.Sideband\n\t}\n\treturn \"\"\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/gitproto/smarthttp.go",
  "content": "package gitproto\n\nimport (\n\t\"bytes\"\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"crypto/tls\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"io\"\n\t\"net/http\"\n\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/protocol/packp/capability\"\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/transport\"\n\ttransporthttp \"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/transport/http\"\n)\n\nconst statsPhaseHeader = \"X-Git-Sync-Stats-Phase\"\n\n// Conn represents a connection to a remote Git HTTP endpoint.\ntype Conn struct {\n\tLabel     string\n\tEndpoint  *transport.Endpoint\n\tTransport transport.Transport\n\tHTTP      *http.Client\n\tAuth      transport.AuthMethod\n}\n\n// NewConn creates a new connection to the given endpoint.\nfunc NewConn(ep *transport.Endpoint, label string, auth transport.AuthMethod, rt http.RoundTripper) *Conn {\n\thttpClient := &http.Client{Transport: rt}\n\treturn &Conn{\n\t\tLabel:     label,\n\t\tEndpoint:  ep,\n\t\tTransport: transporthttp.NewClient(httpClient),\n\t\tHTTP:      httpClient,\n\t\tAuth:      auth,\n\t}\n}\n\n// NewHTTPTransport creates an http.Transport with optional TLS skip.\nfunc NewHTTPTransport(skipTLS bool) http.RoundTripper {\n\tif !skipTLS {\n\t\treturn http.DefaultTransport\n\t}\n\tif cloned, ok := http.DefaultTransport.(*http.Transport); ok {\n\t\ttc := cloned.Clone()\n\t\tif tc.TLSClientConfig == nil {\n\t\t\ttc.TLSClientConfig = &tls.Config{MinVersion: tls.VersionTLS12}\n\t\t}\n\t\ttc.TLSClientConfig.InsecureSkipVerify = true\n\t\treturn tc\n\t}\n\treturn http.DefaultTransport\n}\n\n// RequestInfoRefs fetches /info/refs for the given service.\nfunc RequestInfoRefs(ctx context.Context, conn *Conn, service, gitProtocol string) ([]byte, error) {\n\turl := fmt.Sprintf(\"%s/info/refs?service=%s\", conn.Endpoint.String(), service)\n\treq, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodGet, url, nil)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treq.Header.Set(\"Accept\", \"*/*\")\n\treq.Header.Set(\"User-Agent\", capability.DefaultAgent())\n\treq.Header.Set(statsPhaseHeader, service+\" info-refs\")\n\tif gitProtocol != \"\" {\n\t\treq.Header.Set(\"Git-Protocol\", gitProtocol)\n\t}\n\tApplyAuth(req, conn.Auth)\n\n\tres, err := conn.HTTP.Do(req)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tdefer res.Body.Close()\n\tif err := transporthttp.NewErr(res); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\t// Bound the read to prevent unbounded memory allocation (issue #9).\n\tconst maxInfoRefsSize = 64 * 1024 * 1024 // 64 MiB\n\tlr := io.LimitReader(res.Body, maxInfoRefsSize+1)\n\tdata, err := io.ReadAll(lr)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tif int64(len(data)) > maxInfoRefsSize {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"info/refs response exceeds %d byte limit\", maxInfoRefsSize)\n\t}\n\treturn data, nil\n}\n\n// PostRPC sends a buffered POST to the given service and returns the full response body.\nfunc PostRPC(ctx context.Context, conn *Conn, service string, body []byte, v2 bool, phase string) ([]byte, error) {\n\treader, err := PostRPCStream(ctx, conn, service, body, v2, phase)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tdefer reader.Close()\n\treturn io.ReadAll(reader)\n}\n\n// PostRPCStream sends a POST to the given service and returns the response body\n// as a streaming reader. Caller must close the returned ReadCloser.\nfunc PostRPCStream(ctx context.Context, conn *Conn, service string, body []byte, v2 bool, phase string) (io.ReadCloser, error) {\n\turl := fmt.Sprintf(\"%s/%s\", conn.Endpoint.String(), service)\n\treq, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodPost, url, bytes.NewReader(body))\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treq.Header.Set(\"Content-Type\", fmt.Sprintf(\"application/x-%s-request\", service))\n\treq.Header.Set(\"Accept\", fmt.Sprintf(\"application/x-%s-result\", service))\n\treq.Header.Set(\"User-Agent\", capability.DefaultAgent())\n\treq.Header.Set(statsPhaseHeader, phase)\n\tif v2 {\n\t\treq.Header.Set(\"Git-Protocol\", \"version=2\")\n\t}\n\tApplyAuth(req, conn.Auth)\n\n\tres, err := conn.HTTP.Do(req)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tif err := transporthttp.NewErr(res); err != nil {\n\t\t_ = res.Body.Close()\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn res.Body, nil\n}\n\n// ApplyAuth applies the given auth method to an HTTP request.\nfunc ApplyAuth(req *http.Request, auth transport.AuthMethod) {\n\tswitch a := auth.(type) {\n\tcase *transporthttp.BasicAuth:\n\t\ta.SetAuth(req)\n\tcase *transporthttp.TokenAuth:\n\t\ta.SetAuth(req)\n\t}\n}\n"
}

{

  "content": "package gitproto\n\nimport (\n\t\"bytes\"\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing\"\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/protocol/packp\"\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/transport\"\n)\n\n// RefService encapsulates the result of source ref discovery and the negotiated\n// protocol, providing methods for subsequent fetch and pack operations.\ntype RefService struct {\n\tProtocol string // \"v1\" or \"v2\"\n\tV1Adv    *packp.AdvRefs\n\tV2Caps   *V2Capabilities\n}\n\n// ListSourceRefs discovers refs from the source using the configured protocol mode.\n// Returns the list of refs and a RefService for subsequent operations.\nfunc ListSourceRefs(ctx context.Context, conn *Conn, protocolMode string, refPrefixes []string) ([]*plumbing.Reference, *RefService, error) {\n\tswitch protocolMode {\n\tcase \"v1\":\n\t\tadv, refs, err := listSourceRefsV1(ctx, conn)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn refs, &RefService{Protocol: \"v1\", V1Adv: adv}, nil\n\n\tcase \"auto\", \"v2\":\n\t\tdata, err := RequestInfoRefs(ctx, conn, transport.UploadPackServiceName, \"version=2\")\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tif caps, err := DecodeV2Capabilities(bytes.NewReader(data)); err == nil {\n\t\t\tif !caps.Supports(\"ls-refs\") || !caps.Supports(\"fetch\") {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, nil, fmt.Errorf(\"source does not advertise required protocol v2 commands\")\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\trefs, err := listSourceRefsV2(ctx, conn, caps, refPrefixes)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, nil, err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn refs, &RefService{Protocol: \"v2\", V2Caps: caps}, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\tif protocolMode == \"v2\" {\n\t\t\treturn nil, nil, fmt.Errorf(\"source did not negotiate protocol v2\")\n\t\t}\n\t\t// Fall back to v1\n\t\tadv, err := decodeV1AdvRefs(data)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\trefs, err := AdvRefsToSlice(adv)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn refs, &RefService{Protocol: \"v1\", V1Adv: adv}, nil\n\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn nil, nil, fmt.Errorf(\"unsupported protocol mode %q\", protocolMode)\n\t}\n}\n\n// AdvertisedRefsV1 fetches and decodes v1 advertised refs for the given service.\nfunc AdvertisedRefsV1(ctx context.Context, conn *Conn, service string) (*packp.AdvRefs, error) {\n\tdata, err := RequestInfoRefs(ctx, conn, service, \"\")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn decodeV1AdvRefs(data)\n}\n\n// AdvRefsToSlice converts an AdvRefs to a slice of references.\nfunc AdvRefsToSlice(ar *packp.AdvRefs) ([]*plumbing.Reference, error) {\n\trefs, err := ar.AllReferences()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\titer, err := refs.IterReferences()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tdefer iter.Close()\n\n\tvar out []*plumbing.Reference\n\terr = iter.ForEach(func(ref *plumbing.Reference) error {\n\t\tout = append(out, ref)\n\t\treturn nil\n\t})\n\treturn out, err\n}\n\n// AdvRefsCaps returns the sorted capability list from an AdvRefs.\nfunc AdvRefsCaps(adv *packp.AdvRefs) []string {\n\tif adv == nil || adv.Capabilities == nil {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\tall := adv.Capabilities.All()\n\titems := make([]string, 0, len(all))\n\tfor _, cap := range all {\n\t\tvalues := adv.Capabilities.Get(cap)\n\t\tif len(values) == 0 {\n\t\t\titems = append(items, string(cap))\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor _, value := range values {\n\t\t\titems = append(items, string(cap)+\"=\"+value)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn items\n}\n\nfunc listSourceRefsV1(ctx context.Context, conn *Conn) (*packp.AdvRefs, []*plumbing.Reference, error) {\n\tadv, err := AdvertisedRefsV1(ctx, conn, transport.UploadPackServiceName)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, err\n\t}\n\trefs, err := AdvRefsToSlice(adv)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn adv, refs, nil\n}\n\nfunc listSourceRefsV2(ctx context.Context, conn *Conn, caps *V2Capabilities, prefixes []string) ([]*plumbing.Reference, error) {\n\targs := []string{\"peel\"}\n\tfor _, prefix := range prefixes {\n\t\targs = append(args, \"ref-prefix \"+prefix)\n\t}\n\tbody, err := EncodeCommand(\"ls-refs\", caps.RequestCapabilities(), args)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tdata, err := PostRPC(ctx, conn, transport.UploadPackServiceName, body, true, \"upload-pack ls-refs\")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn decodeV2LSRefs(bytes.NewReader(data))\n}\n\nfunc decodeV2LSRefs(r *bytes.Reader) ([]*plumbing.Reference, error) {\n\treader := NewPacketReader(r)\n\tvar refs []*plumbing.Reference\n\tfor {\n\t\tkind, payload, err := reader.ReadPacket()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tif kind == PacketFlush {\n\t\t\treturn refs, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\tif kind != PacketData {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"unexpected packet type %v in ls-refs response\", kind)\n\t\t}\n\t\tfields := strings.Fields(strings.TrimSpace(string(payload)))\n\t\tif len(fields) < 2 {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"malformed ls-refs response line %q\", payload)\n\t\t}\n\t\thash := plumbing.NewHash(fields[0])\n\t\tname := plumbing.ReferenceName(fields[1])\n\t\trefs = append(refs, plumbing.NewHashReference(name, hash))\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc decodeV1AdvRefs(data []byte) (*packp.AdvRefs, error) {\n\tar := packp.NewAdvRefs()\n\tif err := ar.Decode(bytes.NewReader(data)); err != nil {\n\t\tif err == packp.ErrEmptyAdvRefs {\n\t\t\treturn nil, transport.ErrEmptyRemoteRepository\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn ar, nil\n}\n\n// RefHashMap converts a reference slice to a map of name→hash.\nfunc RefHashMap(refs []*plumbing.Reference) map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash {\n\tout := make(map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash, len(refs))\n\tfor _, ref := range refs {\n\t\tif ref.Type() == plumbing.HashReference {\n\t\t\tout[ref.Name()] = ref.Hash()\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn out\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/gitproto/fetch.go",
  "content": "package gitproto\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"errors\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"io\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\n\tgit \"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5\"\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing\"\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/format/packfile\"\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/protocol/packp\"\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/protocol/packp/capability\"\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/protocol/packp/sideband\"\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/storer\"\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/transport\"\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/utils/ioutil\"\n)\n\n// DesiredRef describes a single ref we want to fetch from source.\ntype DesiredRef struct {\n\tSourceRef  plumbing.ReferenceName\n\tTargetRef  plumbing.ReferenceName\n\tSourceHash plumbing.Hash\n\tIsTag      bool\n}\n\n// FetchToStore fetches objects from source into the given store, using the\n// appropriate protocol version.\nfunc (s *RefService) FetchToStore(\n\tctx context.Context,\n\tstore storer.Storer,\n\tconn *Conn,\n\tdesired map[plumbing.ReferenceName]DesiredRef,\n\ttargetRefs map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash,\n) error {\n\tswitch s.Protocol {\n\tcase \"v2\":\n\t\treturn fetchToStoreV2(ctx, store, conn, s.V2Caps, desired, targetRefs)\n\tcase \"v1\":\n\t\treturn fetchToStoreV1(ctx, store, conn, s.V1Adv, desired, targetRefs)\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"unsupported source protocol %q\", s.Protocol)\n\t}\n}\n\n// FetchPack fetches a packfile from source and returns the pack stream as a reader.\n// Caller must close the returned ReadCloser.\nfunc (s *RefService) FetchPack(\n\tctx context.Context,\n\tconn *Conn,\n\tdesired map[plumbing.ReferenceName]DesiredRef,\n\ttargetRefs map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash,\n) (io.ReadCloser, error) {\n\tswitch s.Protocol {\n\tcase \"v2\":\n\t\treturn fetchPackV2(ctx, conn, s.V2Caps, desired, targetRefs)\n\tcase \"v1\":\n\t\treturn fetchPackV1(ctx, conn, s.V1Adv, desired, targetRefs)\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"unsupported source protocol %q\", s.Protocol)\n\t}\n}\n\n// FetchCommitGraph fetches only the commit graph (tree:0 filter) for a ref.\n// Requires v2 with filter support.\nfunc (s *RefService) FetchCommitGraph(\n\tctx context.Context,\n\tstore storer.Storer,\n\tconn *Conn,\n\tref DesiredRef,\n) error {\n\tif s.Protocol != \"v2\" {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"commit graph fetch requires protocol v2\")\n\t}\n\tif !s.V2Caps.FetchSupports(\"filter\") {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"source does not advertise fetch filter support\")\n\t}\n\n\tcmdArgs := []string{\n\t\t\"ofs-delta\",\n\t\t\"no-progress\",\n\t\t\"filter tree:0\",\n\t\t\"want \" + ref.SourceHash.String(),\n\t\t\"done\",\n\t}\n\tbody, err := EncodeCommand(\"fetch\", s.V2Caps.RequestCapabilities(), cmdArgs)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\treader, err := PostRPCStream(ctx, conn, transport.UploadPackServiceName, body, true, \"upload-pack fetch\")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tdefer ioutil.CheckClose(reader, &err)\n\treturn storeV2FetchPack(store, reader)\n}\n\n// Capabilities returns the sorted capability list for display.\nfunc (s *RefService) Capabilities() []string {\n\tswitch s.Protocol {\n\tcase \"v2\":\n\t\treturn s.V2Caps.SortedKeys()\n\tcase \"v1\":\n\t\treturn AdvRefsCaps(s.V1Adv)\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n}\n\n// --- V2 fetch implementation ---\n\nfunc fetchToStoreV2(\n\tctx context.Context,\n\tstore storer.Storer,\n\tconn *Conn,\n\tcaps *V2Capabilities,\n\tdesired map[plumbing.ReferenceName]DesiredRef,\n\ttargetRefs map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash,\n) error {\n\twants := collectWants(desired)\n\thaves := SortedUniqueHashes(refValues(targetRefs))\n\tif len(wants) == 0 {\n\t\treturn git.NoErrAlreadyUpToDate\n\t}\n\n\tcmdArgs := make([]string, 0, len(wants)+len(haves)+4)\n\tcmdArgs = append(cmdArgs, \"ofs-delta\", \"no-progress\")\n\tfor _, h := range wants {\n\t\tcmdArgs = append(cmdArgs, \"want \"+h.String())\n\t}\n\tfor _, h := range haves {\n\t\tcmdArgs = append(cmdArgs, \"have \"+h.String())\n\t}\n\tcmdArgs = append(cmdArgs, \"done\")\n\n\tbody, err := EncodeCommand(\"fetch\", caps.RequestCapabilities(), cmdArgs)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\treader, err := PostRPCStream(ctx, conn, transport.UploadPackServiceName, body, true, \"upload-pack fetch\")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tdefer ioutil.CheckClose(reader, &err)\n\treturn storeV2FetchPack(store, reader)\n}\n\nfunc fetchPackV2(\n\tctx context.Context,\n\tconn *Conn,\n\tcaps *V2Capabilities,\n\tdesired map[plumbing.ReferenceName]DesiredRef,\n\ttargetRefs map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash,\n) (io.ReadCloser, error) {\n\twants := collectWants(desired)\n\thaves := SortedUniqueHashes(refValues(targetRefs))\n\tif len(wants) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil, git.NoErrAlreadyUpToDate\n\t}\n\n\tcmdArgs := make([]string, 0, len(wants)+len(haves)+4)\n\tcmdArgs = append(cmdArgs, \"ofs-delta\", \"no-progress\")\n\t// Only request include-tag if the server supports it (issue #6).\n\tif hasTag(desired) && caps.FetchSupports(\"include-tag\") {\n\t\tcmdArgs = append(cmdArgs, \"include-tag\")\n\t}\n\tfor _, h := range wants {\n\t\tcmdArgs = append(cmdArgs, \"want \"+h.String())\n\t}\n\tfor _, h := range haves {\n\t\tcmdArgs = append(cmdArgs, \"have \"+h.String())\n\t}\n\tcmdArgs = append(cmdArgs, \"done\")\n\n\tbody, err := EncodeCommand(\"fetch\", caps.RequestCapabilities(), cmdArgs)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treader, err := PostRPCStream(ctx, conn, transport.UploadPackServiceName, body, true, \"upload-pack fetch\")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tpackStream, err := openV2PackStream(reader)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\t_ = reader.Close()\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn packStream, nil\n}\n\nfunc storeV2FetchPack(store storer.Storer, r io.Reader) error {\n\treader := NewPacketReader(r)\n\tfor {\n\t\tkind, payload, err := reader.ReadPacket()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tif errors.Is(err, io.EOF) {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"decode protocol v2 fetch response: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tswitch kind {\n\t\tcase PacketFlush:\n\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\tcase PacketDelim, PacketResponseEnd:\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\tcase PacketData:\n\t\t\tline := string(payload)\n\t\t\tswitch line {\n\t\t\tcase \"packfile\\n\":\n\t\t\t\tdemux := sideband.NewDemuxer(sideband.Sideband64k, reader.BufReader())\n\t\t\t\treturn packfile.UpdateObjectStorage(store, demux)\n\t\t\tcase \"acknowledgments\\n\", \"shallow-info\\n\":\n\t\t\t\tif err := SkipSection(reader); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tdefault:\n\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"unexpected protocol v2 fetch section %q\", strings.TrimSpace(line))\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc openV2PackStream(body io.ReadCloser) (io.ReadCloser, error) {\n\treader := NewPacketReader(body)\n\tfor {\n\t\tkind, payload, err := reader.ReadPacket()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tif errors.Is(err, io.EOF) {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, io.ErrUnexpectedEOF\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"decode protocol v2 fetch response: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tswitch kind {\n\t\tcase PacketFlush:\n\t\t\treturn nil, io.ErrUnexpectedEOF\n\t\tcase PacketDelim, PacketResponseEnd:\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\tcase PacketData:\n\t\t\tline := string(payload)\n\t\t\tswitch line {\n\t\t\tcase \"packfile\\n\":\n\t\t\t\treturn &wrappedRC{\n\t\t\t\t\tReader: sideband.NewDemuxer(sideband.Sideband64k, reader.BufReader()),\n\t\t\t\t\tCloser: body,\n\t\t\t\t}, nil\n\t\t\tcase \"acknowledgments\\n\", \"shallow-info\\n\":\n\t\t\t\tif err := SkipSection(reader); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tdefault:\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"unexpected protocol v2 fetch section %q\", strings.TrimSpace(line))\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\n// --- V1 fetch implementation ---\n\nfunc fetchToStoreV1(\n\tctx context.Context,\n\tstore storer.Storer,\n\tconn *Conn,\n\tadv *packp.AdvRefs,\n\tdesired map[plumbing.ReferenceName]DesiredRef,\n\ttargetRefs map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash,\n) error {\n\tsession, err := conn.Transport.NewUploadPackSession(conn.Endpoint, conn.Auth)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"open source upload-pack session: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tdefer session.Close()\n\n\treq := packp.NewUploadPackRequestFromCapabilities(adv.Capabilities)\n\tfor _, ref := range desired {\n\t\treq.Wants = append(req.Wants, ref.SourceHash)\n\t}\n\treq.Wants = SortedUniqueHashes(req.Wants)\n\treq.Haves = SortedUniqueHashes(refValues(targetRefs))\n\tif len(req.Wants) == 0 {\n\t\treturn git.NoErrAlreadyUpToDate\n\t}\n\tif adv.Capabilities.Supports(capability.NoProgress) {\n\t\t_ = req.Capabilities.Set(capability.NoProgress)\n\t}\n\tif hasTag(desired) && adv.Capabilities.Supports(capability.IncludeTag) {\n\t\t_ = req.Capabilities.Set(capability.IncludeTag)\n\t}\n\n\treader, err := session.UploadPack(ctx, req)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tif errors.Is(err, transport.ErrEmptyUploadPackRequest) {\n\t\t\treturn git.NoErrAlreadyUpToDate\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"source upload-pack: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tdefer ioutil.CheckClose(reader, &err)\n\n\tsbReader := buildSidebandReader(req.Capabilities, reader, nil)\n\treturn packfile.UpdateObjectStorage(store, sbReader)\n}\n\nfunc fetchPackV1(\n\tctx context.Context,\n\tconn *Conn,\n\tadv *packp.AdvRefs,\n\tdesired map[plumbing.ReferenceName]DesiredRef,\n\ttargetRefs map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash,\n) (io.ReadCloser, error) {\n\tsession, err := conn.Transport.NewUploadPackSession(conn.Endpoint, conn.Auth)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"open source upload-pack session: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treq := packp.NewUploadPackRequestFromCapabilities(adv.Capabilities)\n\tfor _, ref := range desired {\n\t\treq.Wants = append(req.Wants, ref.SourceHash)\n\t}\n\treq.Wants = SortedUniqueHashes(req.Wants)\n\treq.Haves = SortedUniqueHashes(refValues(targetRefs))\n\tif len(req.Wants) == 0 {\n\t\t_ = session.Close()\n\t\treturn nil, git.NoErrAlreadyUpToDate\n\t}\n\tif adv.Capabilities.Supports(capability.NoProgress) {\n\t\t_ = req.Capabilities.Set(capability.NoProgress)\n\t}\n\tif hasTag(desired) && adv.Capabilities.Supports(capability.IncludeTag) {\n\t\t_ = req.Capabilities.Set(capability.IncludeTag)\n\t}\n\n\treader, err := session.UploadPack(ctx, req)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\t_ = session.Close()\n\t\tif errors.Is(err, transport.ErrEmptyUploadPackRequest) {\n\t\t\treturn nil, git.NoErrAlreadyUpToDate\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"source upload-pack: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn &sessionRC{\n\t\tReader: buildSidebandReader(req.Capabilities, reader, nil),\n\t\tcloseFn: func() error {\n\t\t\t_ = reader.Close()\n\t\t\treturn session.Close()\n\t\t},\n\t}, nil\n}\n\n// buildSidebandReader wraps a reader with sideband demuxing if the negotiated\n// capabilities include sideband support. Prefers sideband-64k (issue #4).\nfunc buildSidebandReader(caps *capability.List, reader io.Reader, progress sideband.Progress) io.Reader {\n\tvar t sideband.Type\n\tswitch {\n\tcase caps.Supports(capability.Sideband64k):\n\t\tt = sideband.Sideband64k\n\tcase caps.Supports(capability.Sideband):\n\t\tt = sideband.Sideband\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn reader\n\t}\n\td := sideband.NewDemuxer(t, reader)\n\td.Progress = progress\n\treturn d\n}\n\n// --- helpers ---\n\nfunc collectWants(desired map[plumbing.ReferenceName]DesiredRef) []plumbing.Hash {\n\thashes := make([]plumbing.Hash, 0, len(desired))\n\tfor _, ref := range desired {\n\t\thashes = append(hashes, ref.SourceHash)\n\t}\n\treturn SortedUniqueHashes(hashes)\n}\n\nfunc hasTag(desired map[plumbing.ReferenceName]DesiredRef) bool {\n\tfor _, ref := range desired {\n\t\tif ref.IsTag {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n\nfunc refValues(m map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash) []plumbing.Hash {\n\tout := make([]plumbing.Hash, 0, len(m))\n\tfor _, h := range m {\n\t\tif !h.IsZero() {\n\t\t\tout = append(out, h)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn out\n}\n\n// SortedUniqueHashes deduplicates and sorts a hash slice.\nfunc SortedUniqueHashes(input []plumbing.Hash) []plumbing.Hash {\n\tseen := make(map[plumbing.Hash]bool, len(input))\n\tout := make([]plumbing.Hash, 0, len(input))\n\tfor _, h := range input {\n\t\tif seen[h] {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tseen[h] = true\n\t\tout = append(out, h)\n\t}\n\tplumbing.HashesSort(out)\n\treturn out\n}\n\ntype wrappedRC struct {\n\tio.Reader\n\tio.Closer\n}\n\ntype sessionRC struct {\n\tio.Reader\n\tcloseFn func() error\n}\n\nfunc (r *sessionRC) Close() error {\n\tif r.closeFn == nil {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\treturn r.closeFn()\n}\n"
}

{

  "content": "package gitproto\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"io\"\n\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing\"\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/format/packfile\"\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/protocol/packp\"\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/protocol/packp/capability\"\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/storer\"\n)\n\n// PushCommand represents a single ref update command.\ntype PushCommand struct {\n\tName   plumbing.ReferenceName\n\tOld    plumbing.Hash\n\tNew    plumbing.Hash\n\tDelete bool\n}\n\n// PushObjects pushes locally-materialized objects to the target.\nfunc PushObjects(\n\tctx context.Context,\n\tconn *Conn,\n\tadv *packp.AdvRefs,\n\tcommands []PushCommand,\n\tstore storer.Storer,\n\thashes []plumbing.Hash,\n\tverbose bool,\n) error {\n\tsession, err := conn.Transport.NewReceivePackSession(conn.Endpoint, conn.Auth)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"open target receive-pack session: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tdefer session.Close()\n\n\treq := packp.NewReferenceUpdateRequestFromCapabilities(adv.Capabilities)\n\treq.Progress = progressWriter(verbose)\n\tif sb := PreferredSideband(adv.Capabilities); sb != \"\" {\n\t\t_ = req.Capabilities.Set(sb)\n\t}\n\n\thasDelete := false\n\thasUpdates := false\n\tfor _, cmd := range commands {\n\t\tc := &packp.Command{Name: cmd.Name, Old: cmd.Old}\n\t\tif cmd.Delete {\n\t\t\tc.New = plumbing.ZeroHash\n\t\t\thasDelete = true\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tc.New = cmd.New\n\t\t\thasUpdates = true\n\t\t}\n\t\treq.Commands = append(req.Commands, c)\n\t}\n\tif hasDelete {\n\t\tif !adv.Capabilities.Supports(capability.DeleteRefs) {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"target does not support delete-refs\")\n\t\t}\n\t\t_ = req.Capabilities.Set(capability.DeleteRefs)\n\t}\n\n\treturn executePush(ctx, session, store, req, hashes, hasUpdates, !adv.Capabilities.Supports(capability.OFSDelta))\n}\n\n// PushPack pushes a pack stream (relay) to the target.\nfunc PushPack(\n\tctx context.Context,\n\tconn *Conn,\n\tadv *packp.AdvRefs,\n\tcommands []PushCommand,\n\tpack io.ReadCloser,\n\tverbose bool,\n) error {\n\tsession, err := conn.Transport.NewReceivePackSession(conn.Endpoint, conn.Auth)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"open target receive-pack session: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tdefer session.Close()\n\n\treq := packp.NewReferenceUpdateRequestFromCapabilities(adv.Capabilities)\n\treq.Progress = progressWriter(verbose)\n\tif sb := PreferredSideband(adv.Capabilities); sb != \"\" {\n\t\t_ = req.Capabilities.Set(sb)\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, cmd := range commands {\n\t\tc := &packp.Command{Name: cmd.Name, Old: cmd.Old}\n\t\tif cmd.Delete {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"pack push only supports create and update actions\")\n\t\t}\n\t\tc.New = cmd.New\n\t\treq.Commands = append(req.Commands, c)\n\t}\n\n\treq.Packfile = pack\n\treport, err := session.ReceivePack(ctx, req)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\t_ = pack.Close()\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tif closeErr := pack.Close(); closeErr != nil {\n\t\treturn closeErr\n\t}\n\tif report != nil {\n\t\treturn report.Error()\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// PushCommands sends ref update commands without a pack (for ref-only changes).\nfunc PushCommands(\n\tctx context.Context,\n\tconn *Conn,\n\tadv *packp.AdvRefs,\n\tcommands []PushCommand,\n\tverbose bool,\n) error {\n\tsession, err := conn.Transport.NewReceivePackSession(conn.Endpoint, conn.Auth)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"open target receive-pack session: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tdefer session.Close()\n\n\treq := packp.NewReferenceUpdateRequestFromCapabilities(adv.Capabilities)\n\treq.Progress = progressWriter(verbose)\n\tif sb := PreferredSideband(adv.Capabilities); sb != \"\" {\n\t\t_ = req.Capabilities.Set(sb)\n\t}\n\n\thasDelete := false\n\tfor _, cmd := range commands {\n\t\tc := &packp.Command{Name: cmd.Name, Old: cmd.Old}\n\t\tif cmd.Delete {\n\t\t\tc.New = plumbing.ZeroHash\n\t\t\thasDelete = true\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tc.New = cmd.New\n\t\t}\n\t\treq.Commands = append(req.Commands, c)\n\t}\n\tif hasDelete {\n\t\tif !adv.Capabilities.Supports(capability.DeleteRefs) {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"target does not support delete-refs\")\n\t\t}\n\t\t_ = req.Capabilities.Set(capability.DeleteRefs)\n\t}\n\n\treturn executePush(ctx, session, nil, req, nil, false, false)\n}\n\nfunc executePush(\n\tctx context.Context,\n\tsession transport.ReceivePackSession,\n\tstore storer.Storer,\n\treq *packp.ReferenceUpdateRequest,\n\thashes []plumbing.Hash,\n\tsendPack bool,\n\tuseRefDeltas bool,\n) error {\n\tif !sendPack {\n\t\treport, err := session.ReceivePack(ctx, req)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t\tif report != nil {\n\t\t\treturn report.Error()\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\trd, wr := io.Pipe()\n\treq.Packfile = rd\n\tdone := make(chan error, 1)\n\n\tgo func() {\n\t\tenc := packfile.NewEncoder(wr, store, useRefDeltas)\n\t\tif _, err := enc.Encode(hashes, 10); err != nil {\n\t\t\tdone <- wr.CloseWithError(err)\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\tdone <- wr.Close()\n\t}()\n\n\treport, err := session.ReceivePack(ctx, req)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\t_ = rd.Close()\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tif err := <-done; err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tif report != nil {\n\t\treturn report.Error()\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// progressWriter returns os.Stderr if verbose, nil otherwise.\nfunc progressWriter(verbose bool) io.Writer {\n\tif !verbose {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\t// We import os here to return Stderr.\n\treturn nil // Callers that need verbose output should use their own writers.\n}\n"
}

{

  "content": "package planner\n\nimport (\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing\"\n)\n\n// RefKind distinguishes branch refs from tag refs.\ntype RefKind string\n\nconst (\n\tRefKindBranch RefKind = \"branch\"\n\tRefKindTag    RefKind = \"tag\"\n)\n\n// Action represents the planned operation on a ref.\ntype Action string\n\nconst (\n\tActionCreate Action = \"create\"\n\tActionUpdate Action = \"update\"\n\tActionDelete Action = \"delete\"\n\tActionSkip   Action = \"skip\"\n\tActionBlock  Action = \"block\"\n)\n\n// RefMapping is a user-specified source:target mapping.\ntype RefMapping struct {\n\tSource string\n\tTarget string\n}\n\n// DesiredRef represents a source ref that should be mirrored to a target ref.\ntype DesiredRef struct {\n\tKind       RefKind\n\tLabel      string\n\tSourceRef  plumbing.ReferenceName\n\tTargetRef  plumbing.ReferenceName\n\tSourceHash plumbing.Hash\n}\n\n// ManagedTarget tracks which target refs are managed by git-sync.\ntype ManagedTarget struct {\n\tKind  RefKind\n\tLabel string\n}\n\n// BranchPlan describes the planned action for a single ref.\ntype BranchPlan struct {\n\tBranch     string                 `json:\"branch\"`\n\tSourceRef  plumbing.ReferenceName `json:\"source_ref\"`\n\tTargetRef  plumbing.ReferenceName `json:\"target_ref\"`\n\tSourceHash plumbing.Hash          `json:\"source_hash\"`\n\tTargetHash plumbing.Hash          `json:\"target_hash\"`\n\tKind       RefKind                `json:\"kind\"`\n\tAction     Action                 `json:\"action\"`\n\tReason     string                 `json:\"reason\"`\n}\n\nfunc (p BranchPlan) MarshalJSON() ([]byte, error) {\n\ttype bp struct {\n\t\tBranch     string  `json:\"branch\"`\n\t\tSourceRef  string  `json:\"source_ref\"`\n\t\tTargetRef  string  `json:\"target_ref\"`\n\t\tSourceHash string  `json:\"source_hash\"`\n\t\tTargetHash string  `json:\"target_hash\"`\n\t\tKind       RefKind `json:\"kind\"`\n\t\tAction     Action  `json:\"action\"`\n\t\tReason     string  `json:\"reason\"`\n\t}\n\treturn json.Marshal(bp{\n\t\tBranch:     p.Branch,\n\t\tSourceRef:  p.SourceRef.String(),\n\t\tTargetRef:  p.TargetRef.String(),\n\t\tSourceHash: p.SourceHash.String(),\n\t\tTargetHash: p.TargetHash.String(),\n\t\tKind:       p.Kind,\n\t\tAction:     p.Action,\n\t\tReason:     p.Reason,\n\t})\n}\n\n// ShortHash returns the first 8 characters of a hash, or \"<zero>\" for zero hashes.\nfunc ShortHash(hash plumbing.Hash) string {\n\tif hash.IsZero() {\n\t\treturn \"<zero>\"\n\t}\n\ts := hash.String()\n\tif len(s) > 8 {\n\t\treturn s[:8]\n\t}\n\treturn s\n}\n\n// RefKindFromName infers the ref kind from a fully qualified ref name.\nfunc RefKindFromName(name plumbing.ReferenceName) RefKind {\n\tswitch {\n\tcase name.IsBranch():\n\t\treturn RefKindBranch\n\tcase name.IsTag():\n\t\treturn RefKindTag\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn \"\"\n\t}\n}\n\n// ActionForTargetHash returns ActionCreate for zero hashes, ActionUpdate otherwise.\nfunc ActionForTargetHash(hash plumbing.Hash) Action {\n\tif hash.IsZero() {\n\t\treturn ActionCreate\n\t}\n\treturn ActionUpdate\n}\n\n// BranchMapFromRefHashMap extracts short branch names from a ref hash map.\nfunc BranchMapFromRefHashMap(refs map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash) map[string]plumbing.Hash {\n\tbranches := make(map[string]plumbing.Hash)\n\tfor name, hash := range refs {\n\t\tif name.IsBranch() {\n\t\t\tbranches[name.Short()] = hash\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn branches\n}\n\n// SelectBranches filters branches to only the requested ones.\n// If requested is empty, returns all.\nfunc SelectBranches(source map[string]plumbing.Hash, requested []string) map[string]plumbing.Hash {\n\tif len(requested) == 0 {\n\t\treturn source\n\t}\n\tselected := make(map[string]plumbing.Hash, len(requested))\n\tfor _, branch := range requested {\n\t\tif hash, ok := source[branch]; ok {\n\t\t\tselected[branch] = hash\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn selected\n}\n\n// RefPrefixes computes the ref-prefix arguments for v2 ls-refs based on\n// the user's configuration.\nfunc RefPrefixes(mappings []RefMapping, includeTags bool) []string {\n\tprefixSet := map[string]struct{}{}\n\tif len(mappings) > 0 {\n\t\tfor _, m := range mappings {\n\t\t\tsrc := strings.TrimSpace(m.Source)\n\t\t\tif strings.HasPrefix(src, \"refs/tags/\") {\n\t\t\t\tprefixSet[\"refs/tags/\"] = struct{}{}\n\t\t\t} else if strings.HasPrefix(src, \"refs/heads/\") {\n\t\t\t\tprefixSet[\"refs/heads/\"] = struct{}{}\n\t\t\t} else if !strings.HasPrefix(src, \"refs/\") {\n\t\t\t\tprefixSet[\"refs/heads/\"] = struct{}{}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t} else {\n\t\tprefixSet[\"refs/heads/\"] = struct{}{}\n\t}\n\tif includeTags {\n\t\tprefixSet[\"refs/tags/\"] = struct{}{}\n\t}\n\tprefixes := make([]string, 0, len(prefixSet))\n\tfor p := range prefixSet {\n\t\tprefixes = append(prefixes, p)\n\t}\n\tsortStrings(prefixes)\n\treturn prefixes\n}\n\nfunc sortStrings(s []string) {\n\tfor i := 1; i < len(s); i++ {\n\t\tfor j := i; j > 0 && s[j] < s[j-1]; j-- {\n\t\t\ts[j], s[j-1] = s[j-1], s[j]\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\n// CopyRefHashMap returns a shallow copy of a ref hash map.\nfunc CopyRefHashMap(input map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash) map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash {\n\tout := make(map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash, len(input))\n\tfor k, v := range input {\n\t\tout[k] = v\n\t}\n\treturn out\n}\n\n// DesiredSubset returns the subset of desired refs that match the given plans.\nfunc DesiredSubset(\n\tdesired map[plumbing.ReferenceName]DesiredRef,\n\tplans []BranchPlan,\n) map[plumbing.ReferenceName]DesiredRef {\n\tout := make(map[plumbing.ReferenceName]DesiredRef, len(plans))\n\tfor _, plan := range plans {\n\t\tif ref, ok := desired[plan.TargetRef]; ok {\n\t\t\tout[plan.TargetRef] = ref\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn out\n}\n\n// SingleDesired builds a single-entry desired ref map.\nfunc SingleDesired(sourceRef, targetRef plumbing.ReferenceName, hash plumbing.Hash) map[plumbing.ReferenceName]DesiredRef {\n\treturn map[plumbing.ReferenceName]DesiredRef{\n\t\ttargetRef: {\n\t\t\tKind:       RefKindBranch,\n\t\t\tLabel:      targetRef.Short(),\n\t\t\tSourceRef:  sourceRef,\n\t\t\tTargetRef:  targetRef,\n\t\t\tSourceHash: hash,\n\t\t},\n\t}\n}\n\n// SingleHaveMap builds a single-entry have map for fetch negotiation.\nfunc SingleHaveMap(hash plumbing.Hash) map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash {\n\tif hash.IsZero() {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\treturn map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash{\n\t\tplumbing.ReferenceName(\"refs/gitsync/have\"): hash,\n\t}\n}\n\n// BootstrapTempRef returns the temporary ref name used during batched bootstrap.\nfunc BootstrapTempRef(targetRef plumbing.ReferenceName) plumbing.ReferenceName {\n\treturn plumbing.ReferenceName(\"refs/gitsync/bootstrap/heads/\" + targetRef.Short())\n}\n\n// FormatPlanLine formats a single plan entry for human-readable output.\nfunc FormatPlanLine(plan BranchPlan) string {\n\tlabel := plan.Branch\n\tif plan.TargetRef != \"\" {\n\t\tlabel = plan.TargetRef.String()\n\t}\n\tline := fmt.Sprintf(\"%s %s\", strings.ToUpper(string(plan.Action)), label)\n\tif plan.Reason != \"\" {\n\t\tline += \" - \" + plan.Reason\n\t}\n\treturn line\n}\n"
}

{

  "content": "package planner\n\nimport (\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing\"\n)\n\n// NormalizeMapping validates and normalizes a single ref mapping.\n// It rejects branch-to-tag and tag-to-branch cross-kind mappings (issue #3).\nfunc NormalizeMapping(m RefMapping) (plumbing.ReferenceName, plumbing.ReferenceName, RefKind, error) {\n\tsrc := strings.TrimSpace(m.Source)\n\tdst := strings.TrimSpace(m.Target)\n\tif src == \"\" || dst == \"\" {\n\t\treturn \"\", \"\", \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"invalid mapping %q:%q: source and target must be non-empty\", m.Source, m.Target)\n\t}\n\n\tsrcFQ := strings.HasPrefix(src, \"refs/\")\n\tdstFQ := strings.HasPrefix(dst, \"refs/\")\n\n\t// Both fully qualified\n\tif srcFQ && dstFQ {\n\t\tsourceRef := plumbing.ReferenceName(src)\n\t\ttargetRef := plumbing.ReferenceName(dst)\n\t\tsrcKind := RefKindFromName(sourceRef)\n\t\tdstKind := RefKindFromName(targetRef)\n\t\tif srcKind == \"\" {\n\t\t\treturn \"\", \"\", \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"unsupported source ref kind: %s\", src)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif dstKind == \"\" {\n\t\t\treturn \"\", \"\", \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"unsupported target ref kind: %s\", dst)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif srcKind != dstKind {\n\t\t\treturn \"\", \"\", \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"cross-kind mapping not allowed: %s (%s) -> %s (%s)\", src, srcKind, dst, dstKind)\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn sourceRef, targetRef, dstKind, nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Both short names -> branch mapping\n\tif !srcFQ && !dstFQ {\n\t\treturn plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(src), plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(dst), RefKindBranch, nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Mixed: one FQ and one short -> reject as ambiguous (issue #3)\n\treturn \"\", \"\", \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"ambiguous mapping: cannot mix fully-qualified and short ref names: %q -> %q\", src, dst)\n}\n\n// ValidateMappings normalizes all mappings and checks for duplicate targets (issue #2).\n// Validation happens before any network activity.\nfunc ValidateMappings(mappings []RefMapping) ([]NormalizedMapping, error) {\n\tif len(mappings) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil, nil\n\t}\n\n\tnormalized := make([]NormalizedMapping, 0, len(mappings))\n\ttargetSeen := make(map[plumbing.ReferenceName]string, len(mappings))\n\n\tfor _, m := range mappings {\n\t\tsrcRef, dstRef, kind, err := NormalizeMapping(m)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Check for duplicate target refs (issue #2)\n\t\tif prev, exists := targetSeen[dstRef]; exists {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"duplicate target ref %s: mapped from both %q and %q\", dstRef, prev, m.Source)\n\t\t}\n\t\ttargetSeen[dstRef] = m.Source\n\n\t\tnormalized = append(normalized, NormalizedMapping{\n\t\t\tSourceRef: srcRef,\n\t\t\tTargetRef: dstRef,\n\t\t\tKind:      kind,\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\treturn normalized, nil\n}\n\n// NormalizedMapping is a validated and normalized ref mapping.\ntype NormalizedMapping struct {\n\tSourceRef plumbing.ReferenceName\n\tTargetRef plumbing.ReferenceName\n\tKind      RefKind\n}\n"
}

All mapping validation happens here,\n// before any network activity (issue #2, #3).\nfunc BuildDesiredRefs(\n\tsourceRefs map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash,\n\tcfg PlanConfig,\n) (map[plumbing.ReferenceName]DesiredRef, map[plumbing.ReferenceName]ManagedTarget, error) {\n\tdesired := make(map[plumbing.ReferenceName]DesiredRef)\n\tmanaged := make(map[plumbing.ReferenceName]ManagedTarget)\n\n\taddManaged := func(sourceRef, targetRef plumbing.ReferenceName, kind RefKind, hash plumbing.Hash) error {\n\t\tif hash.IsZero() {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"source ref %s not found\", sourceRef)\n\t\t}\n\t\tshort := targetRef.Short()\n\t\tdesired[targetRef] = DesiredRef{\n\t\t\tKind:       kind,\n\t\t\tLabel:      short,\n\t\t\tSourceRef:  sourceRef,\n\t\t\tTargetRef:  targetRef,\n\t\t\tSourceHash: hash,\n\t\t}\n\t\tmanaged[targetRef] = ManagedTarget{Kind: kind, Label: short}\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\tif len(cfg.Mappings) > 0 {\n\t\t// Validate all mappings up front (issue #2, #3)\n\t\tnormalized, err := ValidateMappings(cfg.Mappings)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor _, nm := range normalized {\n\t\t\tif err := addManaged(nm.SourceRef, nm.TargetRef, nm.Kind, sourceRefs[nm.SourceRef]); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, nil, err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t} else {\n\t\tbranches := BranchMapFromRefHashMap(sourceRefs)\n\t\tselected := SelectBranches(branches, cfg.Branches)\n\t\tfor branch, hash := range selected {\n\t\t\trefName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(branch)\n\t\t\tif err := addManaged(refName, refName, RefKindBranch, hash); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, nil, err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tif cfg.IncludeTags {\n\t\tfor refName, hash := range sourceRefs {\n\t\t\tif !refName.IsTag() {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif err := addManaged(refName, refName, RefKindTag, hash); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, nil, err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn desired, managed, nil\n}\n\n// BuildPlans generates the action plans for each managed ref.\nfunc BuildPlans(\n\tstore storer.EncodedObjectStorer,\n\tdesired map[plumbing.ReferenceName]DesiredRef,\n\ttargetRefs map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash,\n\tmanaged map[plumbing.ReferenceName]ManagedTarget,\n\tcfg PlanConfig,\n) ([]BranchPlan, error) {\n\tif cfg.Prune {\n\t\tfor targetRef := range targetRefs {\n\t\t\tif _, ok := managed[targetRef]; ok {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tswitch {\n\t\t\tcase targetRef.IsTag() && cfg.IncludeTags:\n\t\t\t\tmanaged[targetRef] = ManagedTarget{Kind: RefKindTag, Label: targetRef.Short()}\n\t\t\tcase targetRef.IsBranch() && len(cfg.Mappings) == 0 && len(cfg.Branches) == 0:\n\t\t\t\tmanaged[targetRef] = ManagedTarget{Kind: RefKindBranch, Label: targetRef.Short()}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\ttargetNames := make([]plumbing.ReferenceName, 0, len(managed))\n\tfor name := range managed {\n\t\ttargetNames = append(targetNames, name)\n\t}\n\tsort.Slice(targetNames, func(i, j int) bool { return targetNames[i] < targetNames[j] })\n\n\tplans := make([]BranchPlan, 0, len(targetNames))\n\tfor _, targetRef := range targetNames {\n\t\tinfo := managed[targetRef]\n\t\twant, existsInDesired := desired[targetRef]\n\t\ttargetHash, existsOnTarget := targetRefs[targetRef]\n\n\t\tif !existsInDesired {\n\t\t\tif cfg.Prune && existsOnTarget {\n\t\t\t\tplans = append(plans, BranchPlan{\n\t\t\t\t\tBranch:     info.Label,\n\t\t\t\t\tTargetRef:  targetRef,\n\t\t\t\t\tTargetHash: targetHash,\n\t\t\t\t\tKind:       info.Kind,\n\t\t\t\t\tAction:     ActionDelete,\n\t\t\t\t\tReason:     fmt.Sprintf(\"%s -> <deleted>\", ShortHash(targetHash)),\n\t\t\t\t})\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif !existsOnTarget {\n\t\t\tplans = append(plans, BranchPlan{\n\t\t\t\tBranch:     want.Label,\n\t\t\t\tSourceRef:  want.SourceRef,\n\t\t\t\tTargetRef:  want.TargetRef,\n\t\t\t\tSourceHash: want.SourceHash,\n\t\t\t\tKind:       want.Kind,\n\t\t\t\tAction:     ActionCreate,\n\t\t\t\tReason:     fmt.Sprintf(\"%s -> <new>\", ShortHash(want.SourceHash)),\n\t\t\t})\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tplan, err := PlanRef(store, want, targetHash, cfg.Force)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tplans = append(plans, plan)\n\t}\n\n\tsort.Slice(plans, func(i, j int) bool {\n\t\treturn plans[i].TargetRef.String() < plans[j].TargetRef.String()\n\t})\n\treturn plans, nil\n}\n\n// BuildBootstrapPlans creates plans for an empty-target bootstrap.\nfunc BuildBootstrapPlans(\n\tdesired map[plumbing.ReferenceName]DesiredRef,\n\ttargetRefs map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash,\n) ([]BranchPlan, error) {\n\ttargetNames := make([]plumbing.ReferenceName, 0, len(desired))\n\tfor _, want := range desired {\n\t\ttargetNames = append(targetNames, want.TargetRef)\n\t}\n\tsort.Slice(targetNames, func(i, j int) bool { return targetNames[i] < targetNames[j] })\n\n\tplans := make([]BranchPlan, 0, len(targetNames))\n\tfor _, targetRef := range targetNames {\n\t\ttargetHash := targetRefs[targetRef]\n\t\tif !targetHash.IsZero() {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"target ref %s already exists; use sync for non-bootstrap runs\", targetRef)\n\t\t}\n\t\twant := desired[targetRef]\n\t\tplans = append(plans, BranchPlan{\n\t\t\tBranch:     want.Label,\n\t\t\tSourceRef:  want.SourceRef,\n\t\t\tTargetRef:  want.TargetRef,\n\t\t\tSourceHash: want.SourceHash,\n\t\t\tTargetHash: plumbing.ZeroHash,\n\t\t\tKind:       want.Kind,\n\t\t\tAction:     ActionCreate,\n\t\t\tReason:     fmt.Sprintf(\"create %s at %s\", want.TargetRef, ShortHash(want.SourceHash)),\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\treturn plans, nil\n}\n\n// PlanRef determines the action for a single ref that exists on both source and target.\nfunc PlanRef(store storer.EncodedObjectStorer, want DesiredRef, targetHash plumbing.Hash, force bool) (BranchPlan, error) {\n\tplan := BranchPlan{\n\t\tBranch:     want.Label,\n\t\tSourceRef:  want.SourceRef,\n\t\tTargetRef:  want.TargetRef,\n\t\tSourceHash: want.SourceHash,\n\t\tTargetHash: targetHash,\n\t\tKind:       want.Kind,\n\t}\n\n\tif want.SourceHash == targetHash {\n\t\tplan.Action = ActionSkip\n\t\tplan.Reason = fmt.Sprintf(\"%s already current\", ShortHash(want.SourceHash))\n\t\treturn plan, nil\n\t}\n\n\tif want.Kind == RefKindTag {\n\t\tif force {\n\t\t\tplan.Action = ActionUpdate\n\t\t\tplan.Reason = fmt.Sprintf(\"%s -> %s (force tag update)\", ShortHash(targetHash), ShortHash(want.SourceHash))\n\t\t\treturn plan, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\tplan.Action = ActionBlock\n\t\tplan.Reason = fmt.Sprintf(\"%s differs from %s; use --force to retarget tag\", ShortHash(targetHash), ShortHash(want.SourceHash))\n\t\treturn plan, nil\n\t}\n\n\tisFF, err := ReachesCommit(store, want.SourceHash, targetHash)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn plan, fmt.Errorf(\"check fast-forward for %s: %w\", want.TargetRef, err)\n\t}\n\tif isFF {\n\t\tplan.Action = ActionUpdate\n\t\tplan.Reason = fmt.Sprintf(\"%s -> %s\", ShortHash(targetHash), ShortHash(want.SourceHash))\n\t\treturn plan, nil\n\t}\n\n\tif force {\n\t\tplan.Action = ActionUpdate\n\t\tplan.Reason = fmt.Sprintf(\"%s -> %s (force)\", ShortHash(targetHash), ShortHash(want.SourceHash))\n\t\treturn plan, nil\n\t}\n\n\tplan.Action = ActionBlock\n\tplan.Reason = fmt.Sprintf(\"%s is not an ancestor of %s\", ShortHash(targetHash), ShortHash(want.SourceHash))\n\treturn plan, nil\n}\n\n// ReachesCommit checks whether the commit at startHash has targetHash as an ancestor.\nfunc ReachesCommit(store storer.EncodedObjectStorer, startHash, targetHash plumbing.Hash) (bool, error) {\n\tif startHash == targetHash {\n\t\treturn true, nil\n\t}\n\n\tstart, err := object.GetCommit(store, startHash)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false, fmt.Errorf(\"load source commit %s: %w\", startHash, err)\n\t}\n\n\tseen := map[plumbing.Hash]bool{}\n\tstack := []*object.Commit{start}\n\n\tfor len(stack) > 0 {\n\t\tcurrent := stack[len(stack)-1]\n\t\tstack = stack[:len(stack)-1]\n\t\tif seen[current.Hash] {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tseen[current.Hash] = true\n\n\t\tfor _, parentHash := range current.ParentHashes {\n\t\t\tif parentHash == targetHash {\n\t\t\t\treturn true, nil\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif seen[parentHash] {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tparent, err := object.GetCommit(store, parentHash)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tif errors.Is(err, plumbing.ErrObjectNotFound) {\n\t\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\treturn false, err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tstack = append(stack, parent)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false, nil\n}\n\n// ObjectsToPush computes the set of objects that need to be sent to the target.\nfunc ObjectsToPush(store storer.EncodedObjectStorer, wants []plumbing.Hash, targetRefs map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash) ([]plumbing.Hash, error) {\n\thaveSet := make(map[plumbing.Hash]struct{})\n\tfor _, h := range targetRefs {\n\t\tif !h.IsZero() {\n\t\t\thaveSet[h] = struct{}{}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tfilteredWants := make([]plumbing.Hash, 0, len(wants))\n\tfor _, h := range wants {\n\t\tif _, ok := haveSet[h]; !ok {\n\t\t\tfilteredWants = append(filteredWants, h)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif len(filteredWants) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil, nil\n\t}\n\n\tseen := make(map[plumbing.Hash]bool, len(filteredWants)*4)\n\tobjects := make([]plumbing.Hash, 0, len(filteredWants)*16)\n\tfor _, h := range filteredWants {\n\t\tif err := collectObjects(store, h, haveSet, seen, &objects); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn objects, nil\n}\n\nfunc collectObjects(\n\tstore storer.EncodedObjectStorer,\n\thash plumbing.Hash,\n\thaves map[plumbing.Hash]struct{},\n\tseen map[plumbing.Hash]bool,\n\tout *[]plumbing.Hash,\n) error {\n\tif hash.IsZero() {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\tif _, ok := haves[hash]; ok {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\tif seen[hash] {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\tseen[hash] = true\n\n\tobj, err := store.EncodedObject(plumbing.AnyObject, hash)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"load object %s: %w\", hash, err)\n\t}\n\n\tswitch obj.Type() {\n\tcase plumbing.CommitObject:\n\t\tcommit, err := object.GetCommit(store, hash)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"load commit %s: %w\", hash, err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err := collectObjects(store, commit.TreeHash, haves, seen, out); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor _, ph := range commit.ParentHashes {\n\t\t\tif err := collectObjects(store, ph, haves, seen, out); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\tcase plumbing.TreeObject:\n\t\ttree, err := object.GetTree(store, hash)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"load tree %s: %w\", hash, err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor _, entry := range tree.Entries {\n\t\t\tif err := collectObjects(store, entry.Hash, haves, seen, out); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\tcase plumbing.TagObject:\n\t\ttag, err := object.GetTag(store, hash)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"load tag %s: %w\", hash, err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err := collectObjects(store, tag.Target, haves, seen, out); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\tcase plumbing.BlobObject:\n\t\t// leaf object\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"unsupported object type %s for %s\", obj.Type(), hash)\n\t}\n\n\t*out = append(*out, hash)\n\treturn nil\n}\n"
}

auth != nil {\n\t\treturn auth, nil\n\t}\n\tif ep == nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil\n\t}\n\tif ep.Protocol != \"http\" && ep.Protocol != \"https\" {\n\t\treturn nil, nil\n\t}\n\tif username, password, ok := LookupEntireDBCredential(raw, ep); ok {\n\t\treturn &transporthttp.BasicAuth{Username: username, Password: password}, nil\n\t}\n\tif username, password, ok := lookupGitCredential(ep); ok {\n\t\treturn &transporthttp.BasicAuth{Username: username, Password: password}, nil\n\t}\n\treturn nil, nil\n}\n\nfunc explicitAuth(raw Endpoint) transport.AuthMethod {\n\tif raw.BearerToken != \"\" {\n\t\treturn &transporthttp.TokenAuth{Token: raw.BearerToken}\n\t}\n\tif raw.Token != \"\" {\n\t\tusername := raw.Username\n\t\tif username == \"\" {\n\t\t\tusername = \"git\"\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn &transporthttp.BasicAuth{Username: username, Password: raw.Token}\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// GitCredentialFillCommand is replaceable for testing.\nvar GitCredentialFillCommand = func(ctx context.Context, input string) ([]byte, error) {\n\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, \"git\", \"credential\", \"fill\")\n\tcmd.Stdin = strings.NewReader(input)\n\treturn cmd.Output()\n}\n\nfunc lookupGitCredential(ep *transport.Endpoint) (string, string, bool) {\n\tinput := credentialFillInput(ep)\n\tif input == \"\" {\n\t\treturn \"\", \"\", false\n\t}\n\toutput, err := GitCredentialFillCommand(context.Background(), input)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", \"\", false\n\t}\n\tvalues := parseCredentialOutput(output)\n\tpassword := values[\"password\"]\n\tif password == \"\" {\n\t\treturn \"\", \"\", false\n\t}\n\tusername := values[\"username\"]\n\tif username == \"\" {\n\t\tif ep.User != \"\" {\n\t\t\tusername = ep.User\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tusername = \"git\"\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn username, password, true\n}\n\nfunc credentialFillInput(ep *transport.Endpoint) string {\n\tif ep == nil || ep.Host == \"\" {\n\t\treturn \"\"\n\t}\n\tvar b strings.Builder\n\tfmt.Fprintf(&b, \"protocol=%s\\nhost=%s\\n\", ep.Protocol, ep.Host)\n\tif path := strings.TrimPrefix(ep.Path, \"/\"); path != \"\" {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(&b, \"path=%s\\n\", path)\n\t}\n\tif ep.User != \"\" {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(&b, \"username=%s\\n\", ep.User)\n\t}\n\tb.WriteString(\"\\n\")\n\treturn b.String()\n}\n\nfunc parseCredentialOutput(output []byte) map[string]string {\n\tvalues := map[string]string{}\n\tfor _, line := range strings.Split(string(output), \"\\n\") {\n\t\tline = strings.TrimSpace(line)\n\t\tif line == \"\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tk, v, ok := strings.Cut(line, \"=\")\n\t\tif ok {\n\t\t\tvalues[k] = v\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn values\n}\n"
}

{\n\tAccessToken  string `json:\"access_token\"`\n\tRefreshToken string `json:\"refresh_token\"`\n\tExpiresIn    int64  `json:\"expires_in\"`\n}\n\n// LookupEntireDBCredential looks up credentials false\n\t}\n\tcredHost := endpointCredentialHost(ep)\n\ttoken, ok := lookupEntireDBToken(credHost, endpointBaseURL(ep), raw.SkipTLSVerify)\n\tif !ok || token == \"\" {\n\t\treturn \"\", \"\", false\n\t}\n\tusername := raw.Username\n\tif username == \"\" {\n\t\tusername = \"git\"\n\t}\n\treturn username, token, true\n}\n\nfunc endpointBaseURL(ep *transport.Endpoint) string {\n\tif ep == nil || ep.Host == \"\" {\n\t\treturn \"\"\n\t}\n\tscheme := ep.Protocol\n\tif scheme == \"\" {\n\t\tscheme = \"https\"\n\t}\n\thost := ep.Host\n\tif ep.Port > 0 {\n\t\thost = fmt.Sprintf(\"%s:%d\", host, fmt.Sprintf(\"%s:%d\", ep.Host, ep.Port)\n\t}\n\treturn ep.Host\n}\n\nfunc lookupEntireDBToken(host, baseURL string, skipTLS bool) (string, bool) {\n\tconfigDir := os.Getenv(\"ENTIRE_CONFIG_DIR\")\n\tif configDir == \"\" {\n\t\thome, err := os.UserHomeDir()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn \"\", false\n\t\t}\n\t\tconfigDir = filepath.Join(home, \".config\", \"entire\")\n\t}\n\n\tusername, ok := loadEntireDBActiveUser(host, configDir)\n\tif !ok || username == \"\" {\n\t\treturn \"\", false\n\t}\n\ttoken, err := getTokenWithRefresh(context.Background(), host, username, baseURL, skipTLS)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", false\n\t}\n\treturn token, token != \"\"\n}\n\nfunc loadEntireDBActiveUser(host, configDir string) (string, bool) {\n\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(configDir, \"hosts.json\"))\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", false\n\t}\n\tvar hosts map[string]*entireAuthHostInfo\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(data, &hosts); err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", false\n\t}\n\tinfo := hosts[host]\n\tif info == nil || info.ActiveUser == \"\" {\n\t\treturn \"\", false\n\t}\n\treturn info.ActiveUser, true\n}\n\n// getTokenWithRefresh retrieves a token, refreshing it if expired.\n// On refresh failure, returns the stale token with a nil error rather than\n// propagating the refresh error silently (issue #7).\nfunc getTokenWithRefresh(ctx context.Context, host, username, baseURL string, skipTLS bool) (string, error) {\n\tencoded, err := ReadStoredToken(credentialService(host), username)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", err\n\t}\n\ttoken, expiresAt := decodeTokenWithExpiration(encoded)\n\tif token == \"\" {\n\t\treturn \"\", nil\n\t}\n\tif !tokenExpiredOrExpiring(expiresAt) {\n\t\treturn token, nil\n\t}\n\trefreshed, err := refreshAccessToken(ctx, host, username, baseURL, skipTLS)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\t// Issue #7: surface refresh failure explicitly instead of silently reusing stale token.\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"token expired and refresh failed for %s@%s: %w\", username, host, err)\n\t}\n\treturn refreshed, nil\n}\n\nfunc decodeTokenWithExpiration(encoded string) (string, time.Time) {\n\tidx := strings.LastIndex(encoded, \"|\")\n\tif idx == -1 {\n\t\treturn encoded, time.Time{}\n\t}\n\ttoken := encoded[:idx]\n\tts, err := strconv.ParseInt(encoded[idx+1:], 10, 64)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn encoded, time.Time{}\n\t}\n\treturn token, time.Unix(ts, 0)\n}\n\nfunc tokenExpiredOrExpiring(expiresAt time.Time) bool {\n\tif expiresAt.IsZero() {\n\t\treturn true\n\t}\n\treturn time.Now().Add(5 * time.Minute).After(expiresAt)\n}\n\nfunc refreshAccessToken(ctx context.Context, host, username, baseURL string, skipTLS bool) (string, error) {\n\trefreshToken, err := ReadStoredToken(credentialService(host)+\":refresh\", username)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", err\n\t}\n\tif refreshToken == \"\" || baseURL == \"\" {\n\t\treturn \"\", errors.New(\"missing refresh token or base url\")\n\t}\n\n\tform := url.Values{}\n\tform.Set(\"grant_type\", \"refresh_token\")\n\tform.Set(\"refresh_token\", refreshToken)\n\tform.Set(\"client_id\", entireCLIClientID)\n\n\treq, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodPost, strings.TrimRight(baseURL, \"/\")+\"/oauth/token\", strings.NewReader(form.Encode()))\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", err\n\t}\n\treq.Header.Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/x-www-form-urlencoded\")\n\n\tclient := &http.Client{\n\t\tTimeout: 30 * time.Second,\n\t\tTransport: &http.Transport{\n\t\t\tTLSClientConfig: &tls.Config{InsecureSkipVerify: skipTLS}, //nolint:gosec\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\tresp, err := client.Do(req)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", err\n\t}\n\tdefer resp.Body.Close()\n\tif resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"refresh failed with status %d\", resp.StatusCode)\n\t}\n\n\tvar tokenResp oauthTokenResponse\n\tif err := json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(&tokenResp); err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", err\n\t}\n\tif tokenResp.AccessToken == \"\" {\n\t\treturn \"\", errors.New(\"empty access token in refresh response\")\n\t}\n\n\tif err := WriteStoredToken(\n\t\tcredentialService(host),\n\t\tusername,\n\t\tencodeTokenWithExpiration(tokenResp.AccessToken, tokenResp.ExpiresIn),\n\t); err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", err\n\t}\n\tif tokenResp.RefreshToken != \"\" {\n\t\t_ = WriteStoredToken(credentialService(host)+\":refresh\", username, tokenResp.RefreshToken)\n\t}\n\treturn tokenResp.AccessToken, nil\n}\n\nfunc encodeTokenWithExpiration(token string, expiresIn int64) string {\n\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%s|%d\", token, time.Now().Unix()+expiresIn)\n}\n\nfunc credentialService(host string) string {\n\treturn \"entire:\" + host\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/auth/tokenstore.go",
  "content": "package auth\n\nimport (\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"path/filepath\"\n\n\t\"github.com/zalando/go-keyring\"\n)\n\n// ReadStoredToken reads a token from the configured store (keyring or file).\nfunc ReadStoredToken(service, username string) (string, error) {\n\tif os.Getenv(\"ENTIRE_TOKEN_STORE\") == \"file\" {\n\t\treturn readFileToken(fileTokenPath(), service, username)\n\t}\n\treturn keyring.Get(service, username)\n}\n\n// WriteStoredToken writes a token to the configured store.\n// The file store uses atomic write with a lock (issue #10).\nfunc WriteStoredToken(service, username, password string) error {\n\tif os.Getenv(\"ENTIRE_TOKEN_STORE\") == \"file\" {\n\t\treturn writeFileToken(fileTokenPath(), service, username, password)\n\t}\n\treturn keyring.Set(service, username, password)\n}\n\nfunc fileTokenPath() string {\n\tpath := os.Getenv(\"ENTIRE_TOKEN_STORE_PATH\")\n\tif path != \"\" {\n\t\treturn path\n\t}\n\thome, err := os.UserHomeDir()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\"\n\t}\n\treturn filepath.Join(home, \".config\", \"entiredb\", \"tokens.json\")\n}\n\nfunc readFileToken(path, service, username string) (string, error) {\n\tif path == \"\" {\n\t\treturn \"\", keyring.ErrNotFound\n\t}\n\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(path)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tif os.IsNotExist(err) {\n\t\t\treturn \"\", keyring.ErrNotFound\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn \"\", err\n\t}\n\tvar store map[string]map[string]string\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(data, &store); err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", err\n\t}\n\tusers := store[service]\n\tif users == nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", keyring.ErrNotFound\n\t}\n\tpassword, ok := users[username]\n\tif !ok {\n\t\treturn \"\", keyring.ErrNotFound\n\t}\n\treturn password, nil\n}\n\n// writeFileToken writes a token to the file store.\n// Note: issue #10 identifies that this lacks locking. For now we scope\n// file store to single-process/dev usage and document that limitation.\nfunc writeFileToken(path, service, username, password string) error {\n\tif path == \"\" {\n\t\treturn os.ErrInvalid\n\t}\n\tstore := map[string]map[string]string{}\n\tif data, err := os.ReadFile(path); err == nil {\n\t\tif err := json.Unmarshal(data, &store); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t} else if !os.IsNotExist(err) {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tif store[service] == nil {\n\t\tstore[service] = map[string]string{}\n\t}\n\tstore[service][username] = password\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(path), 0o700); err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tdata, err := json.Marshal(store)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\treturn os.WriteFile(path, data, 0o600)\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/planner/checkpoint.go",
  "content": "package planner\n\nimport (\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"sort\"\n\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing\"\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/object\"\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/storer\"\n)\n\n// BootstrapBatch holds the checkpoint plan for a single branch during batched bootstrap.\ntype BootstrapBatch struct {\n\tPlan        BranchPlan\n\tTempRef     plumbing.ReferenceName\n\tResumeHash  plumbing.Hash\n\tCheckpoints []plumbing.Hash\n}\n\n// FirstParentChain walks the first-parent chain from tip back to root,\n// returning the chain in root-to-tip order.\nfunc FirstParentChain(store storer.EncodedObjectStorer, tip plumbing.Hash) ([]plumbing.Hash, error) {\n\tcommit, err := object.GetCommit(store, tip)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treversed := make([]plumbing.Hash, 0, 128)\n\tfor {\n\t\treversed = append(reversed, commit.Hash)\n\t\tif len(commit.ParentHashes) == 0 {\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\t\tcommit, err = object.GetCommit(store, commit.ParentHashes[0])\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tchain := make([]plumbing.Hash, 0, len(reversed))\n\tfor i := len(reversed) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {\n\t\tchain = append(chain, reversed[i])\n\t}\n\treturn chain, nil\n}\n\n// SampledCheckpointCandidates generates a set of candidate indices to probe,\n// sorted from largest (preferred) to smallest.\nfunc SampledCheckpointCandidates(lo, hi int, prevSpan int) []int {\n\tif lo > hi {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\tset := map[int]struct{}{}\n\tadd := func(idx int) {\n\t\tif idx < lo {\n\t\t\tidx = lo\n\t\t}\n\t\tif idx > hi {\n\t\t\tidx = hi\n\t\t}\n\t\tset[idx] = struct{}{}\n\t}\n\n\tprojected := hi\n\tif prevSpan > 0 {\n\t\tprojected = lo + prevSpan - 1\n\t}\n\tadd(projected)\n\n\tconst sampleCount = 4\n\tcurrent := projected\n\tfor i := 0; i < sampleCount-1; i++ {\n\t\tif current <= lo {\n\t\t\tadd(lo)\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tdistance := current - lo\n\t\tcurrent = lo + distance/2\n\t\tadd(current)\n\t}\n\tadd(lo)\n\n\tcandidates := make([]int, 0, len(set))\n\tfor idx := range set {\n\t\tcandidates = append(candidates, idx)\n\t}\n\tsort.Sort(sort.Reverse(sort.IntSlice(candidates)))\n\treturn candidates\n}\n\n// SampledCheckpointUnderLimit finds the largest checkpoint index that fits within\n// the batch limit, using a sampling strategy to reduce probes (issue #14).\nfunc SampledCheckpointUnderLimit(\n\tchain []plumbing.Hash,\n\tprevIdx int,\n\tprevSpan int,\n\tprobe func(idx int) (tooLarge bool, err error),\n) (int, error) {\n\tlo := prevIdx + 1\n\thi := len(chain) - 1\n\tif lo > hi {\n\t\treturn -1, nil\n\t}\n\n\tsamples := SampledCheckpointCandidates(lo, hi, prevSpan)\n\tbest := -1\n\tfor _, idx := range samples {\n\t\ttooLarge, err := probe(idx)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn -1, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tif tooLarge {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tbest = idx\n\t\tbreak\n\t}\n\tif best != -1 {\n\t\treturn best, nil\n\t}\n\n\tif prevSpan > 1 {\n\t\tshrunk := prevSpan / 2\n\t\tif shrunk < 1 {\n\t\t\tshrunk = 1\n\t\t}\n\t\tidx := lo + shrunk - 1\n\t\tif idx > hi {\n\t\t\tidx = hi\n\t\t}\n\t\tif idx >= lo {\n\t\t\ttooLarge, err := probe(idx)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn -1, err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif !tooLarge {\n\t\t\t\treturn idx, nil\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\ttooLarge, err := probe(lo)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn -1, err\n\t}\n\tif tooLarge {\n\t\treturn -1, nil\n\t}\n\treturn lo, nil\n}\n\n// BootstrapResumeIndex finds the starting index in a checkpoint list given a resume hash.\nfunc BootstrapResumeIndex(checkpoints []plumbing.Hash, resumeHash plumbing.Hash) (int, error) {\n\tif resumeHash.IsZero() {\n\t\treturn 0, nil\n\t}\n\tfor idx, checkpoint := range checkpoints {\n\t\tif checkpoint == resumeHash {\n\t\t\treturn idx + 1, nil\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn 0, fmt.Errorf(\"temp ref hash %s does not match any planned checkpoint\", resumeHash)\n}\n"
}

{

  "content": "package planner\n\nimport (\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing\"\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/protocol/packp\"\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/protocol/packp/capability\"\n)\n\n// CanBootstrapRelay checks whether all desired target refs are absent on the target,\n// making a bootstrap relay possible.\nfunc CanBootstrapRelay(\n\tforce, prune bool,\n\tdesired map[plumbing.ReferenceName]DesiredRef,\n\ttargetRefs map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash,\n) (bool, string) {\n\tif force || prune {\n\t\treturn false, \"bootstrap-disabled-by-force-or-prune\"\n\t}\n\tif len(desired) == 0 {\n\t\treturn false, \"bootstrap-no-managed-refs\"\n\t}\n\tfor targetRef := range desired {\n\t\tif !targetRefs[targetRef].IsZero() {\n\t\t\treturn false, \"bootstrap-target-ref-exists\"\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn true, \"empty-target-managed-refs\"\n}\n\n// CanIncrementalRelay checks whether all plans are eligible for the incremental\n// relay fast-path (fast-forward branch updates + new tag creates).\nfunc CanIncrementalRelay(force, prune, dryRun bool, plans []BranchPlan, targetAdv *packp.AdvRefs) (bool, string) {\n\tif force || prune || dryRun {\n\t\treturn false, \"incremental-disabled-by-force-prune-or-dry-run\"\n\t}\n\tif len(plans) == 0 {\n\t\treturn false, \"incremental-no-plans\"\n\t}\n\tif targetAdv == nil || targetAdv.Capabilities == nil {\n\t\treturn false, \"incremental-missing-target-capabilities\"\n\t}\n\tif targetAdv.Capabilities.Supports(capability.Capability(\"no-thin\")) {\n\t\treturn false, \"incremental-target-no-thin\"\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, plan := range plans {\n\t\tswitch plan.Kind {\n\t\tcase RefKindBranch:\n\t\t\tif !plan.SourceRef.IsBranch() || !plan.TargetRef.IsBranch() {\n\t\t\t\treturn false, \"incremental-non-branch-mapping\"\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif plan.Action != ActionUpdate {\n\t\t\t\treturn false, \"incremental-branch-action-not-update\"\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif plan.TargetHash.IsZero() {\n\t\t\t\treturn false, \"incremental-branch-target-missing\"\n\t\t\t}\n\t\tcase RefKindTag:\n\t\t\tif !plan.SourceRef.IsTag() || !plan.TargetRef.IsTag() {\n\t\t\t\treturn false, \"incremental-non-tag-mapping\"\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif plan.Action != ActionCreate {\n\t\t\t\treturn false, \"incremental-tag-action-not-create\"\n\t\t\t}\n\t\tdefault:\n\t\t\treturn false, \"incremental-unsupported-ref-kind\"\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn true, \"fast-forward-branch-or-tag-create\"\n}\n\n// CanFullTagCreateRelay checks whether all plans are tag creates, eligible for\n// a full-pack tag relay.\nfunc CanFullTagCreateRelay(plans []BranchPlan) (bool, string) {\n\tif len(plans) == 0 {\n\t\treturn false, \"incremental-no-plans\"\n\t}\n\tfor _, plan := range plans {\n\t\tif plan.Kind != RefKindTag {\n\t\t\treturn false, \"incremental-tag-relay-non-tag-plan\"\n\t\t}\n\t\tif !plan.SourceRef.IsTag() || !plan.TargetRef.IsTag() {\n\t\t\treturn false, \"incremental-tag-relay-non-tag-mapping\"\n\t\t}\n\t\tif plan.Action != ActionCreate {\n\t\t\treturn false, \"incremental-tag-relay-tag-action-not-create\"\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn true, \"tag-create-full-pack\"\n}\n\n// RelayFallbackReason returns the reason why relay was not used.\nfunc RelayFallbackReason(force, prune, dryRun bool, plans []BranchPlan, targetAdv *packp.AdvRefs) string {\n\tif ok, reason := CanIncrementalRelay(force, prune, dryRun, plans, targetAdv); ok {\n\t\treturn reason\n\t} else if ok, reason := CanFullTagCreateRelay(plans); ok {\n\t\treturn reason\n\t} else {\n\t\treturn reason\n\t}\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/syncer/stats.go",
  "content": "package syncer\n\nimport (\n\t\"io\"\n\t\"net/http\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\t\"sync\"\n)\n\n// ServiceStats tracks transfer statistics for a single service.\ntype ServiceStats struct {\n\tName          string `json:\"name\"`\n\tRequests      int    `json:\"requests\"`\n\tRequestBytes  int64  `json:\"request_bytes\"`\n\tResponseBytes int64  `json:\"response_bytes\"`\n\tWants         int    `json:\"wants\"`\n\tHaves         int    `json:\"haves\"`\n\tCommands      int    `json:\"commands\"`\n}\n\n// Stats holds the collected transfer statistics.\ntype Stats struct {\n\tEnabled bool                     `json:\"enabled\"`\n\tItems   map[string]*ServiceStats `json:\"items\"`\n}\n\n// statsCollector is a concurrency-safe stats collector (issue #8).\ntype statsCollector struct {\n\tenabled bool\n\tmu      sync.Mutex\n\titems   map[string]*ServiceStats\n}\n\nfunc newStats(enabled bool) *statsCollector {\n\treturn &statsCollector{enabled: enabled, items: map[string]*ServiceStats{}}\n}\n\nfunc (s *statsCollector) ensure(name string) *ServiceStats {\n\titem, ok := s.items[name]\n\tif !ok {\n\t\titem = &ServiceStats{Name: name}\n\t\ts.items[name] = item\n\t}\n\treturn item\n}\n\nfunc (s *statsCollector) addWantsHaves(name string, wants, haves int) {\n\tif !s.enabled {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\ts.mu.Lock()\n\tdefer s.mu.Unlock()\n\titem := s.ensure(name)\n\titem.Wants += wants\n\titem.Haves += haves\n}\n\nfunc (s *statsCollector) addCommands(name string, commands int) {\n\tif !s.enabled {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\ts.mu.Lock()\n\tdefer s.mu.Unlock()\n\titem := s.ensure(name)\n\titem.Commands += commands\n}\n\nfunc (s *statsCollector) recordRoundTrip(name string, requestBytes, responseBytes int64) {\n\tif !s.enabled {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\ts.mu.Lock()\n\tdefer s.mu.Unlock()\n\titem := s.ensure(name)\n\titem.Requests++\n\titem.RequestBytes += requestBytes\n\titem.ResponseBytes += responseBytes\n}\n\nfunc (s *statsCollector) snapshot() Stats {\n\ts.mu.Lock()\n\tdefer s.mu.Unlock()\n\tout := Stats{Enabled: s.enabled, Items: make(map[string]*ServiceStats, len(s.items))}\n\tfor key, item := range s.items {\n\t\tcopyItem := *item\n\t\tout.Items[key] = &copyItem\n\t}\n\treturn out\n}\n\nconst statsPhaseHeader = \"X-Git-Sync-Stats-Phase\"\n\n// countingRoundTripper wraps an HTTP transport to record transfer stats.\ntype countingRoundTripper struct {\n\tbase  http.RoundTripper\n\tlabel string\n\tstats *statsCollector\n}\n\nfunc (rt *countingRoundTripper) RoundTrip(req *http.Request) (*http.Response, error) {\n\tres, err := rt.base.RoundTrip(req)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\n\tserviceName := req.Header.Get(statsPhaseHeader)\n\tif serviceName == \"\" {\n\t\tserviceName = req.URL.Query().Get(\"service\")\n\t\tif serviceName == \"\" {\n\t\t\tserviceName = strings.TrimPrefix(req.URL.Path[strings.LastIndex(req.URL.Path, \"/\")+1:], \"/\")\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tname := strings.TrimSpace(rt.label + \" \" + serviceName)\n\trequestBytes := req.ContentLength\n\tif requestBytes < 0 {\n\t\trequestBytes = 0\n\t}\n\n\tres.Body = &countingReadCloser{\n\t\tReadCloser: res.Body,\n\t\tonClose: func(n int64) {\n\t\t\trt.stats.recordRoundTrip(name, requestBytes, n)\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\treturn res, nil\n}\n\ntype countingReadCloser struct {\n\tio.ReadCloser\n\tn       int64\n\tonClose func(int64)\n}\n\nfunc (c *countingReadCloser) Read(p []byte) (int, error) {\n\tn, err := c.ReadCloser.Read(p)\n\tc.n += int64(n)\n\treturn n, err\n}\n\nfunc (c *countingReadCloser) Close() error {\n\terr := c.ReadCloser.Close()\n\tif c.onClose != nil {\n\t\tc.onClose(c.n)\n\t\tc.onClose = nil\n\t}\n\treturn err\n}\n"
}

{
