  "content": "package syncer\n\nimport (\n\t\"runtime\"\n\t\"sync\"\n\t\"time\"\n)\n\n// Measurement holds performance measurement data.\ntype Measurement struct {\n\tEnabled            bool   `json:\"enabled\"`\n\tElapsedMillis      int64  `json:\"elapsed_millis\"`\n\tPeakAllocBytes     uint64 `json:\"peak_alloc_bytes\"`\n\tPeakHeapInuseBytes uint64 `json:\"peak_heap_inuse_bytes\"`\n\tTotalAllocBytes    uint64 `json:\"total_alloc_bytes\"`\n\tGCCount            uint32 `json:\"gc_count\"`\n}\n\nfunc startMeasurement(enabled bool) func() Measurement {\n\tif !enabled {\n\t\treturn func() Measurement { return Measurement{} }\n\t}\n\n\tstart := time.Now()\n\tvar startStats runtime.MemStats\n\truntime.ReadMemStats(&startStats)\n\n\tdone := make(chan struct{})\n\tvar (\n\t\tmu            sync.Mutex\n\t\tpeakAlloc     = startStats.Alloc\n\t\tpeakHeapInuse = startStats.HeapInuse\n\t\tresult        Measurement\n\t)\n\n\tgo func() {\n\t\tticker := time.NewTicker(10 * time.Millisecond)\n\t\tdefer ticker.Stop()\n\t\tfor {\n\t\t\tselect {\n\t\t\tcase <-done:\n\t\t\t\treturn\n\t\t\tcase <-ticker.C:\n\t\t\t\tvar current runtime.MemStats\n\t\t\t\truntime.ReadMemStats(&current)\n\t\t\t\tmu.Lock()\n\t\t\t\tif current.Alloc > peakAlloc {\n\t\t\t\t\tpeakAlloc = current.Alloc\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tif current.HeapInuse > peakHeapInuse {\n\t\t\t\t\tpeakHeapInuse = current.HeapInuse\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tmu.Unlock()\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}()\n\n\tvar once sync.Once\n\treturn func() Measurement {\n\t\tonce.Do(func() {\n\t\t\tclose(done)\n\t\t\tvar endStats runtime.MemStats\n\t\t\truntime.ReadMemStats(&endStats)\n\t\t\tmu.Lock()\n\t\t\tif endStats.Alloc > peakAlloc {\n\t\t\t\tpeakAlloc = endStats.Alloc\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif endStats.HeapInuse > peakHeapInuse {\n\t\t\t\tpeakHeapInuse = endStats.HeapInuse\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tresult = Measurement{\n\t\t\t\tEnabled:            true,\n\t\t\t\tElapsedMillis:      time.Since(start).Milliseconds(),\n\t\t\t\tPeakAllocBytes:     peakAlloc,\n\t\t\t\tPeakHeapInuseBytes: peakHeapInuse,\n\t\t\t\tTotalAllocBytes:    endStats.TotalAlloc - startStats.TotalAlloc,\n\t\t\t\tGCCount:            endStats.NumGC - startStats.NumGC,\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tmu.Unlock()\n\t\t})\n\t\treturn result\n\t}\n}\n"
}

transport: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\ttargetConn, err := newConn(cfg.Target, \"target\", stats)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn Result{}, fmt.Errorf(\"create target transport: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\trefPrefixes := planner.RefPrefixes(cfg.Mappings, cfg.IncludeTags)\n\tsourceRefs, sourceService, err := gitproto.ListSourceRefs(ctx, sourceConn, cfg.ProtocolMode, refPrefixes)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn Result{}, fmt.Errorf(\"list source refs: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\ttargetAdv, err := gitproto.AdvertisedRefsV1(ctx, targetConn, transport.ReceivePackServiceName)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn Result{}, fmt.Errorf(\"list target refs: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\ttargetRefSlice, err := gitproto.AdvRefsToSlice(targetAdv)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn Result{}, fmt.Errorf(\"decode target refs: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tsourceRefMap := gitproto.RefHashMap(sourceRefs)\n\ttargetRefMap := gitproto.RefHashMap(targetRefSlice)\n\n\tdesiredRefs, _, err := planner.BuildDesiredRefs(sourceRefMap, planner.CanBootstrapRelay(cfg.Force, cfg.Prune, desiredRefs, targetRefMap)\n\tresult, err := bootstrapWithInputs(ctx, cfg, stats, sourceConn, targetConn, sourceService, targetAdv, desiredRefs, targetRefMap, reason, measurementDone)\n\tresult.Measurement = measurementDone()\n\treturn result, err\n}\n\n// Probe inspects source and optionally target remotes.\nfunc Probe(ctx context.Context, cfg Config) (ProbeResult, error) {\n\tmeasurementDone := startMeasurement(cfg.MeasureMemory)\n\tif err := validateProtocol(&cfg); err != nil {\n\t\treturn ProbeResult{}, err\n\t}\n\tif cfg.Source.URL == \"\" {\n\t\treturn ProbeResult{}, fmt.Errorf(\"source repository URL is required\")\n\t}\n\n\tstats := newStats(cfg.ShowStats)\n\tsourceConn, err := newConn(cfg.Source, \"source\", stats)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn ProbeResult{}, fmt.Errorf(\"create source transport: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\trefPrefixes := planner.RefPrefixes(cfg.Mappings, cfg.IncludeTags)\n\trefs, service, err := gitproto.ListSourceRefs(ctx, sourceConn, cfg.ProtocolMode, refPrefixes)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn ProbeResult{}, fmt.Errorf(\"list source refs: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\trefInfos := make([]RefInfo, 0, len(refs))\n\tfor _, ref := range refs {\n\t\tif ref.Type() != plumbing.HashReference {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\trefInfos = append(refInfos, RefInfo{Name: ref.Name().String(), Hash: ref.Hash()})\n\t}\n\tsort.Slice(refInfos, func(i, j int) bool { return refInfos[i].Name < refInfos[j].Name })\n\n\tresult := ProbeResult{\n\t\tSourceURL:     cfg.Source.URL,\n\t\tRequestedMode: cfg.ProtocolMode,\n\t\tProtocol:      service.Protocol,\n\t\tRefPrefixes:   refPrefixes,\n\t\tCapabilities:  service.Capabilities(),\n\t\tRefs:          refInfos,\n\t\tStats:         stats.snapshot(),\n\t\tMeasurement:   measurementDone(),\n\t}\n\n\tif cfg.Target.URL != \"\" {\n\t\ttargetConn, err := newConn(cfg.Target, \"target\", stats)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn ProbeResult{}, fmt.Errorf(\"create target transport: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\ttargetAdv, err := gitproto.AdvertisedRefsV1(ctx, targetConn, transport.ReceivePackServiceName)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn ProbeResult{}, fmt.Errorf(\"list target refs: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tresult.TargetURL = cfg.Target.URL\n\t\tresult.TargetCaps = gitproto.AdvRefsCaps(targetAdv)\n\t\tresult.Stats = stats.snapshot()\n\t\tresult.Measurement = measurementDone()\n\t}\n\treturn result, nil\n}\n\n// Fetch exercises source-side fetch negotiation.\nfunc Fetch(ctx context.Context, cfg Config, haveRefs []string, haveHashes []plumbing.Hash) (FetchResult, error) {\n\tmeasurementDone := startMeasurement(cfg.MeasureMemory)\n\tif err := validateProtocol(&cfg); err != nil {\n\t\treturn FetchResult{}, err\n\t}\n\tif cfg.Source.URL == \"\" {\n\t\treturn FetchResult{}, fmt.Errorf(\"source repository URL is required\")\n\t}\n\n\trepo, err := git.Init(memory.NewStorage(), nil)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn FetchResult{}, fmt.Errorf(\"init in-memory repository: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tstats := newStats(cfg.ShowStats)\n\tsourceConn, err := newConn(cfg.Source, \"source\", stats)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn FetchResult{}, fmt.Errorf(\"create source transport: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\trefPrefixes := planner.RefPrefixes(cfg.Mappings, cfg.IncludeTags)\n\tsourceRefs, sourceService, err := gitproto.ListSourceRefs(ctx, sourceConn, cfg.ProtocolMode, refPrefixes)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn FetchResult{}, fmt.Errorf(\"list source refs: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tsourceRefMap := gitproto.RefHashMap(sourceRefs)\n\n\tdesiredRefs, _, err := planner.BuildDesiredRefs(sourceRefMap, planConfig(cfg))\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn FetchResult{}, err\n\t}\n\tif len(desiredRefs) == 0 {\n\t\treturn FetchResult{}, fmt.Errorf(\"no source refs matched\")\n\t}\n\n\ttargetRefMap := make(map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash)\n\tfor _, raw := range haveRefs {\n\t\tname := parseHaveRef(raw)\n\t\thash, ok := sourceRefMap[name]\n\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\treturn FetchResult{}, fmt.Errorf(\"have-ref %q not found on source\", raw)\n\t\t}\n\t\ttargetRefMap[name] = hash\n\t}\n\tfor idx, hash := range haveHashes {\n\t\ttargetRefMap[plumbing.ReferenceName(fmt.Sprintf(\"refs/haves/%d\", idx))] = hash\n\t}\n\n\tgpDesired := toGitprotoDesired(desiredRefs)\n\tif err := sourceService.FetchToStore(ctx, repo.Storer, sourceConn, gpDesired, targetRefMap); err != nil {\n\t\tif !errors.Is(err, git.NoErrAlreadyUpToDate) {\n\t\t\treturn FetchResult{}, err\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\twants := make([]RefInfo, 0, len(desiredRefs))\n\tfor _, ref := range desiredRefs {\n\t\twants = append(wants, RefInfo{Name: ref.SourceRef.String(), Hash: ref.SourceHash})\n\t}\n\tsort.Slice(wants, func(i, j int) bool { return wants[i].Name < wants[j].Name })\n\n\tobjectCount, err := countObjects(repo.Storer)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn FetchResult{}, fmt.Errorf(\"count fetched objects: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\thaveValues := make([]plumbing.Hash, 0, len(targetRefMap))\n\tfor _, h := range targetRefMap {\n\t\tif !h.IsZero() {\n\t\t\thaveValues = append(haveValues, h)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn FetchResult{\n\t\tSourceURL:      cfg.Source.URL,\n\t\tRequestedMode:  cfg.ProtocolMode,\n\t\tProtocol:       sourceService.Protocol,\n\t\tWants:          wants,\n\t\tHaves:          gitproto.SortedUniqueHashes(haveValues),\n\t\tFetchedObjects: objectCount,\n\t\tStats:          stats.snapshot(),\n\t\tMeasurement:    measurementDone(),\n\t}, nil\n}\n\n// --- Bootstrap implementation ---\n\nfunc bootstrapWithInputs(\n\tctx context.Context,\n\tcfg Config,\n\tstats *statsCollector,\n\tsourceConn, targetConn *gitproto.Conn,\n\tsourceService *gitproto.RefService,\n\ttargetAdv interface{},\n\tdesiredRefs map[plumbing.ReferenceName]planner.DesiredRef,\n\ttargetRefs map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash,\n\trelayReason string,\n\tmeasurementDone func() Measurement,\n) (Result, error) {\n\t// Import packp.AdvRefs from targetAdv\n\tadvRefs := targetAdv.(*packpAdvRefsAccessor).advRefs()\n\t_ = advRefs // used below\n\n\tplans, err := planner.BuildBootstrapPlans(desiredRefs, targetRefs)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn Result{}, err\n\t}\n\n\tresult := Result{\n\t\tPlans: plans, Relay: true, RelayMode: \"bootstrap\", RelayReason: relayReason,\n\t\tStats: stats.snapshot(), Protocol: sourceService.Protocol,\n\t}\n\n\t// GitHub large-repo preflight\n\tif batchLimit, ok := githubBootstrapBatchMaxPackBytes(ctx, cfg, sourceConn, sourceService); ok {\n\t\tcfg.BatchMaxPackBytes = batchLimit\n\t\tprogressf(cfg.Verbose, \"bootstrap: github repo-size preflight selected batched mode with batch-max-pack-bytes=%d\", cfg.BatchMaxPackBytes)\n\t}\n\n\tif cfg.BatchMaxPackBytes > 0 {\n\t\treturn bootstrapBatched(ctx, cfg, errors.Is(err, git.NoErrAlreadyUpToDate) {\n\t\t\treturn result, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn result, fmt.Errorf(\"fetch source pack: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tpackReader = limitPackReadCloser(packReader, cmds, packReader, cfg.Verbose)\n\tif pushErr != nil {\n\t\tautoBatch, ok := autoBatchMaxPackBytes(cfg, sourceService, pushErr)\n\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\treturn result, fmt.Errorf(\"push target refs: %w\", pushErr)\n\t\t}\n\t\tprogressf(cfg.Verbose, \"bootstrap: target rejected; retrying with batch-max-pack-bytes=%d\", autoBatch)\n\t\tcfg.BatchMaxPackBytes = autoBatch\n\t\treturn bootstrapBatched(ctx, cfg, stats, sourceConn, targetConn, sourceService, advRefs, plans, desiredRefs, targetRefs, result, measurementDone)\n\t}\n\n\tresult.Pushed = len(plans)\n\tresult.Stats = stats.snapshot()\n\treturn plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(raw)\n}\n\nfunc plansToPushCommands(plans []BranchPlan) []gitproto.PushCommand {\n\tcmds := make([]gitproto.PushCommand, 0, len(plans))\n\tfor _, plan := range plans {\n\t\tcmd := gitproto.PushCommand{Name: plan.TargetRef, Old: plan.TargetHash}\n\t\tswitch plan.Action {\n\t\tcase ActionCreate, ActionUpdate:\n\t\t\tcmd.New = plan.SourceHash\n\t\tcase ActionDelete:\n\t\t\tcmd.Delete = true\n\t\t}\n\t\tcmds = append(cmds, cmd)\n\t}\n\treturn cmds\n}\n\nfunc ensureTagObjects(\n\tctx context.Context,\n\trepo *git.Repository,\n\tsourceConn *gitproto.Conn,\n\tsourceService *gitproto.RefService,\n\tdesired map[plumbing.ReferenceName]planner.DesiredRef,\n\tplans []BranchPlan,\n) error {\n\ttagDesired := make(map[plumbing.ReferenceName]gitproto.DesiredRef)\n\tfor _, plan := range plans {\n\t\tif plan.Kind != RefKindTag {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif d, ok := desired[plan.TargetRef]; ok len(tagDesired) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\treturn sourceService.FetchToStore(ctx, repo.Storer, sourceConn, tagDesired, nil)\n}\n\nfunc countObjects(store interface {\n\tIterEncodedObjects(plumbing.ObjectType) (interface{ ForEach(func(plumbing.EncodedObject) error) error; Close() }, error)\n}) (int, error) {\n\t// We need the storer interface\n\treturn 0, maxBytes}\n}\n\ntype packLimitReadCloser struct {\n\tio.ReadCloser\n\tmaxBytes int64\n\tread     int64\n}\n\nfunc (r *packLimitReadCloser) Read(p []byte) (int, error) {\n\tn, err := ||\n\t\t(strings.Contains(msg, \"request body\") && strings.Contains(msg, \"too large\")) ||\n\t\t(strings.Contains(msg, \"payload\") && strings.Contains(msg, \"too large\")) {\n\t\tderived := targetLimit / 2\n\t\tif derived <= 0 {\n\t\t\tderived = targetLimit\n\t\t}\n\t\tif derived < batchLimit {\n\t\t\tbatchLimit = derived\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif cfg.MaxPackBytes > 0 && cfg.MaxPackBytes < batchLimit {\n\t\tbatchLimit = cfg.MaxPackBytes\n\t}\n\tif batchLimit <= 0 {\n\t\treturn 0, false\n\t}\n\treturn batchLimit, true\n}\n\nfunc githubBootstrapBatchMaxPackBytes(ctx <= 0 {\n\t\treturn 0, false\n\t}\n\treturn batchLimit, true\n}\n\nfunc lookupGitHubRepoSizeKB(ctx context.Context, sourceConn *gitproto.Conn) (int64, bool) {\n\towner, repo, ok := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodGet, apiURL, nil)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, false\n\t}\n\treq.Header.Set(\"Accept\", \"application/vnd.github+json\")\n\treq.Header.Set(\"X-GitHub-Api-Version\", \"2022-11-28\")\n\treq.Header.Set(\"User-Agent\", capability.DefaultAgent())\n\treq.Header.Set(statsPhaseHeader, \"github repo metadata\")\n\tresp, err := sourceConn.HTTP.Do(req)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, false\n\t}\n\tdefer resp.Body.Close()\n\tif resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {\n\t\treturn 0, false\n\t}\n\tvar payload struct {\n\t\tSize int64 `json:\"size\"`\n\t}\n\tif err := json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(&payload); err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, false\n\t}\n\tif payload.Size <= 0 {\n\t\treturn 0, false\n\t}\n\treturn payload.Size, true\n}\n\nfunc githubOwnerRepo(conn *gitproto.Conn) (string, string, bool) {\n\tif conn == nil || conn.Endpoint == nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", \"\", false\n\t}\n\tep := conn.Endpoint\n\tif ep.Protocol != \"http\" && ep.Protocol != \"https\" {\n\t\treturn \"\", \"\", false\n\t}\n\tif !strings.EqualFold(ep.Host, \"github.com\") {\n\t\treturn \"\", \"\", false\n\t}\n\tpath := strings.Trim(ep.Path, \"/\")\n\tif strings.HasSuffix(path, \".git\") {\n\t\tpath = strings.TrimSuffix(path, \".git\")\n\t}\n\tparts := strings.Split(path, \"/\")\n\tif len(parts) != 2 || parts[0] == \"\" || parts[1] == \"\" {\n\t\treturn \"\", \"\", false\n\t}\n\treturn parts[0], parts[1], true\n}\n\n// packpAdvRefsAccessor is a thin wrapper to pass AdvRefs through the interface boundary.\ntype packpAdvRefsAccessor struct{}\n\nfunc (a *packpAdvRefsAccessor) advRefs() interface{} { return nil }\n"

planner.ActionForTargetHash(current),\n\t\t\t\tReason: fmt.Sprintf(\"%s -> %s via %s\", planner.ShortHash(current), planner.ShortHash(checkpoint), batch.TempRef),\n\t\t\t}}\n\t\t\tif idx == len(batch.Checkpoints)-1 {\n\t\t\t\tstagePlans = append(stagePlans, BranchPlan{\n\t\t\t\t\tBranch: batch.Plan.Branch, SourceRef: batch.Plan.SourceRef,\n\t\t\t\t\tTargetRef: batch.Plan.TargetRef, SourceHash: checkpoint,\n\t\t\t\t\tTargetHash: plumbing.ZeroHash, Kind: batch.Plan.Kind,\n\t\t\t\t\tAction: ActionCreate,\n\t\t\t\t\tReason: fmt.Sprintf(\"create %s at %s\", batch.Plan.TargetRef, planner.ShortHash(checkpoint)),\n\t\t\t\t})\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tdesired := toGitprotoDesiredSingle(batch.Plan.SourceRef, batch.TempRef, checkpoint)\n\t\t\thaves := planner.SingleHaveMap(current)\n\t\t\tpackReader, err := sourceService.FetchPack(ctx, sourceConn, desired, haves)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn result, fmt.Errorf(\"fetch source batch pack for %s: %w\", batch.Plan.TargetRef, err)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tpackReader = limitPackReadCloser(packReader, batchLimit)\n\t\t\tcmds := plansToPushCommands(stagePlans)\n\t\t\tif err := gitproto.PushPack(ctx, targetConn, targetAdv, cmds, packReader, cfg.Verbose); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn result, fmt.Errorf(\"push bootstrap batch for %s: %w\", batch.Plan.TargetRef, err)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tprogressf(cfg.Verbose, \"bootstrap-batch: branch=%s batch=%d/%d complete\",\n\t\t\t\tbatch.Plan.TargetRef, idx+1, len(batch.Checkpoints))\n\t\t\tcurrent = checkpoint\n\t\t\tresult.BatchCount++\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif current.IsZero() {\n\t\t\treturn result, fmt.Errorf(\"bootstrap batching for %s completed with no checkpoint state\", batch.Plan.TargetRef)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif batch.ResumeHash == batch.Plan.SourceHash {\n\t\t\tcmds := []gitproto.PushCommand{{\n\t\t\t\tName: batch.Plan.TargetRef, Old: plumbing.ZeroHash, New: batch.Plan.SourceHash,\n\t\t\t}}\n\t\t\tif err := gitproto.PushCommands(ctx, targetConn, targetAdv, cmds, cfg.Verbose); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn result, fmt.Errorf(\"resume bootstrap cutover for %s: %w\", batch.Plan.TargetRef, err)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Delete temp ref\n\t\tcmds := []gitproto.PushCommand{{Name: batch.TempRef, Old: current, Delete: true}}\n\t\tif err := gitproto.PushCommands(ctx, targetConn, targetAdv, cmds, cfg.Verbose); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn result, fmt.Errorf(\"delete bootstrap temp ref for %s: %w\", batch.Plan.TargetRef, err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tprogressf(cfg.Verbose, \"bootstrap-batch: branch=%s finalized\", batch.Plan.TargetRef)\n\t}\n\n\t// Tag phase (issue #1: tag creation independent of pack necessity)\n\tif len(tagPlans) > 0 {\n\t\tprogressf(cfg.Verbose, \"bootstrap-batch: pushing %d tag(s) after branch batches\", len(tagPlans))\n\t\ttagTargetRefs := planner.CopyRefHashMap(targetRefs)\n\t\tfor _, batch := range batches {\n\t\t\ttagTargetRefs[batch.Plan.TargetRef] = batch.Plan.SourceHash\n\t\t}\n\t\tpackReader, err := sourceService.FetchPack(ctx, sourceConn, tagDesired, tagTargetRefs)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tif errors.Is(err, git.NoErrAlreadyUpToDate) {\n\t\t\t\t// Objects already present — still need to create tag refs (issue #1).\n\t\t\t\tcmds := plansToPushCommands(tagPlans)\n\t\t\t\tif err := gitproto.PushCommands(ctx, targetConn, targetAdv, cmds, cfg.Verbose); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn result, fmt.Errorf(\"create tag refs after bootstrap: %w\", err)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\treturn result, fmt.Errorf(\"fetch bootstrap tag pack: %w\", err)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tpackReader = limitPackReadCloser(packReader, cfg.MaxPackBytes)\n\t\t\tcmds := plansToPushCommands(tagPlans)\n\t\t\tif err := gitproto.PushPack(ctx, targetConn, targetAdv, cmds, packReader, cfg.Verbose); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn result, fmt.Errorf(\"push bootstrap tags: %w\", err)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tresult.Pushed = len(plans)\n\tresult.Batching = true\n\tresult.RelayMode = \"bootstrap-batch\"\n\tresult.Stats = stats.snapshot()\n\treturn result, nil\n}\n\nfunc planBootstrapBatches(\n\tctx context.Context,\n\tcfg Config,\n\tsourceConn *gitproto.Conn,\n\tsourceService *gitproto.RefService,\n\tdesired []planner.DesiredRef,\n\ttargetRefs map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash,\n) ([]planner.BootstrapBatch, error) {\n\tout := make([]planner.BootstrapBatch, 0, len(desired))\n\tfor _, ref := range desired {\n\t\tcheckpoints, err := planBranchCheckpoints(ctx, cfg, sourceConn, sourceService, ref)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tout = append(out, planner.BootstrapBatch{\n\t\t\tPlan: BranchPlan{\n\t\t\t\tBranch: ref.Label, SourceRef: ref.SourceRef,\n\t\t\t\tTargetRef: ref.TargetRef, SourceHash: ref.SourceHash,\n\t\t\t\tKind: ref.Kind, Action: ActionCreate,\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tTempRef:     planner.BootstrapTempRef(ref.TargetRef),\n\t\t\tResumeHash:  targetRefs[planner.BootstrapTempRef(ref.TargetRef)],\n\t\t\tCheckpoints: checkpoints,\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\treturn out, nil\n}\n\nfunc planBranchCheckpoints(\n\tctx context.Context,\n\tcfg Config,\n\tsourceConn *gitproto.Conn,\n\tsourceService *gitproto.RefService,\n\tref planner.DesiredRef,\n) ([]plumbing.Hash, error) {\n\tprogressf(cfg.Verbose, \"bootstrap-batch: fetching commit graph for %s\", ref.TargetRef)\n\tgraphStore := memory.NewStorage()\n\tgpRef := gitproto.DesiredRef{\n\t\tSourceRef: ref.SourceRef, TargetRef: ref.TargetRef, SourceHash: ref.SourceHash,\n\t}\n\tif err := sourceService.FetchCommitGraph(ctx, graphStore, sourceConn, gpRef); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"fetch bootstrap planning graph for %s: %w\", ref.TargetRef, err)\n\t}\n\n\tchain, err := planner.FirstParentChain(graphStore, ref.SourceHash)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"walk first-parent chain for %s: %w\", ref.TargetRef, err)\n\t}\n\tif len(chain) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"empty first-parent chain for %s\", ref.TargetRef)\n\t}\n\n\tcheckpoints := make([]plumbing.Hash, 0, len(chain))\n\tprevIdx := -1\n\tprevHash := plumbing.ZeroHash\n\tprevSpan := 0\n\tfor prevIdx < len(chain)-1 {\n\t\tbestIdx, err := planner.SampledCheckpointUnderLimit(chain, prevIdx, prevSpan, func(idx int) (bool, error) {\n\t\t\ttooLarge, err := sourcePackExceedsLimit(ctx, sourceConn, sourceService, ref, chain[idx], prevHash, cfg.BatchMaxPackBytes)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn false, fmt.Errorf(\"measure bootstrap batch for %s at %s: %w\", ref.TargetRef, planner.ShortHash(chain[idx]), err)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif tooLarge {\n\t\t\t\tprogressf(cfg.Verbose, \"bootstrap-batch: sample %s exceeds limit=%d\", planner.ShortHash(chain[idx]), cfg.BatchMaxPackBytes)\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\tprogressf(cfg.Verbose, \"bootstrap-batch: sample %s fits limit=%d\", planner.ShortHash(chain[idx]), cfg.BatchMaxPackBytes)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn tooLarge, nil\n\t\t})\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tif bestIdx <= prevIdx {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"could not find bootstrap checkpoint for %s under batch-max-pack-bytes=%d\", ref.TargetRef, cfg.BatchMaxPackBytes)\n\t\t}\n\t\tprevSpan = bestIdx - prevIdx\n\t\tprevIdx = bestIdx\n\t\tprevHash = chain[bestIdx]\n\t\tcheckpoints = append(checkpoints, prevHash)\n\t\tprogressf(cfg.Verbose, \"bootstrap-batch: branch=%s planned-checkpoint=%s selected=%d chain-len=%d\",\n\t\t\tref.TargetRef, planner.ShortHash(prevHash), len(checkpoints), len(chain))\n\t}\n\treturn checkpoints, nil\n}\n\nfunc sourcePackExceedsLimit(\n\tctx context.Context,\n\tsourceConn *gitproto.Conn,\n\tsourceService *gitproto.RefService,\n\tref planner.DesiredRef,\n\twant, have plumbing.Hash,\n\tlimit int64,\n) (bool, error) {\n\tdesired := toGitprotoDesiredSingle(ref.SourceRef, ref.TargetRef, want)\n\thaves := planner.SingleHaveMap(have)\n\tpackReader, err := sourceService.FetchPack(ctx, sourceConn, desired, haves)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false, err\n\t}\n\tdefer packReader.Close()\n\t_, err = io.Copy(io.Discard, limitPackReadCloser(packReader, limit))\n\tif err == nil {\n\t\treturn false, nil\n\t}\n\tif strings.Contains(err.Error(), \"source pack exceeded max-pack-bytes limit\") {\n\t\treturn true, nil\n\t}\n\treturn false, err\n}\n\nfunc toGitprotoDesiredSingle(sourceRef, targetRef plumbing.ReferenceName, hash plumbing.Hash) map[plumbing.ReferenceName]gitproto.DesiredRef {\n\treturn map[plumbing.ReferenceName]gitproto.DesiredRef{\n\t\ttargetRef: {SourceRef: sourceRef, TargetRef: targetRef, SourceHash: hash},\n\t}\n}\n"
}

18	func TestSelectBranches(t *testing.T) {
19		source := map[string]plumbing.Hash{
20			"main": plumbing.NewHash("1111111111111111111111111111111111111111"),
21			"dev":  plumbing.NewHash("2222222222222222222222222222222222222222"),
22		}
23	
24		got := selectBranches(source, []string{"dev", "missing"})
25		if len(got) != 1 || got["dev"] != source["dev"] {
26			t.Fatalf("unexpected branch selection: %#v", got)
27		}
28	}

30	func TestPlanBranchSkip(t *testing.T) {
31		hash := plumbing.NewHash("1111111111111111111111111111111111111111")
32		plan, err := planBranch(nil, "main", hash, hash)
33		if err != nil {
34			t.Fatalf("planBranch returned error: %v", err)
35		}
36		if plan.Action != ActionSkip {
37			t.Fatalf("expected skip, got %s", plan.Action)
38		}
39	}

41	func TestPlanBranchCreate(t *testing.T) {
42		repo, err := git.Init(memory.NewStorage(), nil)
43		if err != nil {
44			t.Fatalf("init repo: %v", err)
45		}
46		sourceHash := seedCommit(t, repo, nil)
47	
48		plan := BranchPlan{
49			Branch:     "main",
50			SourceHash: sourceHash,
51			Action:     ActionCreate,
52		}
53	
54		if plan.Action != ActionCreate {
55			t.Fatalf("expected create")
56		}
57	}

59	func TestPlanBranchFastForwardAndBlock(t *testing.T) {
60		repo, err := git.Init(memory.NewStorage(), nil)
61		if err != nil {
62			t.Fatalf("init repo: %v", err)
63		}
64	
65		root := seedCommit(t, repo, nil)
66		next := seedCommit(t, repo, []plumbing.Hash{root})
67		side := seedCommit(t, repo, []plumbing.Hash{root})
68	
69		ffPlan, err := planBranch(repo, "main", next, root)
70		if err != nil {
71			t.Fatalf("planBranch fast-forward: %v", err)
72		}
73		if ffPlan.Action != ActionUpdate {
74			t.Fatalf("expected update, got %s", ffPlan.Action)
75		}
76	
77		blockPlan, err := planBranch(repo, "main", side, next)
78		if err != nil {
79			t.Fatalf("planBranch block: %v", err)
80		}
81		if blockPlan.Action != ActionBlock {
82			t.Fatalf("expected block, got %s", blockPlan.Action)
83		}
84	}

86	func seedCommit(t *testing.T, repo *git.Repository, parents []plumbing.Hash) plumbing.Hash {
87		t.Helper()
88	
89		now := time.Now().UTC()
90	
91		obj := repo.Storer.NewEncodedObject()
92		commit := &object.Commit{
93			Author: object.Signature{
94				Name:  "test",
95				Email: "test@example.com",
96				When:  now,
97			},
98			Committer: object.Signature{
99				Name:  "test",
100				Email: "test@example.com",
101				When:  now,
102			},
103			Message:      fmt.Sprintf("test-%d-%d", len(parents), now.UnixNano()),
104			TreeHash:     plumbing.ZeroHash,
105			ParentHashes: parents,
106		}
107	
108		if err := commit.Encode(obj); err != nil {
109			t.Fatalf("encode commit: %v", err)
110		}
111		hash, err := repo.Storer.SetEncodedObject(obj)
112		if err != nil {
113			t.Fatalf("store commit: %v", err)
114		}
115		return hash
116	}
117	
118	func TestSampledCheckpointCandidates(t *testing.T) {
119		candidates := sampledCheckpointCandidates(10, 100, 20)
120		if len(candidates) == 0 {
121			t.Fatalf("expected sampled candidates")
122		}
123		if candidates[0] != 29 {
124			t.Fatalf("expected highest candidate first, got %v", candidates)
125		}
126		if !slices.Contains(candidates, 29) {
127			t.Fatalf("expected projected candidate near previous span, got %v", candidates)
128		}
129		if !slices.Contains(candidates, 10) {
130			t.Fatalf("expected lower bound candidate, got %v", candidates)
131		}
132	}
133	
134	func TestSampledCheckpointUnderLimitByProbe(t *testing.T) {
135		chain := make([]plumbing.Hash, 40)
136		for i := range chain {
137			chain[i] = plumbing.NewHash(fmt.Sprintf("%040x", i+1))
138		}
139	
140		var probes []int
141		best, err := sampledCheckpointUnderLimitByProbe(chain, 4, 8, func(idx int) (bool, error) {
142			probes = append(probes, idx)
143			return idx > 19, nil
144		})
145		if err != nil {
146			t.Fatalf("sampledCheckpointUnderLimitByProbe: %v", err)
147		}
148		if best < 12 || best > 19 {
149			t.Fatalf("expected a reasonable sampled checkpoint, got %d", best)
150		}
151		if len(probes) > 6 {
152			t.Fatalf("expected fixed small probe count, got %d probes: %v", len(probes), probes)
153		}
154	}

1	package syncer

3	import (
4		"bytes"

10	func TestPacketReaderHandlesSpecialPackets(t *testing.T) {
11		reader := newPacketReader(bytes.NewBufferString("0000000100020006a\n"))
12	
13		kind, payload, err := reader.ReadPacket()
14		if err != nil {
15			t.Fatalf("read flush: %v", err)
16		}
17		if kind != packetTypeFlush || payload != nil {
18			t.Fatalf("unexpected flush packet: kind=%v payload=%q", kind, payload)
19		}
20	
21		kind, payload, err = reader.ReadPacket()
22		if err != nil {
23			t.Fatalf("read delim: %v", err)
24		}
25		if kind != packetTypeDelim {
26			t.Fatalf("unexpected delim kind: %v", kind)
27		}
28	
29		kind, payload, err = reader.ReadPacket()
30		if err != nil {
31			t.Fatalf("read response-end: %v", err)
32		}
33		if kind != packetTypeResponseEnd {
34			t.Fatalf("unexpected response-end kind: %v", kind)
35		}
36	
37		kind, payload, err = reader.ReadPacket()
38		if err != nil {
39			t.Fatalf("read data: %v", err)
40		}
41		if kind != packetTypeData || string(payload) != "a\n" {
42			t.Fatalf("unexpected data packet: kind=%v payload=%q", kind, payload)
43		}
44	}
45	
46	func TestDecodeV2CapabilityAdvertisement(t *testing.T) {
47		wire := "" +
48			"000eversion 2\n" +
49			"0013ls-refs=unborn\n" +
50			"0012fetch=shallow\n" +
51			"0013agent=git/test\n" +
52			"0000"
53	
54		adv, err := decodeV2CapabilityAdvertisement(bytes.NewBufferString(wire))
55		if err != nil {
56			t.Fatalf("decode advertisement: %v", err)
57		}
58		if !adv.Supports("ls-refs") {
59			t.Fatalf("expected ls-refs capability")
60		}
61		if got := adv.Value("fetch"); got != "shallow" {
62			t.Fatalf("unexpected fetch value %q", got)
63		}
64		if got := adv.Value("agent"); got != "git/test" {
65			t.Fatalf("unexpected agent value %q", got)
66		}
67	}
68	
69	func TestEncodeV2CommandRequest(t *testing.T) {
70		req, err := encodeV2CommandRequest(
71			"ls-refs",
72			[]string{"agent=git-sync/test"},
73			[]string{"peel", "ref-prefix refs/heads/"},
74		)
75		if err != nil {
76			t.Fatalf("encode request: %v", err)
77		}
78	
79		want := "" +
80			"0014command=ls-refs\n" +
81			"0018agent=git-sync/test\n" +
82			"0001" +
83			"0009peel\n" +
84			"001bref-prefix refs/heads/\n" +
85			"0000"
86		if string(req) != want {
87			t.Fatalf("unexpected request:\n%s\nwant:\n%s", req, want)
88		}
89	}
90	
91	func TestSourceFetchRequestV2IncludesIncludeTagForTagSync(t *testing.T) {
92		adv := &v2CapabilityAdvertisement{
93			Capabilities: map[string]string{
94				"fetch": "thin-pack filter",
95			},
96		}
97		desired := map[plumbing.ReferenceName]desiredRef{
98			plumbing.NewTagReferenceName("v1"): {
99				Kind:       RefKindTag,
100				Label:      "v1",
101				SourceRef:  plumbing.NewTagReferenceName("v1"),
102				TargetRef:  plumbing.NewTagReferenceName("v1"),
103				SourceHash: plumbing.NewHash("1111111111111111111111111111111111111111"),
104			},
105		}
106	
107		body, wants, haves, err := sourceFetchRequestV2(adv, desired, nil)
108		if err != nil {
109			t.Fatalf("build fetch request: %v", err)
110		}
111		if wants != 1 || haves != 0 {
112			t.Fatalf("unexpected wants/haves: wants=%d haves=%d", wants, haves)
113		}
114		if !bytes.Contains(body, []byte("include-tag\n")) {
115			t.Fatalf("expected include-tag in fetch request, got:\n%s", body)
116		}
117	}

16	func TestResolveAuthMethodPrefersExplicitToken(t *testing.T) {
17		ep, err := transport.NewEndpoint("https://github.com/entireio/cli.git")
18		if err != nil {
19			t.Fatalf("new endpoint: %v", err)
20		}
21	
22		originalFill := gitCredentialFillCommand
23		t.Cleanup(func() {
24			gitCredentialFillCommand = originalFill
25		})
26		gitCredentialFillCommand = func(ctx context.Context, input string) ([]byte, error) {
27			t.Fatalf("unexpected git credential fill call with input %q", input)
28			return nil, nil
29		}
30	
31		auth, err := resolveAuthMethod(Endpoint{
32			Username: "git",
33			Token:    "explicit-token",
34		}, ep)
35		if err != nil {
36			t.Fatalf("resolve auth: %v", err)
37		}
38	
39		basic, ok := auth.(*transporthttp.BasicAuth)
40		if !ok {
41			t.Fatalf("expected basic auth, got %T", auth)
42		}
43		if basic.Username != "git" || basic.Password != "explicit-token" {
44			t.Fatalf("unexpected auth: %+v", basic)
45		}
46	}

110	func TestResolveAuthMethodRefreshesExpiredEntireDBToken(t *testing.T) {
111		configDir := t.TempDir()
112		tokenStorePath := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "tokens.json")
113		t.Setenv("ENTIRE_CONFIG_DIR", configDir)
114		t.Setenv("ENTIRE_TOKEN_STORE", "file")
115		t.Setenv("ENTIRE_TOKEN_STORE_PATH", tokenStorePath)
116	
117		server := httptest.NewTLSServer(http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
118			if r.URL.Path != "/oauth/token" {
119				t.Fatalf("unexpected path: %s", r.URL.Path)
120			}
121			if err := r.ParseForm(); err != nil {
122				t.Fatalf("parse form: %v", err)
123			}
124			if got := r.Form.Get("refresh_token"); got != "refresh-token" {
125				t.Fatalf("unexpected refresh token: %s", got)
126			}
127			w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
128			_, _ = w.Write([]byte(`{"access_token":"new-token","refresh_token":"new-refresh","expires_in":3600}`))
129		}))
130		defer server.Close()
131	
132		ep, err := transport.NewEndpoint(server.URL + "/git/test/repo")
133		if err != nil {
134			t.Fatalf("new endpoint: %v", err)
135		}
136		credHost := endpointCredentialHost(ep)
137		writeEntireDBHostsFile(t, configDir, credHost, "test-user")
138		if err := writeEntireDBStoredToken("entire:"+credHost, "test-user", encodeTokenWithExpiration("expired-token", -3600)); err != nil {
139			t.Fatalf("write expired token: %v", err)
140		}
141		if err := writeEntireDBStoredToken("entire:"+credHost+":refresh", "test-user", "refresh-token"); err != nil {
142			t.Fatalf("write refresh token: %v", err)
143		}
144	
145		auth, err := resolveAuthMethod(Endpoint{SkipTLSVerify: true}, ep)
146		if err != nil {
147			t.Fatalf("resolve auth: %v", err)
148		}
149	
150		basic, ok := auth.(*transporthttp.BasicAuth)
151		if !ok {
152			t.Fatalf("expected basic auth, got %T", auth)
153		}
154		if basic.Password != "new-token" {
155			t.Fatalf("unexpected password: %q", basic.Password)
156		}
157	
158		stored, err := readEntireDBStoredToken("entire:"+credHost, "test-user")
159		if err != nil {
160			t.Fatalf("read stored token: %v", err)
161		}
162		token, _ := decodeTokenWithExpiration(stored)
163		if token != "new-token" {
164			t.Fatalf("unexpected refreshed token: %q", token)
165		}
166	}
167	
168	func writeEntireDBHostsFile(t *testing.T, configDir, host, username string) {
169		t.Helper()
170		hosts := map[string]map[string]any{
171			host: {
172				"activeUser": username,
173				"users":      []string{username},
174			},
175		}
176		data, err := json.Marshal(hosts)
177		if err != nil {
178			t.Fatalf("marshal hosts: %v", err)
179		}
180		if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(configDir, "hosts.json"), data, 0o600); err != nil {
181			t.Fatalf("write hosts: %v", err)
182		}
183	}
184

got != \"shallow\" {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"unexpected fetch value %q\", got)\n\t}\n\tif got := caps.Value(\"agent\"); got != \"git/test\" {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"unexpected agent value %q\", got)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestEncodeCommand(t *testing.T) {\n\treq, err := EncodeCommand(\n\t\t\"ls-refs\",\n\t\t[]string{\"agent=git-sync/test\"},\n\t\t[]string{\"peel\", \"ref-prefix refs/heads/\"},\n\t)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"encode: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\twant := \"\" +\n\t\t\"0014command=ls-refs\\n\" +\n\t\t\"0018agent=git-sync/test\\n\" +\n\t\t\"0001\" +\n\t\t\"0009peel\\n\" +\n\t\t\"001bref-prefix refs/heads/\\n\" +\n\t\t\"0000\"\n\tif string(req) != want {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"unexpected request:\\n%s\\nwant:\\n%s\", req, want)\n\t}\n}\n"

1556	type v2TestCommandRequest struct {
1557		Command string
1558		Args    []string
1559	}
1560	
1561	func decodeV2TestCommandRequest(body []byte) (v2TestCommandRequest, error) {
1562		reader := gitproto.NewPacketReader(bytes.NewReader(body))
1563		req := v2TestCommandRequest{}
1564		inArgs := false
1565	
1566		for {
1567			kind, payload, err := reader.ReadPacket()
1568			if err != nil {
1569				return req, err
1570			}
1571			switch kind {
1572			case gitproto.PacketFlush:
1573				return req, nil
1574			case gitproto.PacketDelim:
1575				inArgs = true
1576			case gitproto.PacketData:
1577				line := strings.TrimSuffix(string(payload), "\n")
1578				if strings.HasPrefix(line, "command=") {
1579					req.Command = strings.TrimPrefix(line, "command=")
1580					continue
1581				}
1582				if inArgs {
1583					req.Args = append(req.Args, line)
1584				}

23	# git-sync Rewrite Issue List
24	
25	This document converts the review memo into a concrete issue list for a from-scratch rewrite branch.
26	
27	The intent is not to patch the current architecture incrementally first. The intent is to:
28	
29	1. Preserve the parts of the current implementation that are directionally correct.
30	2. Throw away the parts that are structurally wrong or too costly to evolve.
31	3. Rebuild in a way that makes comparison against the current branch explicit.
32	
33	## Rewrite Goal
34	
35	Rebuild `git-sync` as a smaller set of focused packages around the same product goal:
36	
37	- remote-to-remote smart HTTP Git mirroring
38	- empty-target bootstrap relay
39	- narrow incremental relay
40	- fallback materialized push path when relay is not safe
41	
42	## Preserve These Ideas
43	
44	- Relay-first design is correct.
45	- Empty-target bootstrap as a distinct strategy is correct.
46	- Narrow incremental relay instead of trying to make every update streamable is correct.
47	- Typed result/output structs are useful.
48	- Machine-readable output is worth keeping.
49	- Integration-heavy testing is the right testing style for this tool.
50	
51	## Replace These Structural Choices
52	
53	- Monolithic orchestration in `internal/syncer/syncer.go`
54	- Mixed protocol, auth, planning, execution, stats, and measurement concerns in one package
55	- Partially custom, partially `go-git` transport stack
56	- Implicit capability handling spread across request builders
57	- Batch planning that probes by repeatedly fetching full packs and discarding them
58	
59	## Proposed Rewrite Shape
60	
61	- `internal/gitproto`
62	  - pkt-line
63	  - smart HTTP v1/v2
64	  - capability negotiation
65	  - ref advertisement and fetch/push request building
66	- `internal/planner`
67	  - desired ref construction
68	  - mapping normalization and validation
69	  - plan generation
70	  - prune policy
71	  - checkpoint planning
72	- `internal/auth`
73	  - credential resolution chain
74	  - token refresh
75	  - token store backends
76	- `internal/strategy/bootstrap`
77	  - one-shot relay bootstrap

6601	codex
6602	Findings:
6603	
6604	1. High: EntireDB refresh failures are still effectively swallowed instead of surfacing an auth-refresh error. `getTokenWithRefresh` now returns a real error, but [internal/auth/entiredb.go](/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/auth/entiredb.go:75) converts any refresh/read failure into `( "", false )`, and [internal/auth/auth.go](/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/auth/auth.go:23) treats that as “no EntireDB credential” and falls through to git credential helper or anonymous auth. That misses the rewrite requirement in issue #7: an expired token plus refresh failure still won’t produce an explicit refresh error path.
6605	
6606	2. Medium: Mapping validation still happens after remote I/O, so issues #2/#3 are not fixed at the entry-point level. In `Run`, `Bootstrap`, `Probe`, and `Fetch`, the code builds `ref-prefix`es and calls the remotes before `planner.BuildDesiredRefs` performs `ValidateMappings`; representative path: [internal/syncer/syncer.go](/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/syncer/syncer.go:343) then [internal/syncer/syncer.go](/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/syncer/syncer.go:360). The planner comment in [internal/planner/planner.go](/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/planner/planner.go:22) claims this happens before network activity, but it does not. `--map main:stable --map release:stable` will still hit the network before failing.
6607	
6608	3. Medium: Duplicate-target protection is incomplete; `--include-tags` can silently overwrite a mapped target ref. `ValidateMappings` only deduplicates entries inside `cfg.Mappings`, but `BuildDesiredRefs` later writes directly into `desired[targetRef]` / `managed[targetRef]` and then auto-adds tags with the same blind assignment ([internal/planner/planner.go](/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/planner/planner.go:32), [internal/planner/planner.go](/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/planner/planner.go:70)). Example: map `refs/tags/release -> refs/tags/v1` with `--include-tags` and an existing source `refs/tags/v1`; the automatic tag add will overwrite the explicit mapping with no error.
6609	
6610	4. Medium: Issue #9 is only partially fixed; v2 `ls-refs` still buffers an unbounded response in memory. `RequestInfoRefs` is capped, but [internal/gitproto/refs.go](/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/gitproto/refs.go:128) calls [internal/gitproto/smarthttp.go](/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/gitproto/smarthttp.go:91), and `PostRPC` still does a raw `io.ReadAll(reader)`. A malicious or pathological `ls-refs` response can still force unbounded allocation on a normal control path.
6611	
6612	5. Low: `--verbose` push progress regressed. All push paths set `req.Progress = progressWriter(verbose)`, but [internal/gitproto/push.go](/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/gitproto/push.go:203) always returns `nil`, so verbose mode no longer surfaces receive-pack progress.
6613	
6614	Open questions / assumptions:
6615	
6616	- I did not find a concrete in-process race in the rewrite; `go test -race ./internal/...` passed, and the new stats collector looks properly synchronized.
6617	- The package split is materially better than `main`, but `internal/syncer` still owns bootstrap strategy, retry heuristics, and GitHub preflight logic, so the “strategy packages + slim orchestrator” goal is only partially realized.
6618	- The file token store is still not process-safe despite the earlier comment claiming locking; the implementation/comment mismatch is visible in [internal/auth/tokenstore.go](/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/auth/tokenstore.go:19) and [internal/auth/tokenstore.go](/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/auth/tokenstore.go:66).
6619	
6620	Verification:

6622	- Ran `go test ./internal/...`
6623	- Ran `go test -race ./internal/...`

Excellent review from Codex. Let me address the findings:

**Codex Review Findings:**

1. **High: Auth refresh errors swallowed** — `LookupEntireDBCredential` converts refresh errors to `("", false)`, falling through to git cred helper. Need to propagate the error.
2. **Medium: Mapping validation after network I/O** — Need to validate mappings before any remote calls.
3. **Medium: Tag/mapping target collision** — Auto-added tags can overwrite explicit mappings.
4. **Medium: `PostRPC` unbounded read** — `ls-refs` response via `PostRPC` still unbounded.
5. **Low: `progressWriter` returns nil** — Verbose push progress regressed.

Let me fix all five:

  "old_string": "// PostRPC sends a buffered POST to the given service and returns the full response body.\nfunc PostRPC(ctx context.Context, conn *Conn, service string, body []byte, v2 bool, phase string) ([]byte, error) {\n\treader, err := PostRPCStream(ctx, conn, service, body, v2, phase)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tdefer reader.Close()\n\treturn io.ReadAll(reader)\n}",
  "new_string": "// PostRPC sends a buffered POST to the given service and returns the full response body.\n// Responses are bounded to prevent unbounded memory allocation (issue #9).\nfunc PostRPC(ctx context.Context, conn *Conn, service string, body []byte, v2 bool, phase string) ([]byte, error) {\n\treader, err := PostRPCStream(ctx, conn, service, body, v2, phase)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tdefer reader.Close()\n\tconst maxRPCResponse = 128 * 1024 * 1024 // 128 MiB\n\tlr := io.LimitReader(reader, maxRPCResponse+1)\n\tdata, err := io.ReadAll(lr)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tif int64(len(data)) > maxRPCResponse {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"RPC response for %s exceeds %d byte limit\", service, maxRPCResponse)\n\t}\n\treturn data, nil\n}"
}

Here's the summary of what was done:

## Rewrite Summary

### Package Structure (from monolith to focused packages)

| Package | Responsibility | Lines |
|---------|---------------|-------|
| `internal/gitproto` | pkt-line, smart HTTP, capability negotiation, v1/v2 fetch/push | ~1,340 |
| `internal/planner` | mapping validation, ref planning, relay eligibility, checkpoints | ~1,130 |
| `internal/auth` | credential resolution, Entire DB tokens, git credential helper | ~430 |
| `internal/syncer` | slim orchestrator + stats + measurement + bootstrap | ~1,520 |

**Before:** `syncer.go` was 3,143 lines. **After:** the orchestrator is ~990 lines, with all protocol, planning, auth, and strategy logic extracted.

### Issues Addressed from rewrite-issue-list.md

| # | Issue | Status |
|---|-------|--------|
| 1 | Tag refs not created when objects already present | **Fixed** — bootstrap creates tag refs via `PushCommands` fallback |
| 2 | Duplicate target mappings silently accepted | **Fixed** — `ValidateMappings` + `addManaged` collision check |
| 3 | Inconsistent ref kinds / partial qualification | **Fixed** — cross-kind and mixed-qualification rejected |
| 4 | Sideband preference backwards | **Fixed** — `PreferredSideband` prefers sideband-64k |
| 5 | Pack reader leak in bootstrap batch loop | **Fixed** — explicit close discipline |
| 6 | `include-tag` without capability gating | **Fixed** — `caps.FetchSupports("include-tag")` check |
| 7 | OAuth refresh failures swallowed | **Fixed** — errors propagated, not silently reused |
| 8 | Stats not synchronized | **Fixed** — `sync.Mutex` on all stats operations |
| 9 | Unbounded response reads | **Fixed** — `RequestInfoRefs` and `PostRPC` bounded |
| 11-13 | Monolith / duplicated setup / ambient state | **Fixed** — package decomposition |
| 17 | Packet parsing allocations | **Improved** — reusable buffer in `PacketReader` |

### Codex Review Findings (all 5 fixed)
1. Auth refresh error propagation
2. Mapping validation before network I/O
3. Tag/mapping target collision detection
4. `PostRPC` bounded response
5. `progressWriter` returning `os.Stderr`

### Test Results
- `go test ./...` — all passing
- `go test -race ./...` — all passing, no races detected

- OAuth refresh error surfacing (#7)

- **#14 — Batch planning probes:** Sampling strategy is ported but still uses fetch-and-discard; a cheaper heuristic would be a follow-on
- **#15 — Materialized fallback scaling:** Still in-memory; explicit size limits not added

- **#18-22 — Test gaps/benchmarks:** New unit tests for planner and gitproto added; protocol malformed-input tests, behavioral edge cases, and benchmarks are still open
- **Strategy packages** (`internal/strategy/*`): The directories were created but bootstrap/incremental/materialized logic stayed in `syncer` and `bootstrap.go` rather than being fully separated into strategy packages

So the structural rewrite and the correctness fixes are done. The test expansion, benchmarks, and strategy package extraction are the main remaining work. Want me to keep going on any of those?

Here are the findings:

1. **Duplicate `statsPhaseHeader` constant** -- `internal/gitproto/smarthttp.go:16` and `internal/syncer/stats.go:91` both define `const statsPhaseHeader = "X-Git-Sync-Stats-Phase"`. This should be a single exported constant in one package.

2. **Near-duplicate `DesiredRef` types** -- `internal/planner/types.go:37` and `internal/gitproto/fetch.go:22` define structurally similar `DesiredRef` structs (both carry SourceRef, TargetRef, SourceHash). The syncer manually converts between them (`toGitprotoDesired`, `toGitprotoDesiredSingle` in `syncer.go:306` and `bootstrap.go:290`). One canonical type would eliminate the conversion boilerplate.

3. **Near-duplicate push setup blocks** -- `internal/gitproto/push.go`: `PushObjects` (lines 26-68), `PushPack` (lines 71-113), and `PushCommands` (lines 116-154) all repeat session creation, capability negotiation, sideband setup, and delete-refs checking. A shared `newPushRequest` helper could collapse ~30 duplicated lines.

4. **Near-duplicate V2 fetch body construction** -- `internal/gitproto/fetch.go`: `fetchToStoreV2` (lines 128-136) and `fetchPackV2` (lines 163-176) build nearly identical `cmdArgs` slices (ofs-delta, no-progress, wants, haves, done). Only difference is the `include-tag` line. Extract a shared builder.

5. **Dead function** -- `internal/planner/types.go:111`: `ActionForTargetHash` is not called anywhere in the reviewed source files (only appears as a declaration).

6. **Custom insertion sort** -- `internal/planner/types.go:173-179`: `sortStrings` reimplements insertion sort instead of using `sort.Strings` from the standard library.

7. **Redundant nil guard** -- `internal/auth/entiredb.go:57-59`: `endpointBaseURL` re-checks `ep == nil || ep.Host == ""`, but every caller (`LookupEntireDBCredential` at line 38) already returns early on that same condition.

8. **`RelayFallbackReason` always returns last branch's reason** -- `internal/planner/relay.go:93-101`: When both `CanIncrementalRelay` and `CanFullTagCreateRelay` return false, it returns only the tag-relay reason, silently discarding the incremental reason. This looks like a logic bug (the `else` branches shadow each other).

Here are the findings:

**Code reuse issues found:**

1. **`statsPhaseHeader` constant duplicated across packages.**
   - `/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/gitproto/smarthttp.go:16` -- `const statsPhaseHeader = "X-Git-Sync-Stats-Phase"`
   - `/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/syncer/stats.go:91` -- `const statsPhaseHeader = "X-Git-Sync-Stats-Phase"`
   - Both define the identical string. Should be a single exported constant in one package.

2. **`DesiredRef` struct defined in two packages with near-identical fields.**
   - `/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/planner/types.go:37` -- `planner.DesiredRef` (Kind, Label, SourceRef, TargetRef, SourceHash)
   - `/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/gitproto/fetch.go:22` -- `gitproto.DesiredRef` (SourceRef, TargetRef, SourceHash, IsTag)
   - The syncer has a `toGitprotoDesired` converter (syncer.go:306) and `toGitprotoDesiredSingle` (bootstrap.go:290) that manually copies fields between the two. This is a design smell -- a single type or an interface would eliminate the conversion boilerplate.

3. **`buildSidebandReader` duplicates logic already in `PreferredSideband`.**
   - `/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/gitproto/fetch.go:352` -- `buildSidebandReader` re-checks `Sideband64k` vs `Sideband` with the same preference order as `PreferredSideband` at `capability.go:121`. The sideband type selection could delegate to `PreferredSideband` instead of reimplementing the switch.

4. **Hand-rolled insertion sort duplicates `sort.Strings`.**
   - `/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/planner/types.go:173` -- `sortStrings` is a manual insertion sort. The standard library `sort.Strings` (used elsewhere, e.g., syncer.go:251, capability.go:61) does the same thing and is clearer.

5. **Push session setup is copy-pasted three times.**
   - `/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/gitproto/push.go` lines 27-45, 79-99, and 119-143 repeat the pattern: open `NewReceivePackSession`, create `ReferenceUpdateRequest`, set progress/sideband, iterate commands. A shared helper would remove ~40 lines of duplication.

No other significant duplications found. The `auth`, `pktline`, `checkpoint`, and `measurement` files are clean.

Now I have a complete picture. Here are my findings:

---

**Efficiency findings:**

1. **`planner/planner.go:332-336` -- Double object load in `collectObjects`.** `store.EncodedObject()` is called to get the type, then `object.GetCommit/GetTree/GetTag` re-fetches and decodes the same object. The type could be determined from the decoded object directly, saving one store lookup per non-blob object.

2. **`syncer/syncer.go:396+389` -- `FetchToStore` materializes all objects even when relay is possible.** The code fetches into the in-memory store (line 390), then checks `CanIncrementalRelay` (line 428). When relay succeeds, the materialized objects are never used. Moving the relay eligibility check before the fetch would avoid this waste entirely. However, `BuildPlans` (line 396) needs the store for `ReachesCommit`. This is a design-level issue: the fast-forward check forces full materialization even on the relay path.

3. **`syncer/bootstrap.go:265-288` -- Full pack download just to check size.** `sourcePackExceedsLimit` downloads the entire pack and discards it via `io.Copy(io.Discard, ...)` to determine if it exceeds the limit. Each checkpoint probe fetches a full pack from the remote. This is an N-probe network cost that could be avoided if the server's `Content-Length` header were checked first.

4. **`syncer/measurement.go:37` -- `runtime.ReadMemStats` every 10ms.** `ReadMemStats` is a stop-the-world operation. Polling it every 10ms on the hot path adds GC pause overhead. A 100-500ms interval would be far less intrusive.

5. **`planner/planner.go:253-282` -- `ReachesCommit` uses `map[Hash]bool` for `seen`.** Using `map[Hash]struct{}` would save 1 byte per entry (trivial per-key, but this set can grow to the entire commit graph on non-fast-forward checks).

6. **`gitproto/fetch.go:369-375` -- `collectWants` duplicates `SortedUniqueHashes`.** The caller `fetchToStoreV2` at line 123 calls `collectWants` (which sorts+dedupes), then line 124 calls `SortedUniqueHashes` on `refValues` independently. Both are fine, but `fetchPackV2` (line 157-158) does the same pair -- no issue per se, but `collectWants` already calls `SortedUniqueHashes` internally, and the result is immediately string-formatted, so the sort is wasted work (git protocol doesn't require sorted wants).

7. **`auth/tokenstore.go:28-37` -- `fileTokenPath()` recomputed on every call.** Both `ReadStoredToken` and `WriteStoredToken` call `fileTokenPath()` which calls `os.UserHomeDir()` each time. During a refresh flow, this is called at least 3 times (read access token, read refresh token, write access token). Caching the result would be cleaner, though the cost is minor.

Findings 2, 3, and 4 are the most impactful. The rest are minor.

17	// PushCommand represents a single ref update command.
18	type PushCommand struct {
19		Name   plumbing.ReferenceName
20		Old    plumbing.Hash
21		New    plumbing.Hash
22		Delete bool
23	}
24	
25	// PushObjects pushes locally-materialized objects to the target.
26	func PushObjects(
27		ctx context.Context,
28		conn *Conn,
29		adv *packp.AdvRefs,
30		commands []PushCommand,
31		store storer.Storer,
32		hashes []plumbing.Hash,
33		verbose bool,
34	) error {
35		session, err := conn.Transport.NewReceivePackSession(conn.Endpoint, conn.Auth)
36		if err != nil {
37			return fmt.Errorf("open target receive-pack session: %w", err)
38		}
39		defer session.Close()
40	
41		req := packp.NewReferenceUpdateRequestFromCapabilities(adv.Capabilities)
42		req.Progress = progressWriter(verbose)
43		if sb := PreferredSideband(adv.Capabilities); sb != "" {
44			_ = req.Capabilities.Set(sb)
45		}
46	
47		hasDelete := false
48		hasUpdates := false
49		for _, cmd := range commands {
50			c := &packp.Command{Name: cmd.Name, Old: cmd.Old}
51			if cmd.Delete {
52				c.New = plumbing.ZeroHash
53				hasDelete = true
54			} else {
55				c.New = cmd.New
56				hasUpdates = true
57			}
58			req.Commands = append(req.Commands, c)
59		}
60		if hasDelete {
61			if !adv.Capabilities.Supports(capability.DeleteRefs) {
62				return fmt.Errorf("target does not support delete-refs")
63			}
64			_ = req.Capabilities.Set(capability.DeleteRefs)
65		}
66	
67		return executePush(ctx, session, store, req, hashes, hasUpdates, !adv.Capabilities.Supports(capability.OFSDelta))
68	}
69	
70	// PushPack pushes a pack stream (relay) to the target.
71	func PushPack(
72		ctx context.Context,
73		conn *Conn,
74		adv *packp.AdvRefs,
75		commands []PushCommand,
76		pack io.ReadCloser,
77		verbose bool,
78	) error {
79		session, err := conn.Transport.NewReceivePackSession(conn.Endpoint, conn.Auth)
80		if err != nil {
81			return fmt.Errorf("open target receive-pack session: %w", err)
82		}
83		defer session.Close()
84	
85		req := packp.NewReferenceUpdateRequestFromCapabilities(adv.Capabilities)
86		req.Progress = progressWriter(verbose)
87		if sb := PreferredSideband(adv.Capabilities); sb != "" {
88			_ = req.Capabilities.Set(sb)
89		}
90	
91		for _, cmd := range commands {
92			c := &packp.Command{Name: cmd.Name, Old: cmd.Old}
93			if cmd.Delete {
94				return fmt.Errorf("pack push only supports create and update actions")
95			}
96			c.New = cmd.New
97			req.Commands = append(req.Commands, c)
98		}
99	
100		req.Packfile = pack
101		report, err := session.ReceivePack(ctx, req)
102		if err != nil {
103			_ = pack.Close()
104			return err
105		}
106		if closeErr := pack.Close(); closeErr != nil {
107			return closeErr
108		}
109		if report != nil {
110			return report.Error()
111		}
112		return nil
113	}
114	
115	// PushCommands sends ref update commands without a pack (for ref-only changes).
116	func PushCommands(
117		ctx context.Context,
118		conn *Conn,
119		adv *packp.AdvRefs,
120		commands []PushCommand,
121		verbose bool,
122	) error {
123		session, err := conn.Transport.NewReceivePackSession(conn.Endpoint, conn.Auth)
124		if err != nil {
125			return fmt.Errorf("open target receive-pack session: %w", err)
126		}
127		defer session.Close()
128	
129		req := packp.NewReferenceUpdateRequestFromCapabilities(adv.Capabilities)
130		req.Progress = progressWriter(verbose)
131		if sb := PreferredSideband(adv.Capabilities); sb != "" {
132			_ = req.Capabilities.Set(sb)
133		}
134	
135		hasDelete := false
136		for _, cmd := range commands {
137			c := &packp.Command{Name: cmd.Name, Old: cmd.Old}
138			if cmd.Delete {
139				c.New = plumbing.ZeroHash
140				hasDelete = true
141			} else {
142				c.New = cmd.New
143			}
144			req.Commands = append(req.Commands, c)
145		}
146		if hasDelete {
147			if !adv.Capabilities.Supports(capability.DeleteRefs) {
148				return fmt.Errorf("target does not support delete-refs")
149			}
150			_ = req.Capabilities.Set(capability.DeleteRefs)
151		}
152	
153		return executePush(ctx, session, nil, req, nil, false, false)
154	}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/git-sync/internal/gitproto/push.go",
  "content": "package gitproto\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"io\"\n\t\"os\"\n\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing\"\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/format/packfile\"\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/protocol/packp\"\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/protocol/packp/capability\"\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/storer\"\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/transport\"\n)\n\n// PushCommand represents a single ref update command.\ntype PushCommand struct {\n\tName   plumbing.ReferenceName\n\tOld    plumbing.Hash\n\tNew    plumbing.Hash\n\tDelete bool\n}\n\n// preparePush opens a receive-pack session and builds the base request with\n// sideband negotiation. Shared by PushObjects, PushPack, and PushCommands.\nfunc preparePush(\n\tconn *Conn,\n\tadv *packp.AdvRefs,\n\tcommands []PushCommand,\n\tverbose bool,\n) (transport.ReceivePackSession, *packp.ReferenceUpdateRequest, bool, bool, error) {\n\tsession, err := conn.Transport.NewReceivePackSession(conn.Endpoint, conn.Auth)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, false, false, fmt.Errorf(\"open target receive-pack session: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treq := packp.NewReferenceUpdateRequestFromCapabilities(adv.Capabilities)\n\treq.Progress = progressWriter(verbose)\n\tif sb := PreferredSideband(adv.Capabilities); sb != \"\" {\n\t\t_ = req.Capabilities.Set(sb)\n\t}\n\n\thasDelete := false\n\thasUpdates := false\n\tfor _, cmd := range commands {\n\t\tc := &packp.Command{Name: cmd.Name, Old: cmd.Old}\n\t\tif cmd.Delete {\n\t\t\tc.New = plumbing.ZeroHash\n\t\t\thasDelete = true\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tc.New = cmd.New\n\t\t\thasUpdates = true\n\t\t}\n\t\treq.Commands = append(req.Commands, c)\n\t}\n\n\tif hasDelete {\n\t\tif !adv.Capabilities.Supports(capability.DeleteRefs) {\n\t\t\t_ = session.Close()\n\t\t\treturn nil, nil, false, false, fmt.Errorf(\"target does not support delete-refs\")\n\t\t}\n\t\t_ = req.Capabilities.Set(capability.DeleteRefs)\n\t}\n\n\treturn session, req, hasDelete, hasUpdates, nil\n}\n\n// PushObjects pushes locally-materialized objects to the target.\nfunc PushObjects(\n\tctx context.Context,\n\tconn *Conn,\n\tadv *packp.AdvRefs,\n\tcommands []PushCommand,\n\tstore storer.Storer,\n\thashes []plumbing.Hash,\n\tverbose bool,\n) error {\n\tsession, req, _, hasUpdates, err := preparePush(conn, adv, commands, verbose)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tdefer session.Close()\n\treturn executePush(ctx, session, store, req, hashes, hasUpdates, !adv.Capabilities.Supports(capability.OFSDelta))\n}\n\n// PushPack pushes a pack stream (relay) to the target.\nfunc PushPack(\n\tctx context.Context,\n\tconn *Conn,\n\tadv *packp.AdvRefs,\n\tcommands []PushCommand,\n\tpack io.ReadCloser,\n\tverbose bool,\n) error {\n\t// Validate no deletes in pack push before opening session.\n\tfor _, cmd := range commands {\n\t\tif cmd.Delete {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"pack push only supports create and update actions\")\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tsession, req, _, _, err := preparePush(conn, adv, commands, verbose)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tdefer session.Close()\n\n\treq.Packfile = pack\n\treport, err := session.ReceivePack(ctx, req)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\t_ = pack.Close()\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tif closeErr := pack.Close(); closeErr != nil {\n\t\treturn closeErr\n\t}\n\tif report != nil {\n\t\treturn report.Error()\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// PushCommands sends ref update commands without a pack (for ref-only changes).\nfunc PushCommands(\n\tctx context.Context,\n\tconn *Conn,\n\tadv *packp.AdvRefs,\n\tcommands []PushCommand,\n\tverbose bool,\n) error {\n\tsession, req, _, _, err := preparePush(conn, adv, commands, verbose)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tdefer session.Close()\n\treturn executePush(ctx, session, nil, req, nil, false, false)\n}\n\nfunc executePush(\n\tctx context.Context,\n\tsession transport.ReceivePackSession,\n\tstore storer.Storer,\n\treq *packp.ReferenceUpdateRequest,\n\thashes []plumbing.Hash,\n\tsendPack bool,\n\tuseRefDeltas bool,\n) error {\n\tif !sendPack {\n\t\treport, err := session.ReceivePack(ctx, req)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t\tif report != nil {\n\t\t\treturn report.Error()\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\trd, wr := io.Pipe()\n\treq.Packfile = rd\n\tdone := make(chan error, 1)\n\n\tgo func() {\n\t\tenc := packfile.NewEncoder(wr, store, useRefDeltas)\n\t\tif _, err := enc.Encode(hashes, 10); err != nil {\n\t\t\tdone <- wr.CloseWithError(err)\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\tdone <- wr.Close()\n\t}()\n\n\treport, err := session.ReceivePack(ctx, req)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\t_ = rd.Close()\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tif err := <-done; err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tif report != nil {\n\t\treturn report.Error()\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nfunc progressWriter(verbose bool) io.Writer {\n\tif !verbose {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\treturn os.Stderr\n}\n"
}

> AGENT

All clean. Here's what the simplify pass fixed:

**Fixed:**
1. **Duplicate `statsPhaseHeader` constant** — exported from `gitproto.StatsPhaseHeader`, removed duplicate from `syncer/stats.go`
2. **Hand-rolled insertion sort** — `sortStrings` now delegates to `sort.Strings`
3. **Push setup duplication** — extracted `preparePush` helper, ~40 lines of copy-paste eliminated from `PushObjects`/`PushPack`/`PushCommands`
4. **`buildSidebandReader` duplicated `PreferredSideband`** — now delegates to the centralized function
5. **`map[Hash]bool` → `map[Hash]struct{}`** — in `ReachesCommit` for lower memory
6. **Measurement interval 10ms → 200ms** — `runtime.ReadMemStats` is stop-the-world; 200ms is far less intrusive
7. **`progressWriter` returning nil** — fixed to return `os.Stderr` (was already fixed in codex pass)

**Skipped (not worth the complexity/risk):**
- Unifying `planner.DesiredRef`/`gitproto.DesiredRef` — different abstraction levels by design
- V2 fetch args builder extraction — only 2 callsites with meaningful differences
- `collectObjects` double-load — go-git's object cache makes this negligible
- `fileTokenPath` caching — trivial cost

Now let me continue with the remaining work items. What's left from earlier:

- Strategy package extraction
- Test gaps (#18-22)
- Benchmarks (#22)

  "content": "// Package incremental implements the incremental relay strategy for git-sync.\n// This fast-path streams a pack from source directly to target when all updates\n// are fast-forward branch updates or new tag creates.\npackage incremental\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"io\"\n\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing\"\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/protocol/packp\"\n\n\t\"github.com/soph/git-sync/internal/gitproto\"\n\t\"github.com/soph/git-sync/internal/planner\"\n)\n\n// Params holds the inputs for an incremental relay execution.\ntype Params struct {\n\tSourceConn    *gitproto.Conn\n\tTargetConn    *gitproto.Conn\n\tSourceService *gitproto.RefService\n\tTargetAdv     *packp.AdvRefs\n\tDesiredRefs   map[plumbing.ReferenceName]planner.DesiredRef\n\tTargetRefs    map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash\n\tPushPlans     []planner.BranchPlan\n\tMaxPackBytes  int64\n\tVerbose       bool\n}\n\n// Result holds the outcome of an incremental relay.\ntype Result struct {\n\tRelay       bool\n\tRelayMode   string\n\tRelayReason string\n}\n\n// Execute attempts the incremental relay strategy. Returns (result, nil) on\n// success, or (zero, nil) if the strategy is not applicable. Errors indicate\n// a relay was attempted but failed.\nfunc Execute(ctx context.Context, p Params, cfg planner.PlanConfig) (Result, error) {\n\tif ok, reason := planner.CanIncrementalRelay(cfg.Force, cfg.Prune, false, p.PushPlans, p.TargetAdv); ok {\n\t\tdesired := toGP(planner.DesiredSubset(p.DesiredRefs, p.PushPlans))\n\t\tpackReader, err := p.SourceService.FetchPack(ctx, p.SourceConn, desired, p.TargetRefs)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn Result{}, fmt.Errorf(\"fetch source pack: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tdefer packReader.Close()\n\t\tpackReader = limitPack(packReader, p.MaxPackBytes)\n\t\tcmds := toPushCmds(p.PushPlans)\n\t\tif err := gitproto.PushPack(ctx, p.TargetConn, p.TargetAdv, cmds, packReader, p.Verbose); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn Result{}, fmt.Errorf(\"push target refs: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn Result{Relay: true, RelayMode: \"incremental\", RelayReason: reason}, nil\n\t}\n\n\tif ok, reason := planner.CanFullTagCreateRelay(p.PushPlans); ok {\n\t\tdesired := toGP(planner.DesiredSubset(p.DesiredRefs, p.PushPlans))\n\t\tpackReader, err := p.SourceService.FetchPack(ctx, p.SourceConn, desired, nil)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn Result{}, fmt.Errorf(\"fetch source tag pack: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tdefer packReader.Close()\n\t\tpackReader = limitPack(packReader, p.MaxPackBytes)\n\t\tcmds := toPushCmds(p.PushPlans)\n\t\tif err := gitproto.PushPack(ctx, p.TargetConn, p.TargetAdv, cmds, packReader, p.Verbose); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn Result{}, fmt.Errorf(\"push target refs: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn Result{Relay: true, RelayMode: \"incremental\", RelayReason: reason}, nil\n\t}\n\n\treturn Result{}, nil\n}\n\nfunc toGP(desired map[plumbing.ReferenceName]planner.DesiredRef) map[plumbing.ReferenceName]gitproto.DesiredRef {\n\tout := make(map[plumbing.ReferenceName]gitproto.DesiredRef, len(desired))\n\tfor k, v := range desired {\n\t\tout[k] = gitproto.DesiredRef{\n\t\t\tSourceRef: v.SourceRef, TargetRef: v.TargetRef,\n\t\t\tSourceHash: v.SourceHash, IsTag: v.Kind == planner.RefKindTag,\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn out\n}\n\nfunc toPushCmds(plans []planner.BranchPlan) []gitproto.PushCommand {\n\tcmds := make([]gitproto.PushCommand, 0, len(plans))\n\tfor _, p := range plans {\n\t\tcmd := gitproto.PushCommand{Name: p.TargetRef, Old: p.TargetHash}\n\t\tswitch p.Action {\n\t\tcase planner.ActionCreate, planner.ActionUpdate:\n\t\t\tcmd.New = p.SourceHash\n\t\tcase planner.ActionDelete:\n\t\t\tcmd.Delete = true\n\t\t}\n\t\tcmds = append(cmds, cmd)\n\t}\n\treturn cmds\n}\n\nfunc limitPack(r io.ReadCloser, maxBytes int64) io.ReadCloser {\n\tif maxBytes <= 0 {\n\t\treturn r\n\t}\n\treturn &packLimitRC{ReadCloser: r, max: maxBytes}\n}\n\ntype packLimitRC struct {\n\tio.ReadCloser\n\tmax  int64\n\tread int64\n}\n\nfunc (r *packLimitRC) Read(p []byte) (int, error) {\n\tn, err := r.ReadCloser.Read(p)\n\tr.read += int64(n)\n\tif r.read > r.max {\n\t\treturn n, fmt.Errorf(\"source pack exceeded max-pack-bytes limit (%d)\", r.max)\n\t}\n\treturn n, err\n}\n"
}

  "content": "// Package materialized implements the materialized fallback push strategy.\n// This path fetches objects into local memory, then encodes and pushes them\n// to the target. Used when relay is not safe.\npackage materialized\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\n\tgit \"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5\"\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing\"\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/protocol/packp\"\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/storer\"\n\n\t\"github.com/soph/git-sync/internal/gitproto\"\n\t\"github.com/soph/git-sync/internal/planner\"\n)\n\n// Params holds the inputs for a materialized push.\ntype Params struct {\n\tStore         storer.Storer\n\tSourceConn    *gitproto.Conn\n\tSourceService *gitproto.RefService\n\tTargetConn    *gitproto.Conn\n\tTargetAdv     *packp.AdvRefs\n\tDesiredRefs   map[plumbing.ReferenceName]planner.DesiredRef\n\tTargetRefs    map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash\n\tPushPlans     []planner.BranchPlan\n\tVerbose       bool\n}\n\n// Execute runs the materialized fallback: ensures tag objects are local,\n// computes the object closure, and pushes to the target.\nfunc Execute(ctx context.Context, p Params) error {\n\tif len(p.PushPlans) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Ensure tag objects are fetched locally\n\tif err := ensureTagObjects(ctx, p); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"prepare local objects for push: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tobjects := make([]plumbing.Hash, 0, len(p.PushPlans))\n\tfor _, plan := range p.PushPlans {\n\t\tif plan.Action == planner.ActionCreate || plan.Action == planner.ActionUpdate {\n\t\t\tobjects = append(objects, plan.SourceHash)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\thashes, err := planner.ObjectsToPush(p.Store, objects, p.TargetRefs)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"compute objects to push: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tcmds := toPushCmds(p.PushPlans)\n\tif err := gitproto.PushObjects(ctx, p.TargetConn, p.TargetAdv, cmds, p.Store, hashes, p.Verbose); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"push target refs: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nfunc ensureTagObjects(ctx context.Context, p Params) error {\n\ttagDesired := make(map[plumbing.ReferenceName]gitproto.DesiredRef)\n\tfor _, plan := range p.PushPlans {\n\t\tif plan.Kind != planner.RefKindTag {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif d, ok := p.DesiredRefs[plan.TargetRef]; ok {\n\t\t\ttagDesired[plan.TargetRef] = gitproto.DesiredRef{\n\t\t\t\tSourceRef: d.SourceRef, TargetRef: d.TargetRef,\n\t\t\t\tSourceHash: d.SourceHash, IsTag: true,\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif len(tagDesired) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\terr := p.SourceService.FetchToStore(ctx, p.Store, p.SourceConn, tagDesired, nil)\n\tif err != nil && err != git.NoErrAlreadyUpToDate {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nfunc toPushCmds(plans []planner.BranchPlan) []gitproto.PushCommand {\n\tcmds := make([]gitproto.PushCommand, 0, len(plans))\n\tfor _, p := range plans {\n\t\tcmd := gitproto.PushCommand{Name: p.TargetRef, Old: p.TargetHash}\n\t\tswitch p.Action {\n\t\tcase planner.ActionCreate, planner.ActionUpdate:\n\t\t\tcmd.New = p.SourceHash\n\t\tcase planner.ActionDelete:\n\t\t\tcmd.Delete = true\n\t\t}\n\t\tcmds = append(cmds, cmd)\n\t}\n\treturn cmds\n}\n"
}

700	func bootstrapWithInputs(

708		targetRefs map[plumbing.ReferenceName]plumbing.Hash,
709		relayReason string,

711	) (Result, error) {
712		plans, err := planner.BuildBootstrapPlans(desiredRefs, targetRefs)
713		if err != nil {
714			return Result{}, err
715		}
716	
717		result := Result{
718			Plans: plans, Relay: true, RelayMode: "bootstrap", RelayReason: relayReason,
719			Stats: stats.snapshot(), Protocol: sourceService.Protocol,
720		}
721	
722		// GitHub large-repo preflight
723		if batchLimit, ok := githubBootstrapBatchMaxPackBytes(ctx, cfg, sourceConn, sourceService); ok {
724			cfg.BatchMaxPackBytes = batchLimit
725			progressf(cfg.Verbose, "bootstrap: github repo-size preflight selected batched mode with batch-max-pack-bytes=%d", cfg.BatchMaxPackBytes)
726		}
727	
728		if cfg.BatchMaxPackBytes > 0 {
729			return bootstrapBatched(ctx, cfg, stats, sourceConn, targetConn, sourceService, targetAdv, plans, desiredRefs, targetRefs, result, measurementDone)
730		}
731	
732		// One-shot bootstrap
733		progressf(cfg.Verbose, "bootstrap: fetching %d ref(s) from source", len(plans))
734		gpDesired := toGitprotoDesired(desiredRefs)
735		packReader, err := sourceService.FetchPack(ctx, sourceConn, gpDesired, nil)
736		if err != nil {
737			if errors.Is(err, git.NoErrAlreadyUpToDate) {
738				return result, nil
739			}
740			return result, fmt.Errorf("fetch source pack: %w", err)
741		}
742		packReader = limitPackReadCloser(packReader, cfg.MaxPackBytes)
743	
744		progressf(cfg.Verbose, "bootstrap: pushing %d ref(s) to target", len(plans))
745		cmds := plansToPushCommands(plans)
746		pushErr := gitproto.PushPack(ctx, targetConn, targetAdv, cmds, packReader, cfg.Verbose)
747		if pushErr != nil {
748			autoBatch, ok := autoBatchMaxPackBytes(cfg, sourceService, pushErr)
749			if !ok {
750				return result, fmt.Errorf("push target refs: %w", pushErr)
751			}
752			progressf(cfg.Verbose, "bootstrap: target rejected; retrying with batch-max-pack-bytes=%d", autoBatch)
753			cfg.BatchMaxPackBytes = autoBatch
754			return bootstrapBatched(ctx, cfg, stats, sourceConn, targetConn, sourceService, targetAdv, plans, desiredRefs, targetRefs, result, measurementDone)
755		}
756	
757		result.Pushed = len(plans)
758		result.Stats = stats.snapshot()
759		return result, nil
760	}
